As local-living and polycentric planning gain popularity, a key question is whether more localised mobility can preserve the socioeconomic mixing that cities enable. Yet systematic evidence on how different scales of movement contribute to experienced social exposure remains limited. Here, we use anonymised GPS traces from ~20 million devices across 226 functional urban areas in France, Germany and the UK to decompose everyday mobility into three scales - proximal, medial and distal - and link these to the socioeconomic composition of destinations. Proximal trips remain similar in length across neighbourhoods, whereas medial and distal trips lengthen toward the periphery. Distal trips account for 33% of travel but facilitate 42% of inter-group encounters; reallocating them to local movement reduces experienced mixing by up to one third. These findings reveal a trade-off between shorter travel and integrated urban living.

</details>


### [119] [A Generalized Gompertz Law for the Rise and Fall of Empires](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29202)
*Sabin Roman,Vaishnavi Jayakumar,Karoline Wiesner*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种最小动力学模型，统一描述帝国的兴衰过程，揭示了行政与协调成本随时间指数增长（chronophage）是导致扩张后衰退的关键机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有定量模型多关注社会或帝国的扩张，很少在一个统一框架内解释其增长与衰退。本文旨在通过一个统一的形式化模型来弥补这一空白，以更好地理解复杂社会系统演化中的普遍规律。

Method: 构建了一个最小动力学模型，该模型能够产生封闭形式的轨迹，推广了Gompertz定律，并具有最大熵和最优控制两种等价表述；利用历史数据对14个不同时期和地区的帝国进行拟合验证。

Result: 模型成功捕捉了14个不同帝国的兴衰模式，显示出共同的非对称上升-峰值-下降曲线；发现行政和协调成本的指数积累（即'chronophage'机制）抵消了扩张带来的收益，是导致衰退的核心动因。

Conclusion: 大规模社会系统的演化受制于复杂性带来的内在成本，存在一种普适的定量规律：尽管历史背景差异巨大，帝国的兴衰仍遵循由少数可解释参数决定的共同动态模式。

Abstract: Human societies tend to evolve toward increasing scale and complexity, often followed by stagnation and decline. Historical empires provide a prominent example of this trajectory; however, existing quantitative models capture expansion and rarely account for decline within a unified framework. Here, we introduce a minimal dynamical model that describes both growth and collapse in a single formalism. The model yields a closed-form trajectory that generalizes the Gompertz law and admits equivalent maximum-entropy and optimal-control formulations. We show that this functional form captures the rise and fall of fourteen diverse empires across widely varying time scales. Despite substantial historical heterogeneity, all cases follow a common asymmetric rise-peak-decline pattern governed by a small number of interpretable parameters. We identify the mechanism driving this behavior as the chronophage: an exponentially accumulating burden of administrative and coordination costs that offsets the gains of expansion. These findings uncover a general quantitative principle, yielding new insights into how increasing complexity constrains large-scale social systems.

</details>


### [120] [Measurement Validity in LLM Cultural Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29266)
*An Duy Nguyen,Muhammad Aurangzeb Ahmad*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了将大语言模型（LLM）对价值观调查的回答作为人类文化价值观代理的有效性，发现其结果受采样噪声和问题表述方式影响显著，许多情况下模型表现出的文化定位无法与噪声区分，因此当前对模型文化坐标的精确解读缺乏可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究人员希望验证将大语言模型的回应用于推断文化价值观的合理性，并识别其中由随机噪声和提示词变化带来的干扰。

Method: 通过分解多个LLM在不同随机种子和提示重写下的回答变异，使用信噪比（NSR）来评估模型显现的文化位置是否可与噪声区分，并在12个来自四个地理区域的模型上进行测试，对照88个国家的集成价值观调查数据。

Result: 在117个有效的模型-问题对中，49个的NSR超过1.0（占比42%），最高达5.56；两个模型甚至拒绝回答足够数量的问题；提示语气的变化可使模型在文化地图上移动达2.4个单位，接近真实国家间的距离。

Conclusion: 目前从LLM调查回应中进行文化归因的研究需谨慎，必须先建立测量结果的可靠性，否则无法支持对模型文化代表性的精确解释。

Abstract: Researchers increasingly treat LLM survey responses as a proxy for human cultural values. This includes projecting model outputs onto instruments like the Inglehart-Welzel Cultural Map and drawing conclusions about which cultures a model resembles. While a model's answer to a value-laden questions may be interpreted as a cultural signal, it also carries sampling noise and, can be quite sensitive to question framing. In this paper, we separate survey responses, sampling noise and question framing for multiple LLMs. We decompose response variance from these models into variation across random seeds, prompt rewordings. We employ noise-to-signal ratio (NSR) to test whether a model's apparent cultural position is distinguishable from noise. When applied across a dozen models from four geographic origins, calibrated against 88 Integrated Values Survey countries, the answer is often no. NSR exceeds 1.0 on 49 of 117 valid model-question pairs (42%), reaching 5.56 in the worst case. Two models even refuse to answer sufficient number of survey questions outright. Our results corroborate previous findings that LLMs cluster toward Western, English-speaking cultural positions. However, what does not hold up in this study is the precision with which anyone can currently interpret a specific model's coordinates: prompt tone alone can shift a model by 2.4 map units, comparable to the distance between actual countries in the Inglehart-Welzel Cultural Map. These findings suggest that cultural attribution from LLM survey responses requires establishing the reliability of the underlying measurements before interpreting model coordinates as evidence of cultural representation.

</details>


### [121] [Integrating adaptive human behavior into epidemic models with large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29535)
*Yicheng Mao,Haoyang Li,Rob Deardon,Hongru Du*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: GABLE框架利用大语言模型（LLM）模拟疫情中人类的适应性行为，并将其整合到传染病动力学模型中，提升了对人群接触模式和疫情传播的预测能力，尤其在长期预测和政策评估方面优于传统基于移动数据的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统传染病模型难以捕捉随疫情变化而改变的人类互动行为，缺乏对上下文依赖行为的建模能力，限制了预测准确性和政策评估效果。

Method: 提出Generative Adaptive Behavioral Layer for Epidemics (GABLE)，利用大语言模型推断人类对疫情和政策变化的行为响应，并生成年龄结构化的接触矩阵，与机制性传染病模型耦合。

Result: 在法国COVID-19数据上的应用显示，GABLE能准确复现人群混合模式和年龄别接触结构；在短期预测中，LLM生成的接触矩阵优于基于真实移动数据的方法，尤其在较长预测时域表现更优；同时支持前瞻性政策评估，能区分不同政策时机和组合的流行病学影响。

Conclusion: GABLE通过将大语言模型作为灵活的行为层，实现了对情境敏感的人类行为建模，为传染病建模提供了融合自适应行为与疾病传播动态的新框架。

Abstract: Infectious disease transmission is shaped by patterns of human interaction, which adapt as epidemic conditions change. Capturing these context-dependent behaviors remains a fundamental challenge for epidemic models. Here, we recast this challenge by using large language models (LLMs) to represent adaptive human behavior within mechanistic epidemic models. We operationalize this idea through Generative Adaptive Behavioral Layer for Epidemics (GABLE), which adapts LLMs to infer behavioral responses to epidemic and policy conditions and translates them into age-structured contact matrices coupled to a mechanistic epidemic model. Applied to COVID-19 in France, GABLE reproduced responses in population mixing and age-specific contact structures that remained epidemiologically informative. In short-term forecasting, LLM-generated contact matrices outperformed mobility-driven matrices derived from real-world mobility data, with the largest gains at longer horizons. GABLE also extends beyond forecasting to prospective policy evaluation by projecting behavioral and epidemic responses to candidate interventions before implementation. When supplied with subsequently implemented policies, GABLE reproduced epidemic trajectories and generated distinct responses to alternative policy timing and composition. By leveraging LLMs as a flexible behavioral layer, GABLE provides a framework for coupling context-sensitive behavioral generation with epidemic dynamics.

</details>


### [122] [ButterMamba: Butterworth-Enhanced Spatial-Temporal Mamba for Efficient Traffic Flow Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29658)
*Limiao Zhang,Yuhui Lu,Jie Gao,Hao Jiang,Haiping Ma,Xingyi Zhang*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为ButterMamba的新型高效交通流预测框架，基于状态空间模型，通过Butterworth频谱滤波模块去除高频噪声，并采用并行Mamba架构捕捉时空依赖关系，在保证高精度的同时实现了线性计算复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于注意力机制的图神经网络在大规模交通网络中存在计算复杂度高和对传感器数据噪声敏感的问题，难以兼顾实时性和鲁棒性，限制了其在城市交通系统中的应用。

Method: 设计了一个双模块框架：一是Butterworth频谱滤波模块，用于预处理去除高频噪声；二是时空状态混合器，采用并行Mamba结构建模长程时序依赖和复杂空间关联。

Result: 在三个公开数据集上实验表明，ButterMamba在预测精度上优于现有最先进模型，同时显著降低了训练时间和内存消耗。

Conclusion: ButterMamba通过解耦噪声过滤与时空建模，在保持线性计算复杂度的同时实现了高精度和强鲁棒性的交通流预测，适用于大规模城市交通系统。

Abstract: Accurate traffic flow prediction is fundamental to intelligent transportation systems, playing a pivotal role in urban mobility optimization and smart city development. While Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) integrated with time series forecasting have emerged as promising solutions, two critical limitations persist: (1) the quadratic complexity of attention-based architectures hinders real-time deployment in large-scale networks, and (2) high-frequency noise in sensor data significantly degrades prediction reliability. These challenges are particularly acute in metropolitan scenarios where both computational efficiency and noise robustness are paramount. To address these limitations, we introduce \textbf{ButterMamba}, a novel and efficient framework based on State Space Models (SSMs). ButterMamba consists of two key components: (1) a Butterworth Spectral Filtering module that preprocesses the data by removing high-frequency noise, allowing the model to focus on significant underlying trends, and (2) a Spatial-Temporal State Mixer that uses a parallel Mamba architecture to efficiently capture both long-range temporal dependencies and complex spatial correlations across the road network. By decoupling noise filtering from spatial-temporal modeling, ButterMamba achieves superior predictive accuracy with linear computational complexity. Extensive experiments on three public datasets demonstrate that ButterMamba not only outperforms existing state-of-the-art models in terms of prediction accuracy but also considerably reduces training time and memory usage.

</details>


### [123] [Open, Reproducible Per-Bidding-Zone Carbon Intensity for Nordic Power Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29717)
*Eirik Botten Nicolaysen*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种开源、可重复的方法，用于计算北欧电力系统各 bidding zone 的电网碳强度（CI），揭示了现有统一排放因子的不准确性，并强调了区域级碳强度数据对碳感知负荷调度的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的电网碳强度信号常使用统一的默认排放因子，无法准确反映区域间的差异，导致碳排放评估失真，因此需要更精确、细粒度的区域性碳强度计算方法。

Method: 结合 ENTSO-E 提供的按发电类型划分的发电数据与 IPCC AR5 生命周期排放因子，按 bidding zone 进行时间加权计算，并设定明确的缺失数据重要性阈值，实现每区域碳强度的估算。

Result: 2025年应用于九个 bidding zone（NO1-NO5, SE1-SE4）时，碳强度范围为14.5至39.6 gCO2eq/kWh；发现常用的18.0 gCO2eq/kWh统一值在多数区域低估了排放（如NO4低估约2.2倍），而在核能主导的SE3则高估；年际变化分析显示部分区域接近预注册的15%变化阈值；预测实验表明简单基线模型已足够。

Conclusion: 北欧电网中，国家级或统一的排放因子不足以支持精准碳核算，必须采用 bidding zone 级别的分辨率才能真实反映碳排放情况，该方法已开源并贡献至codecarbon库。

Abstract: Carbon-aware scheduling of electrical loads depends on accurate grid carbon-intensity (CI) signals, yet existing tools commonly apply a uniform placeholder emission factor that misrepresents regional variation. This work presents an open, reproducible method for computing per-bidding-zone CI across the Nordic power system. Generation-by-type data from ENTSO-E is combined with IPCC AR5 life-cycle emission factors, duration-weighted per bidding zone, with an explicit materiality threshold for missing data. Applied to nine bidding zones (NO1-NO5, SE1-SE4) for 2025, the method yields CI values ranging from 14.5 to 39.6 gCO2eq/kWh. The results reveal that the uniform 18.0 gCO2eq/kWh placeholder is wrong in both directions: it understates CI for most zones, by a factor of approximately 2.2 for NO4, yet overstates CI for the nuclear-dominated zone SE3, whose production-based CI is the lowest of the nine. Drift is characterised against a pre-registered threshold of 15% year-on-year change, including a borderline case in SE4 and event-driven variation in NO4. Forecasting experiments do not reject the null hypothesis that simple baselines suffice, in either the Norwegian or Swedish zones. The full method, data, and results are released under open licences and archived at DOI 10.5281/zenodo.21042581, with the per-zone values contributed upstream to the codecarbon library. Per-bidding-zone resolution is necessary for accurate carbon accounting in Nordic grids, where country-level averages obscure substantial intra-country variation.

</details>


### [124] [Peer Review at Capacity: An editor's view](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29774)
*Yamir Moreno*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 同行评审压力日益增加，主要体现在邀请审稿人困难、依赖少数专家以及稿件在不同期刊间流转导致的重复工作。问题根源在于审稿劳动分配不均、认可不足，以及出版体系架构不适应当前科研规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示当前同行评审系统面临的压力及其结构性问题，引起对审稿体系改革的关注。

Method: 通过编辑视角观察和分析当前同行评审中的实际现象，如邀请审稿的困难和重复劳动。

Result: 指出同行评审问题的核心不是审稿人数量不足，而是审稿工作的分配、认可机制和出版架构存在缺陷。

Conclusion: 现有的出版体系建立在较小规模科研系统基础上，已不适应当前需求，需进行系统性改革以缓解评审压力。

Abstract: From the editor's desk, the pressure on peer review is no longer an abstract concern. It appears in the growing number of invitations needed to secure an appropriate report, the repeated reliance on a small group of dependable colleagues, and the duplication of work when manuscripts move between journals. The problem is not simply that science has too few potential reviewers. It is that reviewing labor is unevenly distributed, weakly recognized, and organized through a publication architecture built for a smaller system.

</details>


### [125] [Does the Power-Law Advantage in the Tails Outweigh the Global q-Gaussian Description?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30219)
*Eduardo Boor,Roberto da Silva,Joao Carlos Schmitt de Siqueira,J. Roberto Iglesias*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 该论文比较了金融回报分布中幂律与q-高斯分布的拟合效果，发现尽管幂律在极端尾部表现更优，但q-高斯能统一描述整个分布（包括中心峰、主体和尾部），并在不同时间尺度下提供更全面、实用的统计刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上金融市场的重尾分布用幂律描述，但缺乏对整体分布（尤其是中心区域）的一致性建模；本文旨在评估基于Tsallis非广延统计力学的q-高斯分布是否能更全面地刻画金融回报分布。

Method: 使用矩比法估计q-高斯参数，极大似然法估计幂律参数，并通过拟合优度检验、自举重采样和Vuong检验评估两种模型在不同时间尺度及资产类型（股票、传统资产、加密货币）下的表现。

Result: 在仅考虑极端尾部时，幂律拟合更优；但在全分布范围内，q-高斯表现更一致且更具优势，尤其能通过参数q的演化反映聚集高斯性和长短尾态之间的转换。

Conclusion: 虽然幂律更适合描述极端事件，但q-高斯为金融回报的统计演化提供了更广泛且实用的框架，能够统一描述分布的不同区域而无需分段拟合。

Abstract: Financial markets are complex systems whose return distributions exhibit heavy tails, traditionally described by power laws. An alternative framework based on Tsallis nonextensive statistical mechanics allows both the central region and the tails of the distribution to be represented by a single q-Gaussian functional form. In this work, we systematically compare these two descriptions across different time scales, considering stock markets from Brazil and the United States, as well as traditional assets and cryptocurrencies. The q-Gaussian parameters are estimated using the method of moments ratio, whereas the power-law parameters are obtained by maximum likelihood estimation in the asymptotic region. The quality of the fits is assessed using goodness-of-fit measures, bootstrap resampling, and the Vuong test. When the analysis is restricted exclusively to the extreme tails, the power law generally provides a better description, as expected from the asymptotic power-law behavior of the q-Gaussian itself. However, this superiority is not sufficiently pronounced to outweigh the main advantage of the q-Gaussian: its ability to provide a consistent description of the entire distribution, including the central peak, bulk, and tails, over all investigated time scales. Moreover, the evolution of the parameter $q$ offers a simple and direct characterization of aggregational Gaussianity and of the transition between heavy- and short-tailed statistical regimes, without requiring separate fits for different regions of the distribution. These results indicate that, although the power law remains particularly suitable for describing extreme events, the q-Gaussian provides a broader and more practical framework for characterizing the statistical evolution of financial returns.

</details>


<div id='physics.geo-ph'></div>

# physics.geo-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [126] [Is Seismic Forecasting Possible with Physics-based AI?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30861)
*Victoria Keane,Manolis Veveakis,Thomas Poulet*

Main category: physics.geo-ph

TL;DR: 本研究结合板块俯冲的物理机制与AI辅助的流形检测，提出一种基于物理吸引子的方法，成功在新西兰Hikurangi海沟提前一周预测地震，预测能力可达5-6周，并具有十年重复性，表明地震可基于物理机制进行可预测性预报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 慢滑移事件为地震预测提供了重要窗口，但传统滤波方法和数据噪声掩盖了其内在机制，亟需更有效的方法揭示其物理本质并提升预测能力。

Method: 结合俯冲带多物理过程与AI辅助的流形检测技术，构建基于物理的吸引子模型，用于从地表位移序列中识别地震前兆信号。

Result: 在Hikurangi海沟实现提前一周的地震预测，预测时限可达5-6周，且具有十年尺度的重复性。

Conclusion: 慢滑移事件具有根本的确定性机制，基于物理的地震预测是可行的，为未来地震预报提供了新范式。

Abstract: Slow slip events within subduction zones offer a unique window into earthquake prediction. The subducting plate drives dehydration reactions in the fault, causing cyclical slip and observable surface displacements. Earthquake footprints can then be identified in these displacement series through coupling the multi-physics governing the subduction process with regional seismic activity. However, data noise and traditional filtering methods obscure the underlying mechanisms. Here, we alleviate this constraint with our physics-based attractor. By accounting for the physics of subduction paired with AI-assisted manifold detection, we are able to predict an earthquake in New Zealand's Hikurangi trench one week early. Additionally, predictability limits extend to 5-6 weeks with decadal repeatability, pointing to the fundamental determinism of the suggested mechanism through which physics-based seismic forecasting is possible.

</details>


<div id='cs.ET'></div>

# cs.ET [[Back]](#toc)

### [127] [Emergent Behavior and Uncertainty in IoT-Enhanced Business Processes: Challenges and Future Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28919)
*Marco Pegoraro,Sara Pettinari,Ivan Compagnucci,Marco Franceschetti,Ronny Seiger*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 本文探讨了物联网增强型业务流程中由于异构参与者、自主性差异和动态执行环境带来的复杂性和不确定性，提出了在不确定性表示、流程操作化和运行时涌现行为管理三方面的挑战，并基于现状分析指出了研究空白与未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物联网增强的业务流程具有高度复杂性，传统业务流程管理方法难以应对运行时涌现的行为和不确定性，因此需要重新审视现有BPM假设并提出新的研究方向。

Method: 通过一个启发式场景和对现有技术的分析，从不确定性表示、流程操作化和运行时管理三个视角讨论挑战。

Result: 识别出现有研究中的空白，并提出了短期、中期和长期的研究建议。

Conclusion: 为应对物联网增强业务流程中的涌现行为，需建立支持不确定性和动态性的新理论、模型与系统，推动BPM领域的范式演进。

Abstract: IoT-enhanced business processes are characterized by high complexity due to heterogeneous actors, varying levels of autonomy among participating systems, continuously evolving execution contexts spanning the digital and physical worlds, and continuous event streams. In such settings, process behavior partially emerges only at runtime through complex interactions involving humans, IoT devices, physical objects, software systems, agents, and services. This complexity introduces partial observability, uncertainty, and runtime dynamics that are difficult to anticipate and that challenge traditional business process management (BPM) assumptions and systems. We discuss these challenges from three perspectives, addressing 1) uncertainty representation, 2) operationalization of IoT-enhanced processes, and 3) runtime management of emergent behavior. Based on a motivating scenario and an analysis of the state of the art, we identify open research gaps and outline short-, medium-, and long-term recommendations to shape a research agenda on emergent behavior in IoT-enhanced business processes.

</details>


### [128] [Trustless Accountable Data Sharing for Supply Chains: A Reference Architecture and MoreMedDiet Proof-of-Concept](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30992)
*Michal Kit,Montassar Naghmouchi,Maryline Laurent,Badis Hammi,Hella Kaffel Ben Ayed,Mohamed Amine Hamdi,Jihene Khoualdi,Ilhem Abdelhedi Abdelmoula,Sami Sboui*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向供应链的隐私保护、无信任依赖的数据共享通用参考架构，结合许可型分布式账本、物联网数据接入和模块化隐私机制，实现可控透明性，并在MoreMedDiet项目中验证了其可行性与高性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代供应链依赖于互不信任的多方之间敏感商业数据的交换，传统中心化或高披露性的集成方式限制了协作、合规与创新，因此需要一种支持隐私保护和信任最小化的新型架构。

Method: 设计了一个融合许可型区块链（Hyperledger Fabric）、物联网证据采集、模块化隐私机制（如SSI和W3C可验证凭证）的参考架构，并通过MoreMedDiet农场到餐桌项目进行用例映射和概念验证实现。

Result: 实现了可控透明的数据共享，支持安全交换、可审计性、互操作性和法规合规；原型系统在130毫秒内完成可验证凭证的传递，展现出良好性能。

Conclusion: 该架构为农业食品、制药等多主体参与的供应链提供了可复用的设计蓝图，证明了隐私保护与高效信任机制在真实场景中的可行性。

Abstract: Modern supply chains are increasingly dependent on the exchange of sensitive business data between heterogeneous stakeholders who do not trust each other. %under partial trust. Conventional integration approaches typically rely on centralized intermediaries or require extensive data disclosure, which hinders collaboration, compliance, and innovation. We propose a generic reference architecture for privacy preserving, trustless data sharing in supply chains. The architecture combines permissioned distributed ledgers, IoT-based evidence ingestion, and modular privacy mechanisms to enable controlled transparency: stakeholders can extract verifiable insights and support audits while minimizing data exposure.
  We instantiate the architecture in the context of the MoreMedDiet project, which promotes sustainable farm-to-fork food systems and adoption of the Mediterranean diet. By mapping the MoreMedDiet use case into our architecture, we demonstrate how channel topologies, IoT-to-ledger data flows, and smart contract-based quality assurance (QA) can support secure data exchange, transparency, interoperability, and regulatory alignment. Our proof-of-concept illustrates how Self-Sovereign Identity (SSI) and W3C Verifiable Credentials (VCs) can provide portable and auditable access governance over Hyperledger Fabric channels.
  The resulting design offers a reusable blueprint for multi-actor supply chains, including agri-food and pharmaceutical domains. Moreover, the performance achieved -- presenting a verified credential to the next stakeholder in the supply chain in 130 ms -- is promising and demonstrates the potential applicability of the approach in real-world supply chain environments.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [129] [Modeling of Network Constraints in Large-scale Capacity Expansion Optimization of Power Grids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28893)
*Yu Weng,Lara Booth,Priya L. Donti,Ruaridh Macdonald*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文扩展了GenX容量扩展模型，引入基于不动点定理的网络约束，在保持模型可解性的同时纳入交流最优潮流（ACOPF）因素，提升了对电网物理特性影响投资决策的理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有容量扩展模型常忽略非线性的ACOPF约束，导致难以准确评估电网物理特性对发电、储能和输电投资决策的影响，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 通过将基于不动点定理的网络约束嵌入GenX模型，近似考虑ACOPF效应，同时保持模型接近线性运输模型的维度和计算效率。

Result: 新方法在ISO新英格兰电网案例中验证，相比传统运输模型更精确，且比直接嵌入ACOPF的非线性模型计算成本低得多，运行时间仅略有增加。

Conclusion: 该方法在不显著增加计算负担的前提下，有效提升了容量扩展模型对电网物理特性的刻画能力，适用于大规模长期规划问题。

Abstract: Capacity expansion modeling plays a critical role in optimizing the deployment of new generation, storage, and transmission, typically at national and regional levels. To support long-term planning, these models consider a large set of energy technologies and policies, along with decades of weather and demand data. Realistic capacity expansion models thus become high-dimensional optimization problems, with hundreds of millions of variables and constraints, which are challenging to solve. A common strategy to address this complexity is to omit non-linear, non-convex AC optimal power flow (ACOPF) constraints and instead use linearized power balance equations or transport formulations. While these simplifications improve tractability, they limit our understanding of how power flow and the physical properties of power networks impact investment decisions across generation, storage, and transmission infrastructure. This paper addresses this gap by extending the GenX capacity expansion model to incorporate fixed point theorem-based network constraints. These embed ACOPF-based considerations while maintaining the tractability of the planning model, nearly preserving the dimensionality of the transport formulation and incurring only modest runtime increases. This approach is much cheaper than embedding ACOPF directly, making it appropriate for large-scale capacity planning problems. We compare our approach to the original transport-based GenX model as well as a non-linear, non-convex version that incorporates the full ACOPF constraints, for a case study of the ISO New England grid.

</details>


### [130] [Differentiating Minimal-Norm Solutions to Parametric Optimization Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28899)
*Baptiste Plaquevent-Jourdain,Jalal Fadili,Antonio Silveti-Falls*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在参数优化问题中进行广义微分的新方法，适用于传统隐函数定理失效的欠定或病态情况，通过Tikhonov正则化和集值场理论推导出最小范数解映射的广义导数，并验证了其在多种回归与稀疏模型中的适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统隐函数定理要求雅可比矩阵可逆，但在存在多解（如欠定问题）时该条件不成立，限制了隐式微分的应用。因此需要一种更弱且适用范围更广的条件来支持对这类问题的微分。

Method: 引入一个比可逆性更弱的一致值域条件，结合Tikhonov极限正则化方法和保守集值场理论，推导出最小范数解映射的广义导数；进一步在控制广义Hessian特征值的前提下，证明伪逆公式的合理性，并将理论推广到非光滑复合问题。

Result: 理论结果适用于光滑凸目标函数及非光滑问题（如最小二乘、Huber回归和LASSO），并成功扩展了非光滑隐式微分至病态设定；实验部分在数据投毒和数据超清清除任务中验证了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架放宽了传统隐式微分对唯一解和可逆性的依赖，为双层优化和元学习中广泛存在的病态和多解问题提供了坚实的微分工具。

Abstract: Differentiating through parametric optimization problems is central to bilevel programming and meta-learning, often accomplished using approximate implicit differentiation. The implicit function theorem requires inverting a partial Jacobian of the optimality condition, which fails when there are many solutions. Nonetheless, in such cases it is possible to relax invertibility to a strictly weaker uniform range condition, under which it is shown that the minimal-norm solution mapping admits generalized derivatives by using a limiting Tikhonov regularization argument and conservative set-valued field theory. With additional control on the eigenvalues of the generalized Hessians, a pseudoinverse formula is justified. This is established for a class of smooth convex objectives and extended to nonsmooth composite problems. These assumptions are verified for Least-Squares, Huber regression and LASSO. The resulting extension of nonsmooth implicit differentiation to ill-posed settings is examined experimentally on data poisoning and data hypercleaning problems.

</details>


### [131] [Calculus of the Facial Distance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28971)
*Javier Pena,Elias Wirth*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一些计算或下界估计多面体面距离的微积分规则，并将其应用于多种流行的多面体，特别是给出了Birkhoff多面体面距离的下界估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了更好地理解和量化多面体的几何特性，尤其是面距离这一重要参数，研究者希望发展出有效的计算方法。

Method: 通过开发一系列微积分规则来计算或下界估计多面体的面距离，并将这些规则应用于包括Birkhoff多面体在内的多种典型多面体上进行验证和说明。

Result: 成功推导出了可用于计算或下界估计多面体面距离的微积分规则，并具体得到了Birkhoff多面体面距离的一个下界。

Conclusion: 所提出的微积分规则为分析多面体的面距离提供了有效工具，对理解如Birkhoff多面体等复杂结构具有重要意义。

Abstract: We develop a few calculus rules to compute or lower bound the facial distance of a polytope. We illustrate our calculus rules on various popular polytopes. In particular, we provide a provide a lower bound on the facial distance of the Birkhoff polytope.

</details>


### [132] [B$^3$-PWL: GPU-Batched Branch-and-Bound for Piecewise-Linear Optimization with SOS2 Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28988)
*Yilin Guan,Shuqing Luo,Pingzhi Li,Tianlong Chen,Kaidi Xu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向分段线性优化的GPU中心化批量分支定界框架B$^3$-PWL，利用一阶原对偶求解器并行处理LP松弛子问题，并结合SOS2修复启发式与批量可行性泵加速可行解搜索，在多个基准测试中显著优于现有求解器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的分段线性优化求解器主要依赖CPU，难以充分利用现代GPU的并行能力；且已有GPU加速方法多局限于辅助步骤或特定模型（如神经网络），缺乏对通用PWL优化问题的高效支持。因此需要一种真正以GPU为核心的分支定界框架来提升求解效率。

Method: 提出B$^3$-PWL框架，采用GPU上并发求解批量LP松弛子问题的一阶原对偶算法，设计专用的批量化块状稀疏矩阵核函数；同时引入统一的可行性搜索模块，结合SOS2修复启发式和批量可行性泵快速获取可行解，增强剪枝效率。

Result: 在43个PWL-MIP实例组成的基准测试中，B$^3$-PWL相比NVIDIA cuOpt取得9.25倍的几何平均加速比，且所有实例均找到高质量可行解；在公开的阀点机组组合基准上，性能也优于cuOpt、SCIP和HiGHS等主流求解器。

Conclusion: B$^3$-PWL验证了以一阶LP方法为核心引擎的GPU加速分支定界框架在分段线性优化中的有效性与可扩展性，为未来MIP求解器设计提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Piecewise-linear (PWL) optimization problems arise in many mixed-integer programming (MIP) optimization applications, including portfolio optimization, workforce scheduling, and resource allocation. But solving them to global optimality remains computationally expensive because branch-and-bound repeatedly solves LP relaxation subproblems. Existing solvers are largely CPU-centric, leaving the scalability of modern GPUs underutilized. Few prior GPU-accelerated branch-and-bound either targets neural network which is not suitable for general PWL optimization, or accelerates only auxiliary subroutines such as strong branching heuristics within CPU-centric MIP solvers. To bridge this gap, we propose B$^3$-PWL, a GPU-centric batched branch-and-bound framework for piecewise-linear optimization with Special Ordered Set of type 2 (SOS2) constraints. Our method solves batches of LP relaxation subproblems concurrently on the GPU using a first-order primal-dual solver, enabled by a specialized batched block-tiled sparse matrix kernel. To complement bound computation, we further introduce a unified feasibility search module that combines an SOS2 repair primal heuristic with a batched feasibility pump to rapidly obtain feasible incumbents and improve pruning efficiency. On a benchmark of 43 PWL-MIP instances, B$^3$-PWL achieves a 9.25x geometric-mean speedup over NVIDIA cuOpt while reaching high-quality feasible incumbents on every tested instance. On a public valve-point unit-commitment benchmark, it further outperforms NVIDIA cuOpt and the open-source CPU solvers SCIP and HiGHS, demonstrating the potential of first-order LP methods as the central engine of GPU-accelerated branch-and-bound.

</details>


### [133] [A New Primal-Dual Algorithm with Two Convex Combinations and Linesearch for General Convex-Concave Saddle-Point Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28993)
*Zexian Liu,Shuning Liu,Jialong Li*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的带有双凸组合和线搜索的原始-对偶算法NPDAL-n，用于求解一般的凸-凹鞍点问题。该方法通过理论驱动的双凸组合策略解决了参数选择在理论与实验之间的不一致性，并证明了其全局收敛性和次线性收敛速率；当目标函数强凸时，还可实现最优的加速收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有原始-对偶算法中凸组合参数的选择在理论要求与数值实验表现之间存在矛盾：理论倾向于小参数，而实验常偏好大参数。这种不一致限制了算法性能的进一步提升，因此需要一种既能满足理论收敛性又能兼容良好数值表现的新策略。

Method: 引入一种基于两个凸组合的新策略，并将其嵌入原始-对偶框架中，设计出带有线搜索机制的新算法NPDAL-n。采用李雅普诺夫能量下降分析法，在标准假设下证明算法的收敛性；对于强凸情形，进一步构造加速版本以获得最优收敛速率。

Result: 所提NPDAL-n算法在一般凸-凹鞍点问题上具有全局收敛性和O(1/N)的遍历次线性收敛率；当原函数强凸时，加速版本达到最优O(1/N^2)收敛率。双凸组合设计使得参数选取主要由理论保证，不再依赖于数值调参。

Conclusion: 本文提出的双凸组合策略有效弥合了理论与实践在凸组合参数选择上的分歧，提升了原始-对偶算法的鲁棒性与效率，为解决广泛领域的鞍点优化问题提供了新工具。

Abstract: Convex-concave saddle-point problems are ubiquitous across diverse domains, including machine learning, image processing, economics, and equilibrium problems. Primal-dual algorithms provide a highly effective and powerful framework for convex-concave saddle-point problems. Convex combination has become a crucial acceleration technique for primal-dual algorithms, and the integration of this technique has recently made these algorithms a highly active research topic. The choice of the convex combination parameter often has a significant impact on both the theoretical analysis and the numerical performance of the corresponding algorithms. However, the requirements on this parameter imposed by theory are sometimes inconsistent with those suggested by numerical experiments. For instance, theoretical analysis often requires the parameter to be small, while numerical experiments tend sometimes to favor larger values. To address this inconsistency and further advance primal-dual algorithms with convex combination, we develop a novel strategy based on two convex combinations, integrate it into a primal-dual framework, and propose a new primal-dual algorithm with linesearch, termed NPDAL-n, for general convex-concave saddle-point problems. The proposed two convex combinations in NPDAL-n ensure that the permissible range of the convex combination parameters is mainly determined by theoretical considerations, with little regard for numerical performance. Through rigorous Lyapunov energy descent analysis, we establish the global convergence and a sublinear ergodic convergence rate of $\mathcal{O}(1/N)$ for NPDAL-n under standard assumptions. When the primal function is strongly convex, we develop an accelerated version of NPDAL-n that achieves an optimal $\mathcal{O}(1/N^2)$ rate.

</details>


### [134] [The Stochastic Team Orienteering Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29119)
*Alberto Guastalla,Jean-François Côté,Roberto Aringhieri*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了随机团队定向问题（STOP），提出了一种基于整数L-shaped方法的精确算法，并引入优化割和有效不等式来加强模型，通过标准数据集验证了算法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于实际应用中旅行时间具有不确定性，传统确定性模型难以应对，因此需要研究考虑随机旅行时间的团队定向问题。

Method: 将问题建模为两阶段随机整数规划，采用整数L-shaped方法求解，并引入非线性机会约束以确保路线的高可靠性；在第一阶段选择路线，在第二阶段评估期望收益，并加入新的最优性割和有效不等式加强模型。

Result: 所提方法在标准TOP基准数据集上进行了测试，计算结果表明算法能够有效求解STOP问题，并体现出较强的性能表现。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于整数L-shaped的精确算法结合优化割和有效不等式，能够有效解决随机团队定向问题，并在保证路线可靠性的同时最大化期望收益。

Abstract: This paper analyses the Stochastic Team Orienteering Problem (STOP), a stochastic variant of the Team Orienteering Problem (TOP). In the STOP, travel times are represented by random variables. The objective is to determine a set of routes that maximises the expected collected profit. We model the problem as a two-stage stochastic integer program and propose an exact solution method based on the Integer L-shaped method. We also consider a set of non-linear chance constraints to restrict the search to highly reliable routes. In the first-stage, a set of routes is selected, while in the second-stage the recourse evaluates the expected profit of the selected routes. We present a new set of optimality cuts and strengthen the formulation by considering valid inequalities and constraints liftings. Computational results provide a comprehensive analysis of the algorithm using the standard TOP benchmark dataset from the literature.

</details>


### [135] [Regularized extragradient method for structured bilevel optimization in continuous and discrete time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29181)
*Radu Ioan Bot,Enis Chenchene,David Alexander Hulett*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究了在实希尔伯特空间中的双层优化问题，提出了一阶动力系统和正则化近端-外梯度算法，在平滑与非平滑情形下均实现了内外残差的收敛速率及弱收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决双层凸优化问题，尤其是在零集上最小化外层目标函数时的收敛性挑战。

Method: 在平滑情形下设计基于单调流的动力系统；在非平滑情形下提出动态正则化的近端-外梯度算法，并结合几何条件分析收敛性。

Result: 在弱Attouch-Czarnecki型可积条件或sharpness假设下，获得了内外残差的最后迭代收敛速率，并证明轨迹/迭代点弱收敛至最优解。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效处理了双层优化中零集约束的收敛问题，为平滑与非平滑情形提供了统一的收敛分析框架。

Abstract: In a real Hilbert space, we study a bilevel optimization problem that consists in minimizing an outer convex function over the zero set of a maximally monotone operator. In the smooth setting, where the outer objective is convex and Fréchet differentiable and the inner operator is single-valued, continuous and monotone, we associate with the problem a first-order dynamical system that can be viewed as a monotone flow applied to a dynamically regularized operator. Under suitable geometric conditions on the inner problem --- either a weak Attouch-Czarnecki-type integrability condition or the stronger assumption of sharpness --- we establish last-iterate convergence rates for both the outer and inner residuals, together with weak convergence of the trajectories to optimal solutions of the bilevel problem. In the smooth+nonsmooth setting, we enrich the outer objective with a proper, convex, and lower semicontinuous function, while the inner operator is augmented by the subdifferential of a function with the same properties. We propose a regularized proximal-extragradient algorithm in which both the forward and backward steps are performed with respect to dynamically regularized operators and functions, respectively. Under geometric assumptions on the inner problem analogous to those in the smooth setting, we establish last-iterate convergence rates for both the outer and inner residuals, together with weak convergence of the iterates to optimal solutions of the bilevel problem.

</details>


### [136] [Risk-averse design optimization with CVaR constraints via multifidelity tail-region correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29222)
*Gyeolhee Lee,Dongjin Lee*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种多保真度尾区校正方法，用于条件风险价值（CVaR）约束下的风险规避设计优化，显著减少了高保真评估次数，同时保持了精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在风险规避设计中，CVaR约束需要准确的尾部估计，但频繁的高保真评估成本过高，因此需要一种高效且精确的替代方案。

Method: 采用维度分解广义多项式混沌展开（DD-GPCE）作为全局代理模型，并利用协方差量化预测不确定性以定义尾区；在该区域内，通过两阶段策略（探索高不确定性位置，再利用产生大修正的位置）进行高保真校正；使用Tikhonov正则化残差展开构建统一的尾部校正代理模型。

Result: 在十杆桁架问题中，相比多保真重要性采样方法，仅用其1/72到1/274的高保真评估次数即达到接近蒙特卡洛模拟的结果；在吸气阀设计中，质量减少1.05%，弯曲和膨胀的CVaR分别降低17.68%和21.53%，并满足开启要求。

Conclusion: 所提方法通过局部多保真校正，实现了在CVaR约束下高精度、高样本效率的设计优化。

Abstract: Risk-averse design optimization with conditional value-at-risk (CVaR) constraints requires accurate tail estimates, but repeated high-fidelity (HF) evaluations are costly. We propose a multifidelity (MF) tail-region correction method that uses dimensionally decomposed generalized polynomial chaos expansion (DD-GPCE) as a global surrogate and directs a limited HF budget to responses that most strongly influence CVaR. Covariance of the estimated DD-GPCE coefficients quantifies finite-sample prediction uncertainty and defines a confidence-interval-based tail region. Within this region, a two-stage strategy explores locations with high prediction uncertainty and then exploits locations with large correction-induced shifts. A Tikhonov-regularized residual expansion using the same polynomial basis yields a unified tail-corrected surrogate. For a ten-bar truss, the method produced a design close to the crude-MCS reference with approximately 72 to 274 times fewer HF evaluations than the considered MF importance sampling configurations. For a suction valve, it reduced mass by 1.05% and bending and bulging CVaR by 17.68% and 21.53%, respectively, while satisfying the valve-opening requirement. The results demonstrate accurate, HF-sample-efficient CVaR-constrained design through localized MF correction.

</details>


### [137] [Exploiting Micro-Structure in the Generalized Riccati Recursion for Optimal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29238)
*Louis Callens,Wilm Decré*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结构利用的LU分解算法，并将其集成到Riccati递归中，以同时利用最优控制问题中的块对角结构和块内微结构，显著减少了搜索方向的计算时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Riccati递归方法虽能高效求解线性二次子问题，但未能充分利用由一般动力学约束重构引入的块内微结构，限制了计算效率的进一步提升。

Method: 提出一种能够利用块内微结构的LU分解算法，并将其整合进Riccati递归框架中，从而在多个层次上挖掘问题结构的稀疏性与特性。

Result: 在随机线性系统和两个最优控制问题实例上的数值实验表明，搜索方向的计算时间最多减少了20%，尤其在相对零块大小约为0.25且阶段等式约束雅可比较大时提速最明显。

Conclusion: 通过同时利用块对角结构和块内微结构，所提方法有效提升了最优控制问题求解效率，为结构化NLP求解器的设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: Optimal Control Problems (OCPs) have been applied to a variety of applications. Such problems are often transcribed into a Nonlinear Program (NLP) for which solution methods typically solve a Linear Quadratic (LQ) subproblem. To efficiently solve this LQ subproblem, the Riccati recursion exploits the block-diagonal structure that arises from discretizing an OCP using a Multiple Shooting method. However, reformulating general dynamics constraints to fit the required problem structure to use the Riccati recursion introduces additional micro-structure within these block matrices that remains unexploited. This work demonstrates how structure-exploiting solvers such as Fatrop can benefit from exploiting not only the block-diagonal structure but also the micro-structure within those blocks. We propose a structure-exploiting LU decomposition algorithm and integrate it within the Riccati recursion. Numerical results on randomized linear systems and two OCP examples demonstrate a reduction in computation time of the search direction of up to 20%, with the largest speedup observed for relative zero block sizes around 0.25 and large stage-wise equality constraint jacobians.

</details>


### [138] [Nechvile-Transformed Spacecraft Dynamics and Propellant Computation in the 3-Body Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29271)
*Michael J. Dixon,Isaac M. Ross*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文指出在Nechvile框架下，向限制性三体问题的运动方程中直接添加外力项的传统做法是错误的，正确的处理需要将外力乘以$(1 + e \cosθ)^{-3}$因子，并导致火箭方程的修正；应用于地月空间任务时，使用正确代价函数可节省80%推进剂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上认为在限制性三体问题的运动方程中加入外力是简单的线性叠加，但该文发现这种做法忽略了旋转参考系中相对偏心率和真近点角的影响，从而导致控制设计和燃料估算的严重偏差，因此需重新审视外力的引入方式。

Method: 基于Nechvile框架下的无控运动方程，引入经$(1 + e \cosθ)^{-3}$修正的外力项，并结合质量流率方程推导出修正后的动力学模型与相应的Birkhoff理论解法，用于求解地月轨道转移任务中的最优控制问题。

Result: 发现外力项必须乘以$(1 + e \cosθ)^{-3}$才能正确建模；该修正导致动力学方程中出现非匹配的二次与三次项，进而修改了传统的火箭方程；在示例任务中，使用正确的代价函数相比传统二次代价可节省超过80%的推进剂。

Conclusion: 在非惯性旋转框架下进行航天器轨迹优化时，不能简单地将外力直接加入加速度项，必须考虑参考系几何特性对动力学的调制作用；采用修正后的模型可极大提高燃料效率，挑战了现有最优控制中广泛使用的二次代价假设。

Abstract: The uncontrolled equations of motion in the Nechvile frame for the restricted three-body problem have been well-known since at least the 1960s. It would seem that adding an external force to these equations is quite trivial: simply add an external force per mass term to the acceleration equations. Here we show that the last statement is not true. In fact, we show that the additive generic external force must be multiplied by the inverse of $(1 + e \cosθ)^3$ where $e$ is the relative eccentricity of the primaries and $θ$ is the true anomaly of the rotating frame located at the barycenter. Furthermore, when this result is combined with the mass flow rate equation, it generates several surprising results due to the mismatch between the resulting quadratic term and the cubic term in the equations of motion. This leads to a corresponding modification of the rocket equation itself. A Birkhoff-theoretic solution to an illustrative cislunar space mission problem shows propellent savings of 80% with the use of the correct cost functional. The popular quadratic cost utilizes more than $2X$ the minimum propellant consumption.

</details>


### [139] [Value of Information in Dynamic Decision Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29273)
*Dimitry Shaiderman,Eilon Solan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究了在预测有限马尔可夫链状态演化过程中信息的价值，提出预测学习指数来衡量信息获取效率，并分析了不同初始分布下信息价值的变化特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨在部分可观测环境下，信息对预测马尔可夫链未来状态的价值及其收敛特性。

Method: 基于马尔可夫链模型，分析决策者在每阶段选择状态并获得匹配反馈的信息结构，研究信息价值随观测次数变化的规律。

Result: 当链从不变分布开始时，最优终值随观测次数非减且以一致指数速率收敛；引入预测学习指数，表明其可能为有限或无限；而对于非平稳初始分布，价值可能随时间范围严格递减。

Conclusion: 信息价值在平稳初始条件下具有良好的收敛性质，但依赖于初始分布特性，预测学习指数可用于刻画信息利用的充分性。

Abstract: We study the value of information in predicting the evolving state of a finite Markov chain. At each stage, a decision maker chooses a state and observes only whether the current state of the chain matches her choice; the resulting information is used to make a prediction on the state at the final stage of the problem. We show that, when the chain starts from an invariant distribution, the optimal terminal value is non-decreasing with the number of observations and converges at a uniform exponential rate. We introduce the predictive learning index, which measures whether all attainable informational value is extracted after finitely many observations, and show that both finite and infinite indices may occur. In contrast, for nonstationary initial distributions, the value may strictly decrease with the horizon.

</details>


### [140] [Regularized Coupling Maps on Matrix Polytopes: From Assignment to Graph Matching and Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29299)
*Binrui Shen,Shengxin Zhu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了线性分配、图匹配和最优传输三类经典问题中的正则化耦合映射，分析了KL散度正则化与欧几里得正则化在不同几何结构下的算法特性及其在图匹配与最优传输中的互补作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于三类经典问题共享相似的矩阵耦合结构，作者旨在统一分析不同正则化方法如何影响耦合映射，并揭示其在不同任务中的算法表现差异。

Method: 通过分析熵/KL正则化与Frobenius/欧几里得正则化在相同矩阵多面体上的投影机制，比较二者在图匹配（如CSGO, ASM, FRAM）和最优传输（如IP-EOT, PSN）中的应用模式。

Result: 发现KL正则化适用于指数化得分矩阵并通过KL投影实现耦合，而欧几里得正则化则通过欧氏投影直接缩放输入矩阵；两种正则化方法在不同任务中表现出互补的算法优势。

Conclusion: 正则化选择决定了优化的几何结构，而外部任务类型决定了精度标准，因此应根据具体任务需求选择合适的正则化方法以获得最优性能。

Abstract: Three classical problems-linear assignment, graph matching, and optimal transport-share a common structure: they seek an optimal matrix-valued coupling on a matrix polytope. We study regularized coupling maps that encompass two families of regularization: entropy/KL regularization, which exponentiates the input score matrix and projects it onto the relevant matrix polytope via the KL-projection; and the Frobenius/Euclidean regularization, which performs the Euclidean projection of the scaled input matrix onto the same polytope. We trace how these two geometries yield complementary algorithmic tools for graph matching (CSGO, ASM, FRAM) and optimal transport (IP-EOT, PSN). The regularized coupling map serves as a direction generator in graph matching and as a solution representation in optimal transport, with the regularizer determining the geometry and the outer task determining the precision standard.

</details>


### [141] [Negative-Curvature-Informed L-BFGS via Minimal Secant Corrections for Finite Minimax Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29300)
*Wenzhe Zhao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于无约束有限极小极大问题的有限记忆拟牛顿法，通过截断双曲平滑模型处理非光滑最大值，并在检测到真实负曲率时修正L-BFGS的曲率对，设计了三种修正规则且均保持正定性，方法无需特征分解或Krylov搜索，证明了算法的全局收敛性和局部还原为标准L-BFGS的性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对无约束有限极小极大问题中由于非光滑性和负曲率导致优化困难的问题，需要一种既能处理非光滑性又能有效利用拟牛顿法内存效率的方法，同时避免复杂操作如特征分解或负曲率搜索。

Method: 采用截断双曲平滑模型处理非光滑的最大函数，在L-BFGS框架下仅当检测到真实负曲率时修改曲率对；提出了三种修正规则（符号翻转、欧氏最近点问题、B_k^{-1}度量下的最近点问题），均确保正割条件s_k^T\ytilde_k=|s_k^Ty_k|成立；使用标准BFGS更新和L-BFGS两层递归，不引入额外复杂操作。

Result: 证明了所有接受的曲率对保持正定性，满足下降条件并在明确假设下实现Armijo回溯的有限终止；建立了全局Clarke平稳性结果；显示在强凸邻域内三种修正均失效，算法退化为标准L-BFGS。

Conclusion: 该方法在保持L-BFGS简洁高效的同时，有效处理了极小极大问题中的非光滑与负曲率挑战，具备良好的理论收敛性，且无需额外计算开销如特征分解或负曲率探测。

Abstract: We study a limited-memory quasi-Newton method for unconstrained finite minimax problems. The nonsmooth maximum is handled by a truncated hyperbolic smoothing model, while the curvature pairs used by L-BFGS are modified only when a genuine negative-curvature observation is detected. Three correction rules are developed. The first replaces $y_k$ by $-y_k$; the second is obtained from the Euclidean nearest-point problem; and the third is obtained from the nearest-point problem in the $B_k^{-1}$ metric. All three rules enforce the same positive secant curvature $s_k^T\ytilde_k=|s_k^Ty_k|$ on a strict negative-curvature pair. The standard BFGS update and the standard L-BFGS two-loop recursion are then used without any change. In particular, the method does not require an eigendecomposition, a Krylov negative-curvature search, or an isotropic shift $B_k+μ_kI$. We prove that every accepted pair preserves positive definiteness, establish the sufficient-descent property and finite termination of the Armijo backtracking under explicit metric and smoothing assumptions, and derive a global Clarke-stationarity result for the continuation framework. We also show that all three corrections become inactive in a sufficiently strongly convex neighborhood, so the local method reduces to ordinary L-BFGS. The paper separates the common algorithmic framework from the choice of the negative-curvature correction, which allows the three variants to be compared under identical smoothing, line-search, and memory rules.

</details>


### [142] [Strike Price Optimization for ISO New England's Day-Ahead Ancillary Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29366)
*Karl Zhu,Parviz Alivand,Jinye Zhao,Tongxin Zheng,Dimitris Bertsimas*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种优化框架，用于确定ISO新英格兰日前辅助服务中储备产品的最高行权价格，在确保资源有激励准备并满足储备需求的同时，解决效率与可靠性的权衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有行权价格规则未充分考虑不同资源的激励差异和储备需求，可能导致资源准备不足或市场效率低下。

Method: 构建了一个优化框架，通过推导基于条件电价分布和资源价格影响界限的紧致下界证书，确定每个资源的激励阈值，并据此进行线性可行性检查以求得精确解。

Result: 研究表明，任何有限最优行权价格均出现在某一资源的激励阈值处；使用ISO-NE数据和典型燃气资源模拟发现，高热耗率的燃烧涡轮机比联合循环机组更可能限制行权价格的选择。

Conclusion: 该方法能有效平衡资源激励与系统可靠性，为异质性资源环境下的行权价格设定提供了可操作且精确的解决方案。

Abstract: ISO New England's (ISO-NE) Day-Ahead Ancillary Services Initiative settles reserve products as financial call options on real-time energy prices. The system-wide strike price creates an efficiency-reliability tradeoff: increasing it lowers competitive reserve offers, but weakens resources' incentives to incur preparation costs and remain available for real-time performance. The existing strike price rule does not explicitly account for heterogeneous resource incentives and reserve requirements. We develop an optimization framework that selects the highest strike price while ensuring that enough resources retain an incentive to prepare and collectively satisfy the reserve requirements. Because a resource's preparation decision may affect the resulting real-time price distribution, its incentive depends on an unobservable counterfactual. To address this, we derive a tight lower-bound certificate using only the available conditional price distribution and a bound on the resource's price impact. We show that each resource enters the optimization through a single incentive threshold and that any finite optimal strike price occurs at one of these thresholds. This yields a tractable exact solution method based on threshold calculations and a small number of linear feasibility checks. Using reconstructed ISO-NE conditional price distributions and representative gas-fired resources, we find that higher heat-rate combustion turbines are more likely than combined-cycle resources to constrain the strike price choice.

</details>


### [143] [The method of ellipcenters with momentum and relaxation for convex quadratic minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29454)
*Roger Behling,Vincent Guigues,Harry Oviedo*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了椭心法（ME）的两种新变体：RelaxME和MomME，用于求解二次优化问题。二者均被证明具有线性收敛性，并在矩阵仅有两个不同特征值时可一步收敛。数值实验表明，MomME在多数情况下迭代次数最少，且与共轭梯度法在CPU时间上表现相当，展现出良好潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为提升椭心法（ME）在二次优化问题中的性能，特别是加速收敛速度并增强对病态问题的适应能力，本文引入带有松弛和动量机制的改进版本。

Method: 提出两种ME的变体：RelaxME（增加松弛步骤）和MomME（引入动量机制）；理论分析其在线性收敛性及特殊谱结构下的一步收敛性；并通过多组数值实验与共轭梯度法、Barzilai-Borwein方法等进行比较。

Result: 证明了RelaxME和MomME的线性收敛性，并显示当二次型矩阵仅有两个不同特征值时，三种方法均可一步收敛；实验结果表明MomME在迭代次数上最优，而MomME与共轭梯度法在计算时间上表现最佳。

Conclusion: MomME显著提升了椭心类方法的效率，结合动量后在迭代效率和计算时间上可媲美经典优化器，为ME类方法在更广泛问题中的应用提供了乐观前景。

Abstract: The method of ellipcenters (ME) is a recent technique developed for unconstrained minimization. Its iteration relies only on first order information and consists of building a suitable two-dimensional ellipse that tries to capture intrinsic ill-conditioning of the problem. The center of the ellipse is taken as the next iterate, which justifies the name of the method. ME was already shown to converge linearly when the objective function is smooth and strongly convex. The special case when the objective is quadratic was studied in the first paper on ME and is also the subject of our work here. In this paper, we propose two variants of ME: RelaxME which adds in ME an additional relaxation step at each iteration and MomME which embeds momentum in ME. We prove linear convergence of RelaxME and MomME and, in particular, we show convergence of RelaxME and MomME (and also of ME) in one iteration when the matrix of the quadratic form has only two distinct eigenvalues. Finally, we provide the results of numerical experiments which compare on five optimization problems (and different combinations of parameters defining these problems) ME, RelaxME, and MomME, with 3 other optimizers: the conjugate gradient method [10], Barzilai and Borwein gradient method with long step [2], and the gradient method with adaptive spectral step length [7]. On most instances, MomME provides the smallest number of iterations and conjugate gradient and MomME provide the smallest CPU times and similar CPU times. This opens optimistic possibilities for ME with momentum in broader settings.

</details>


### [144] [Stochastic Optimal Control of Hawkes Jump-Diffusion Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29473)
*Daria Sakhanda,Joshué Helí Ricalde-Guerrero*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文发展了一个基于标记Hawkes过程的随机增长模型框架，用于研究环境灾难风险下资本积累与污染的相互作用，证明了在自激励效应较小时，Hawkes模型的价值函数收敛于泊松模型，提供了对自激励效应影响的量化分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了更真实地刻画环境灾难风险的时序聚集性，传统泊松模型无法捕捉历史灾难对后续风险的影响，因此需要引入具有自激励特性的Hawkes过程来改进模型。

Method: 采用次临界马尔可夫Hawkes过程建模灾难发生机制，并通过增广状态空间处理自激励风险；利用Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman方程分析相应的随机控制问题，在小激励尺度下证明Hawkes价值函数向泊松情形的收敛性。

Result: 建立了受控Hawkes动力学的良好适定性和非爆炸性，获得了自激励过程的定量估计，并严格证明了当自激励强度趋于零时，Hawkes模型的价值函数收敛到泊松模型的结果。

Conclusion: 该框架为包含灾难风险自激励特征的经济增长模型提供了理论基础，验证了泊松近似的合理性，并量化了自激励效应对最优增长路径的影响。

Abstract: This paper is devoted to developing a framework for stochastic growth models with environmental risk, in which rare but catastrophic shocks interact with capital accumulation and pollution. Building on the Poisson point process formulation studied in arXiv:2511.13568, we extend the model to disasters driven by a marked Hawkes process, allowing past disasters to temporarily increase the likelihood of subsequent shocks. Our work focuses on a subcritical Markovian Hawkes specification, in which the state space is augmented by the self-excitation component of disaster risk. We establish the well-posedness and nonexplosion of the resulting controlled Hawkes dynamics. Using the Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman characterization of the corresponding Poisson control problem, we obtain quantitative estimates for the Hawkes excitation process and prove, under a small-excitation scaling, that the Hawkes value function converges to its Poisson counterpart as the magnitude of self-excitation vanishes. This provides a rigorous Poisson approximation of the stochastic control problem and quantifies the effect of self-excitation on optimal growth under environmental disaster risk.

</details>


### [145] [Proof of a Conjecture of De Cock and De Moor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29479)
*Jeffrey Humpherys*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对De Cock和De Moor提出的关于随机子空间辨识中两种不同观点之间联系的猜想，给出了一个直接的有限维证明，利用秩一扰动揭示了隐藏的Cauchy矩阵结构，并通过有理插值和密度连续性论证强化了原结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决数学系统与控制理论中的未解问题，验证连接Lyapunov方程与主角度及典型相关分析之间的猜想。

Method: 采用秩一扰动方法揭示Cauchy矩阵结构，结合有理插值与密度连续性论证，在非共振条件下进行直接有限维证明。

Result: 在非共振条件下无需稳定性或可对角化假设即完成证明；原假设中的非奇异性自动满足；在稠密开集上两矩阵相似而非仅共谱。

Conclusion: 该结果加强了原有猜想的表述，提供了更广泛适用的理论支持，并独立于其他方法开辟了新的证明路径。

Abstract: De Cock and De Moor proposed a conjecture connecting two seemingly different viewpoints in stochastic subspace identification, one based on Lyapunov equations and the other on principal angles and canonical correlations. The conjecture was recorded as Problem 9.1 of \emph{Unsolved Problems in Mathematical Systems and Control Theory}. We give a direct finite-dimensional proof under the natural nonresonance condition, without requiring stability or diagonalizability. The key mechanism is the rank-one perturbation, which exposes a hidden Cauchy-matrix structure and reduces the problem to rational interpolation. A density and continuity argument then removes the generic spectral assumptions. The result strengthens the original statement. The eigenvalues agree with algebraic multiplicity, a nonsingularity assumption of the original formulation becomes automatic, and on a dense open set of parameters the two matrices are similar rather than merely cospectral. While this manuscript was being prepared, Gillberg and Löfberg independently posted a proof based on a Lyapunov-kernel identity and the classical $AB$--$BA$ principle. The proof given here was developed independently and follows a different route.

</details>


### [146] [Online Gate-Driven Flow Control in Resin Transfer Moulding Using a Neural-Network Surrogate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29521)
*Nicholas Wright,Oliver Maclaren,Piaras Kelly,Suresh Advani,Ruanui Nicholson*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合估计与控制的策略，利用压力传感器数据和神经网络代理模型实时优化树脂传递模塑中的填充过程，以减少未饱和区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在树脂传递模塑中，由于赛道效应导致流动前沿不均匀推进，容易形成干斑，需要确保纤维预制体完全饱和。

Method: 采用迭代扩展卡尔曼滤波器估计未知的赛道效应强度，并结合神经网络代理模型替代昂贵的有限元-控制体积模型，实时优化辅助入口压力。利用贝叶斯近似误差框架处理代理模型与原模型之间的差异，并证明流前沿几何仅依赖于时间平均的入口压力，从而简化训练。

Result: 在典型的分叉几何结构上验证了该方法，结果显示与无控制基线相比，未填充节点数量显著减少，但改进程度强烈依赖于辅助入口的位置和数量。

Conclusion: 所提出的估计与控制框架能够有效改善树脂传递模塑中的填充均匀性，减少干斑缺陷，具备实际应用潜力，但需合理设计辅助入口布局。

Abstract: In resin transfer moulding, complete saturation of the fibre preform is necessary before the resin front reaches the outlet vent(s), to prevent dry-spot formation. In practice, the flow front rarely advances uniformly due to race-tracking effects. We propose a combined estimation and control strategy to address this issue. We use pressure-sensor data collected during filling to estimate the unknown race-tracking strengths via an iterated extended Kalman filter, and to simultaneously optimise auxiliary gate pressures to prevent the resin from arriving at the vent before complete saturation occurs. To make this approach feasible in real time, we replace the expensive finite element-control volume model with neural network surrogate models. The Bayesian approximation error framework is used to account for the discrepancy between the surrogates and the original model. To make the training of the surrogates feasible with time-dependent control actions, we prove that the flow-front geometry depends only on the time-averaged gate pressures. We demonstrate the methodology on a representative forked geometry, showing that the combined estimation-and-control framework can substantially reduce the number of unfilled nodes at the end of filling compared with an uncontrolled baseline. However, the level of improvement depends strongly on the location and number of auxiliary gates.

</details>


### [147] [Reciprocal-Manifold Annealed KKT Flows for Constrained Optimization: Application to the Nonconvex AC Optimal Power Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29628)
*M Parimi,Aditi Ramteke,Rachit Mehra,Arun Mahindrakar,Navdeep Singh*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种保持全程可行性的连续时间优化框架，适用于安全关键的非线性约束问题，无需投影或每步求解二次规划，计算复杂度由立方降至线性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有优化方法在中间步骤可能违反约束，不适用于安全关键场景；需要一种全程保持可行且高效的方法。

Method: 基于互惠乘子流形设计连续乘子更新律，使动力系统等价于连续对数障碍梯度下降，并引入增强型Uzawa流抑制振荡。

Result: 在IEEE 9/57节点系统AC最优潮流问题中，收敛解接近基准最优值0.4%，全程严格满足约束，单步计算复杂度由立方降为线性。

Conclusion: 该框架能有效实现约束非线性问题的全程可行性、高效计算与鲁棒动态跟踪，适合实时安全关键应用。

Abstract: Safety-critical optimization applications, such as real-time power system operation, maintain feasibility at every intermediate step, not merely at convergence. Existing approaches either violate constraints mid-solve (interior-point methods) or enforce feasibility through per-instant quadratic programming subproblems with cubic computational cost and unbounded worst-case execution time. We propose a continuous-time optimization framework for smooth constrained nonlinear problems that preserves feasibility throughout the optimization process without requiring projection operators, quadratic programming subproblems, or other per-iteration optimization routines.
  The method is built around a reciprocal multiplier manifold, which establishes an explicit relationship between inequality constraints and their associated Lagrange multipliers. By designing a continuous multiplier update law, the manifold is shown to remain forward invariant, while the resulting dynamics are equivalent to continuous-time logarithmic barrier gradient descent. The proposed framework naturally extends to multiple inequality constraints, equality constraints, nonconvex feasible sets, and infeasible initial conditions. The method is further enhanced through an augmented Uzawa flow that eliminates oscillatory transients commonly observed in classical primal-dual saddle-point dynamics.
  The effectiveness of the proposed approach is applied to the AC Optimal Power Flow problem of IEEE 9-bus and IEEE 57-bus systems. Numerical results show convergence to solutions within 0.4\% of the benchmark optimum while maintaining strict feasibility of all constraints. A computational complexity analysis shows that the proposed dynamics reduce the per-step computational cost from cubic to linear complexity. Finally, dynamic tracking studies under time-varying operating conditions demonstrate reliable feasibility preservation.

</details>


### [148] [Transformer-Based Flow Shop Scheduling Using MILP-Generated Training Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29690)
*Roderich Wallrath*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该研究探索了使用Transformer模型解决带有辅助资源的非置换流水车间调度问题，通过将作业-机器-资源元组表示为token序列，并利用MILP生成的数据进行训练，在推理时采用约束解码完成调度。实验表明，Transformer在求解质量上优于遗传算法、NEH启发式和随机搜索，仅略逊于MILP和迭代贪婪算法，展示了其在固定重复作业集场景下的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统运筹学方法在处理复杂调度问题时存在计算瓶颈，而Transformer擅长捕捉序列中复杂的上下文依赖关系，因此探索其在非置换流水车间调度中的应用具有重要意义。

Method: 将调度问题建模为next-token预测任务，使用MILP生成的最优调度序列作为训练数据，对job-machine-secondary resource三元组进行token化；训练后的Transformer在推理阶段通过随机生成前缀并结合约束解码完成完整调度序列。

Result: 在8个作业、4台机器和3个辅助资源的实例上，Transformer生成的调度方案比GA、NEH和随机搜索更优（更小的makespan），仅弱于MILP和IG启发式。

Conclusion: Transformer能够从MILP优化的调度序列中学习到一定模式，基于Transformer的调度方法是值得进一步研究的方向，尤其适用于具有固定且重复作业集合的生产环境。

Abstract: Advances in machine learning (ML) have created new opportunities to complement traditional operations research (OR) methods. In particular, transformer models can capture complex interactions in token sequences by mapping tokens into a high-dimensional embedding space and propagating contextual information via attention. This makes them a candidate to model non-permutation flow shop scheduling with secondary resources as a next-token prediction task, where tokens represent job-machine-secondary resource tuples. For training, mixed-integer linear programming (MILP)-generated schedules are tokenized and used as next-token prediction data. During inference, partial token sequences (prefixes) are randomly generated and completed by the trained transformer through constrained decoding. A computational study is conducted on a flow shop with 8 jobs, 4 machines, and 3 secondary resources, where jobs are selected from a fixed pool of 20 jobs that is sampled during training and provides the candidates during prefix completion. The transformer achieves better solution quality (smaller makespans) compared to a genetic algorithm (GA), the NEH heuristic, and random search. It is outperformed only by the MILP model and the iterated greedy (IG) heuristic. The study concludes that transformer models can, to some extent, learn patterns from MILP-optimized non-permutation flow shop schedules and that transformer-based scheduling represents an interesting direction for future research, particularly in settings with a fixed, recurring job set.

</details>


### [149] [Successive design of backstepping observers for parabolic PDE-ODE systems and its duality to state feedback stabilization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29693)
*Nicole Gehring*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于后退步法的连续观测器设计方法，用于严格前馈形式的抛物型PDE-ODE耦合系统，其结构与已有的多步状态反馈设计对偶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对具有复杂耦合结构的严格前馈PDE-ODE系统，传统观测器设计难以有效处理误差传播问题，因此需要一种能按特定顺序稳定误差并解耦子系统的新型观测器设计方法。

Method: 首先利用虚拟测量进行变换以稳定远离测量端的ODE观测误差子系统，并将其与PDE误差状态解耦；然后采用Volterra积分变换稳定PDE误差子系统，将整体误差动态转化为级联的指数稳定ODE和PDE子系统。

Result: 成功构建了一个级联的、指数稳定的观测误差系统，实现了对严格前馈PDE-ODE系统的有效状态估计，并揭示了该观测器设计与已有反馈控制设计之间的对偶性。

Conclusion: 所提出的后退步观测器设计方法在结构上与多步反馈控制对偶，能够有效处理严格前馈PDE-ODE系统的观测问题，为这类系统的控制与估计提供了统一的设计框架。

Abstract: The paper introduces a successive backstepping observer design for strictly feedforward parabolic PDE-ODE systems, in which the coupling structure determines the order of error stabilization and the corresponding transformations. First, a transformation based on a virtual measurement stabilizes the ODE observer error subsystem, which is most distal from the measurement, while decoupling it from the PDE error state. Second, a Volterra integral transformation is employed to stabilize the PDE error subsystem and to map the overall error dynamics into a cascade of exponentially stable ODE and PDE subsystems. The design is shown to be dual to a recently proposed multi-step state feedback design for parabolic PDE-ODE systems in strict feedback form, thus explaining the structure of the presented observer design.

</details>


### [150] [A Unified Perspective on Conformal Prediction and Wasserstein Distributionally Robust Optimization for Uncertainty Quantification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29789)
*Kehan Long,Yiqi Zhao,Pol Mestres,Lars Lindemann,Nikolay Atanasov,Jorge Cortés*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了共形预测（CP）和分布鲁棒优化（DRO）的统一概率视角，将其视为基于有限校准数据构建数据依赖分位数估计器的方法，用于保证测试得分低于该估计值的高概率。CP通过提升分位数水平进行修正，而DRO通过模糊集半径在值空间进行修正。两者提供相同的校准条件保证，但在尾部行为上表现不同：CP依赖稀疏的上尾顺序统计量，容易在样本稀疏时过度校正，而DRO在值空间修正可能避免此问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于在有限样本和测试时分布偏移下决策的可靠性至关重要，需要对不确定性进行量化。现有方法CP和DRO分别从不同角度解决该问题，但缺乏统一理解。因此，本文旨在建立两者的统一概率框架以揭示其内在联系与差异。

Method: 将CP和DRO统一为基于有限校准数据生成数据依赖分位数估计器的方法，分析二者在校正经验分位数时的不同方式：CP在校正分位数水平，DRO在校正分位数值，并比较其在校准条件保证、尾部分布敏感性等方面的表现。

Result: 发现CP和DRO提供相同的校准条件覆盖保证，但构造方式不同：CP采用分布无关的闭式水平校正，DRO采用依赖未知分布特性的值空间校正，并能在模糊集上提供一致覆盖保证；在尾部行为上，CP易因稀疏样本导致估计过调，而适当选择DRO半径可避免该问题。

Conclusion: CP和DRO是同一类分位数估计器的两种实现方式，具有互补优势。统一视角有助于理解其理论基础，并为在实际应用中结合二者优势提供指导，特别是在处理尾部稀疏数据时DRO可能更具稳定性。

Abstract: Uncertainty quantification from finite data is central to machine learning, optimization, and automation systems, where decisions must remain reliable under limited samples and test-time distribution shift. Conformal prediction (CP) and distributionally robust optimization (DRO) offer two complementary approaches: CP constructs data-dependent prediction sets with distribution-free finite-sample validity under exchangeability, while DRO optimizes worst-case performance over an ambiguity set around an empirical distribution. We develop a unified probabilistic perspective on CP and DRO by viewing both as ways to turn finite calibration data into a data-dependent quantile estimator that a test score falls below with high probability. From this perspective, CP and DRO correct the empirical quantile along two coordinates of the same family of estimators: CP inflates the quantile level, whereas DRO shifts the quantile value through an ambiguity radius. Both methods provide the same calibration-conditional guarantee for the true distribution, requiring the target coverage to hold with high probability over the calibration sample. Their constructions differ, however: CP uses a closed-form, distribution-free level correction, while DRO uses a value-space correction whose certified radius depends on properties of the unknown distribution and additionally guarantees coverage uniformly over the ambiguity set. This distinction emerges in the tails of the score distribution. Because CP relies on sparse upper-tail order statistics of the calibration samples, its level inflation barely moves the estimator when those samples are dense near the target quantile but overshoots when they are sparse, whereas a well-chosen DRO radius corrects in value space and may avoid this overshoot.

</details>


### [151] [Quiver Semistability and Structured Kalman Decompositions for Networked Linear Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29871)
*Kazuo Murota,Tasuku Soma*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了基于σ-半稳定性的网络化线性时不变系统的新可控性和可观测性概念，利用King准则定义了保持网络结构的Kalman型分解，并给出了相应的高效判定算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了更好地理解和分析网络化LTI系统的结构特性，需要发展新的可控性和可观测性理论。

Method: 采用σ-半稳定性理论和King准则，提出了一种新的Kalman分解方法，能够同时分解局部动态和连接动态。

Result: 开发了用于判断所提可控性和可观测性的高效算法，以及寻找Kalman型分解的方法；还提供了针对特定类型箭图表示的σ-半稳定性判定算法。

Conclusion: 新提出的框架不仅深化了对网络化LTI系统结构性质的理解，而且为实际应用中的系统设计与分析提供了有力工具。

Abstract: We introduce new notions of controllability and observability for networked linear time-invariant (LTI) systems based on $σ$-semistability of quiver representations. Utilizing King's criterion for $σ$-semistability, we define a network generalization of the Kalman decomposition for networked LTI systems, which systematically decomposes the local and interconnection dynamics while respecting the underlying network structure. Furthermore, we present efficient algorithms for deciding the proposed controllability and observability of a given networked LTI system and for finding the Kalman-type decomposition. We also show efficient algorithms for deciding the $σ$-semistability of representations of acyclic quivers with self-loops if the weight $σ$ has the same sign for all vertices with self-loops. Such quiver representations and weights arise from networked LTI systems.

</details>


### [152] [Sharp Singularity-Degree Bounds for Equality-Generated SDP-RLT Relaxations of Binary Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29945)
*Hao Hu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了二元集合Shor松弛结合第一层RLT等式后的SDP-RLT松弛的最坏情况奇异性度，并给出了基于矩阵秩和零度的精确上界，显著低于一般情况下的上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奇异性度是半定规划退化程度的重要指标，但通常无法从问题数据事先得知，因此需要寻找可预先确定的上界以辅助分析可行性距离与约束残差之间的误差估计。

Method: 通过将定义线性等式生成的第一层Reformulation-Linearization Technique（RLT）方程加入到非空二元集合的Shor松弛中，构建等式生成的SDP-RLT松弛，并分析其奇异性度。

Result: 当矩阵A的秩为m且0<m<n时，该松弛的奇异性度至多为min{m, n-m}，且此秩-零度界在所有可能的秩范围内均可达到；因此该类问题的最坏情况奇异性度为⌊n/2⌋（n≥2）。

Conclusion: 相较于一般可行SDP系统中n+1阶矩阵变量对应的尖锐上界n，该方法提供的奇异性度上界显著更小，从而为个别松弛问题提供了可预先判断的奇异性度及Hölder指数估计依据。

Abstract: Singularity degree is an important measure of semidefinite programming (SDP) degeneracy, but it is generally unavailable a priori from the problem data. We augment the Shor relaxation of nonempty binary sets $\{x\in\{0,1\}^n:Ax=b\}$ with the first-level Reformulation-Linearization Technique (RLT) equations generated by the defining linear equalities. For the resulting equality-generated SDP-RLT relaxation, we determine the exact worst-case singularity degree. If $\operatorname{rank}(A)=m$ and $0<m<n$, then the associated relaxation has singularity degree at most $\min\{m,n-m\}$, and this rank-nullity bound is attained for every possible rank in this range. Consequently, the worst-case singularity degree over this class is $\lfloor n/2\rfloor$ for $n\geq 2$. This is strikingly smaller than the sharp general bound $n$ for feasible SDP systems with matrix variables of order $n+1$ (Sturm, 2000, Example 2). Thus, for individual relaxations, rank and nullity provide an a priori bound on the otherwise inaccessible singularity degree and on the Hölder exponent in error bounds estimating distance to feasibility from constraint residuals.

</details>


### [153] [On the suboptimality of stochastic MPC with varying constraint horizon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30017)
*Allan Andre Do Nascimento,Andre Bertolace,Antonis Papachristodoulou,Kostas Margellos*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究了在模型预测控制中，仅在较短的约束范围内施加机会约束的方法，通过随机松弛动态规划推导出闭环成本的上界，并针对线性二次问题提供了确定性重构方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了降低在全预测范围内施加随机状态约束带来的计算负担。

Method: 使用随机松弛动态规划，在较短的约束范围内施加机会约束，并推导出平均期望闭环成本的显式上界；对具有仿射机会约束和有界均匀扰动的线性二次问题，通过坐标变换和约束收紧提供确定性重构。

Result: 得到了依赖于预测范围和约束范围的闭环成本上界，提出了适用于特定问题的确定性重构方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法能够在减少计算量的同时保持可接受的性能，揭示了计算复杂度与控制性能之间的权衡。

Abstract: Enforcing stochastic state constraints over the full prediction horizon in Model Predictive Control (MPC) can be computationally demanding. Here we study stochastic MPC without terminal ingredients in which chance constraints are enforced only over a shorter constraint horizon. Using stochastic relaxed dynamic programming, we derive an explicit upper bound on the average expected closed-loop cost that depends on both prediction and constraint horizons. For linear quadratic problems with affine chance constraints and bounded uniform disturbances, we provide a deterministic reformulation via coordinate transformation and constraint tightening. Simulations illustrate the trade-off between computational effort and performance.

</details>


### [154] [Interception Conditions in Multi-Agent Pursuit Games Governed by Linear Dynamics with Gronwall-type Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30123)
*David Terna. Gbande,Abbas Ja'afaru Badakaya,Mehdi Salimi,Aminu Sulaiman Halliru,Jamilu Adamu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了多智能体追逃问题，提出了一种在固定时间范围内确保追捕成功的策略，并给出了保证拦截的充分条件，通过数值例子验证了理论结果的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在存在干扰和控制约束的情况下实现多追捕者对逃逸者的有效拦截，研究需要构建可行的追捕策略并分析成功拦截的条件。

Method: 采用一阶线性微分方程描述追逃双方的运动，利用Gronwall型约束限制控制输入，并构造追捕者的可行政策，推导出确保拦截成功的充分条件。

Result: 得出了在规定时间内实现成功拦截的充分条件，并通过数值仿真验证了所提策略的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的追捕策略能够在有限时间内有效拦截逃逸者，且理论条件能够保证追捕的成功，即使在逃逸者采取对抗行为时依然成立。

Abstract: This paper investigates a multi-agent pursuit problem in which a group of pursuers seeks to intercept one or more evaders within a fixed operational time horizon. The motion of both pursuers and evaders is described by first-order linear differential equations, and the control inputs available to the agents are subject to Gronwall-type constraints. Pursuit is considered successful when at least one pursuer reaches the position of each evader at a finite time within the prescribed duration. Within this framework, we construct permissible strategies for the pursuers and derive sufficient conditions under which interception is guaranteed despite the evaders' counteractions. To support the theoretical results, we present numerical examples that demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed strategies and confirm the analytical conditions for successful pursuit.

</details>


### [155] [Analysis of First-Order Linear Pursuit and Evasion Differential Games with Gronwall-Type Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30128)
*David Terna Gbande,Abbas Ja'afaru Badakaya,Jamilu Adamu,Mehdi Salimi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究了在R^n空间中单个追击者与逃避者之间的追逃问题，玩家的运动由一阶线性微分方程描述，控制函数受Gronwall型不等式约束。追击完成当且仅当追击者与逃避者的状态重合；若在整个过程中两者状态始终不一致，则逃避成功。结果依赖于双方的可达域，并给出了逃避成功的充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨在固定时间内、受限控制条件下，高维空间中追击与逃避问题的可行性及其数学条件。

Method: 利用微分方程建模玩家运动，结合Gronwall型不等式分析控制约束，并通过可达域分析追击与逃避的结果。

Result: 得到了追击完成的条件，依赖于双方的可达域关系；同时给出了一种逃避可能的充分条件。

Conclusion: 在给定控制约束和固定时间下，追击或逃避的成功取决于双方可达域的结构，逃避策略的存在性被严格证明。

Abstract: We examine pursuit and evasion problems in the space $ \mathbb{R}^{n} $ involving a lone pursuer and evader. Motion of each player is governed by a first-order linear differential equation. The players' control functions are subject to the Gronwall-type inequality. The game's duration is fixed and represented by a positive number $\vartheta $. When the state of a pursuer coincides with that of the evader, we then say the pursuit is completed. On the contrary, evasion is possible when throughout the game there is no agreement between the states of pursuer and evader. The result obtained related to the pursuit problem depends on the attainability domains of the two players. On the other hand, one sufficient condition is given for evasion to be possible.

</details>


### [156] [Solution and Optimal Strategies for a Differential Game of Pursuit-Evasion with Point-Wise Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30134)
*Jamilu Adamu,Abbas Ja'afaru Badakaya,Felix Wallace Tarry,Mehdi Salimi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究了在l2空间中，多个追击者与单个逃避者之间的微分博弈问题，给出了博弈的解、最优策略及博弈值估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决在序列空间l2中，具有点态几何约束的追逃微分博弈问题，明确多方追击与单方逃避的动力学对抗机制。

Method: 通过建立一阶微分方程描述追击者的运动，二阶微分方程描述逃避者的运动，在固定时间区间内分析终端距离作为支付函数的博弈过程，构造双方的最优策略并估计博弈值。

Result: 得到了该追逃微分博弈的解，包括追击者和逃避者的最优策略构造，并对博弈值进行了有效估计。

Conclusion: 该研究成功求解了在l2空间中多追击者与单逃避者的追逃微分博弈，明确了各方最优行为及博弈结果。

Abstract: This work study a differential game of pursuit-evasion involving countably many pursuers $p_{1},p_{2},\cdots,p_{m}$ and a single evader $e$ under point-wise (geometric) constraints in the sequence space $l_{2}.$ The pursuers evolve according to specified differential equations of $1^{st}$ order while the evader follows a $2^{nd}$ order differential equation. The game period is a fixed time interval of length $θ$ unit of time. The minimum distance between evader and the pursuer at the terminal time denotes the game payoff. The pursuers aim is to minimize the payoff, whereas the evader seeks to maximize it. We obtain the solution of the game, including optimal strategies of the players construction, as well as game value estimation.

</details>


### [157] [Multi-Agent Receding Horizon Games Framework for Autonomous Market Participation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30136)
*Parth Brahmbhatt,Styliani Avraamidou*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于电化学分布式能源资源参与两阶段电力批发市场（日前和实时）的对等（P2P）协同投标方法，基于滚动时域广义纳什均衡博弈，实现隐私保护、分布式优化，并在PJMarket实证中显著提升经济效益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型和中型电化学DERs难以独立进入有最小投标门槛的电力批发市场；现有第三方聚合商模式存在佣金成本和不公平优化问题，且多数P2P方法未考虑市场的两阶段（日前+实时）结构，导致实用性受限。

Method: 构建一个两阶段滚动时域的广义纳什均衡（GNE）博弈模型，多个DER单元在每5分钟重新优化未来一小时的策略，联合满足日前和实时市场要求；各单元仅共享聚合功率信息，不泄露私有成本或发电数据，实现去中心化、自组织的协同投标。

Result: 在PJM市场的六单元 fleet 上验证，相比各自独立参与，该方法使整体利润提高37%，电力成本降低29%，可再生能源弃电减少92%，且每个单元收益均有所提升。

Conclusion: 所提两阶段分布式P2P博弈框架有效解决了中小规模DERs参与批发市场的门槛与公平性问题，在保护隐私的同时实现了更高的经济性和资源利用率，具备实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Electrochemical distributed energy resources (DERs), such as electrolyzers and battery energy storage systems, consume large amounts of electricity. Buying that power directly from wholesale markets can sharply reduce operating costs. Accessing these markets, however, requires a minimum bid size that individual small or medium-scale units struggle to meet. Grouping several units to bid as a single participant clears this barrier while letting each unit use its own low-cost renewable generation. The common remedy is to hire a third-party aggregator, but aggregators charge commissions and solve a centralized optimization that often favors certain units over others. A fairer alternative is peer-to-peer (P2P) participation, where units form a self-governing group and bid jointly with no central authority. Because each self-interested unit is unwilling to share private data, coordination is best posed as a game-theoretic distributed optimization problem. Existing P2P methods, however, address only single-round spot markets and ignore the two-stage structure of real wholesale markets, where participants commit a day ahead and continuously adjust in real time. We close this gap with a two-stage receding-horizon generalized Nash equilibrium (GNE) game. Each unit re-optimizes its strategy every five minutes over a rolling one-hour horizon, while the group satisfies both market stages collectively. Units exchange only publicly visible aggregate power, never private cost or production data. We apply the approach to a six-unit fleet on the PJM market. It delivers 37% higher profit, 29% lower electricity cost, and 92% less renewable curtailment than individual participation, with every unit better off.

</details>
