On the triangular lattice, Anderson insulator, Mott insulator, and charge-density wave (CDW) phases are found in all cases, with the CDW driven by Coulomb-interaction-mediated nesting. Evidence for a charge-liquid regime is found in the region between the weakly correlated regime and the CDW, characterized by competing charge-ordering wavevectors and the absence of universal scaling behavior, whose extent shrinks progressively from the grand-canonical to the conventional half-filling condition. On the kagome lattice, the CDW phase is completely absent; instead, an insulating ground state, plausibly of the Mott type, is found, with signatures of a possible quantum phase transition at zero temperature. Our results establish the crucial role of lattice geometry and particle-hole symmetry breaking in shaping the phase diagram of correlated electron systems.

</details>


### [207] [Enhanced Diffusion from Twist-Angle Disorder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29062)
*Nicole S Ticea,Yi-Ming Wu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 研究了双层扭转系统中扭曲角无序对单粒子性质的影响，发现几何散射约束显著抑制动量弛豫，并可映射到非线性σ模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索微小且不受控制的空间变化如何在双层扭转材料中产生重大影响。

Method: 将扭曲角建模为两层之间相对位移的旋度，并类比电动力学中的磁场理论。

Result: 发现由扭转结构引起的几何散射约束显著抑制了动量弛豫，且该问题可映射到非线性σ模型（NLSM）。

Conclusion: 扭曲角无序对系统的宏观性质如输运和有序倾向有重要影响，且其效应强于白噪声散射。

Abstract: We consider the effect of twist-angle disorder on the single-particle properties of twisted bilayer systems. These materials are a natural playground for studying how tiny, uncontrolled spatial variations may become unusually consequential; a local change in the twist geometry can give rise to spatially-correlated alterations of the parent Hamiltonian, which in turn affect the coarse-grained properties of the substrate such as transport and ordering tendencies. In analogy to electrodynamics, where the magnetic field is given by the curl of the vector potential, we model the twist angle as the curl of the relative displacement between the two bilayers. We find that geometric scattering constraints imposed here by the twist structure substantially suppress momentum relaxation relative to mean-rate-matched white noise. We quantify these effects and show that the problem can be mapped onto a non-linear sigma model (NLSM).

</details>


### [208] [Duality Between Twist-Angle Disorder and Non-Hermitian Disorder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29064)
*Yi-Ming Wu,Nicole S. Ticea*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出并研究了一种莫尔异质双层系统模型，其中 twist 角在有限范围内保持均匀且定义明确，但在更大距离上随机变形。通过复制技巧框架，展示了无序平均在副本之间产生具有交替符号的非平凡相关性，并在每个局部域边界附近沿特定方向最大化。对于低能莫尔带，同样的相关性可以在费米子耦合到非厄米无序的对偶模型中生成，从而动态规避安德森局域化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索 twist 角无序在莫尔系统中的作用机制，并揭示其与传统无序的本质差异。

Method: 采用复制技巧进行无序平均分析，并构建对偶模型研究费米子与非厄米无序的耦合行为。

Result: 发现 twist 角无序导致副本间具有交替符号的非平凡相关性，且该相关性可在非厄米对偶模型中重现，能够动态避免安德森局域化。

Conclusion: 该工作为研究莫尔系统中的 twist 角无序提供了新视角，揭示了其独特物理特性。

Abstract: We propose and study a model of moire heterobilayer systems where the twist angle remains uniform and well defined only within a finite range, but deforms randomly at larger distances. The local twist angle is then subject to a certain probability distribution that penalizes large fluctuations around its mean value. Within the framework of the replica trick, we show that disorder averaging produces a nontrivial correlation with alternating sign between replicas, maximized only close the boundary of each local domain and along certain directions. We show that for the low-energy moire bands, exactly the same correlation can be generated in a dual model where fermions are coupled to non-Hermitian disorder, which can evade Anderson localization dynamically. This duality provides a new perspective to investigate twist-angle disorder in moire systems, and unveils some of the essential differences between twist-angle disorder and conventional disorder.

</details>


### [209] [Real-space manifestation of ferroic multipoles in altermagnetic MnF$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29089)
*Iurii Kibalin,Dalila Bounoua,José A. Rodriguez Velamazán,Oscar Fabelo,Navid Qureshi,Quentin Faure,Philippe Bourges,Victor Balédent,Jian-Rui Soh,Jeffrey Rau,Paul McClarty,Arsen Gukasov*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过极化中子衍射技术，首次在实空间直接观测到典型反铁磁体MnF$_2$中的多极磁序，揭示了氟离子的有限磁矩和锰的各向异性磁化，证实了允许的五阶磁多极子$O_{52}$的存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在直接观察反铁磁体中的磁多极有序，因为此前这类多极序的真实空间观测一直未能实现。

Method: 利用对称性选择的磁反射，结合极化中子衍射技术，重构MnF$_2$的三维磁化密度，并分离Mn²⁺主导球形贡献、各向异性Mn磁化及氟配体的共价自旋极化。

Result: 成功观测到氟离子的有限磁矩以及与对称性允许的五阶磁多极子$O_{52}$一致的各向异性Mn磁化，提供了反铁磁体中多极有序的直接实验证据。

Conclusion: 极化中子衍射是探测量子材料中隐藏磁多极子的有效手段，本研究为反铁磁体中多极序的存在提供了直接实空间证据。

Abstract: Altermagnets are unconventional spin split magnets arising from the zero spin-orbit coupled limit. They host a magnetic multipolar order parameter yet direct real-space observation of these multipoles has remained elusive. Here we use polarized-neutron diffraction to reconstruct the three-dimensional magnetization density of the prototypical altermagnet MnF$_2$. By exploiting symmetry-selective magnetic reflections, we separate the dominant spherical Mn$^{2+}$ contribution from the much weaker anisotropic Mn magnetization and the covalent spin polarization of the fluorine ligands. The reconstructed spin density reveals a finite fluorine ion moment together with an anisotropic Mn magnetization consistent with the symmetry-allowed altermagnetic rank-5 magnetic multipole $O_{52}$(magnetic triacontadipole). These results provide direct real-space evidence of ferroic multipolar order in an altermagnet and establish polarized-neutron diffraction as a powerful probe of hidden magnetic multipoles in quantum materials.

</details>


### [210] [Electronic phase separation and emergence of a nondimerized insulating phase in VO$_2$ $(110)_{\mathit{R}}$ ultrathin films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29116)
*S. Inoue,D. Shiga,R. Hayasaka,K. Ozawa,A. F. Santander-Syro,H. Kumigashira*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 研究了VO2/TiO2(110)超薄膜中电子结构和V-V二聚化的厚度依赖性，发现即使在无几何约束的情况下，低维增强的Mott不稳定性仍是非二聚化绝缘相出现的关键驱动力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索VO2超薄薄膜中维度降低对电子相变和V-V二聚化的影响机制，特别是在不同晶面取向下是否存在普遍性的物理规律。

Method: 采用原位光电子能谱和X射线吸收谱技术，系统研究VO2/TiO2(110)超薄膜在不同厚度下的电子结构与V-V二聚化行为。

Result: 在1.5 nm以上厚度观察到温度驱动的金属-绝缘体转变；在1 nm时出现无V-V二聚化的绝缘相，且该相随厚度减小呈指数增加并在2.2 nm附近存在有效临界厚度。与(001)取向不同，(110)取向中两种相共存并存在应变介导的相竞争。

Conclusion: Mott不稳定性在低维下增强是VO2超薄膜中非二聚化绝缘相形成的通用机制，而V-V链的几何取向决定了电子相分离的空间演化行为。

Abstract: Using in situ photoemission spectroscopy and x-ray absorption spectroscopy, we investigated the thickness dependence of the electronic structure and V-V dimerization in VO$_2$/TiO$_2$ (110) ultrathin films, in which the one-dimensional V-V chains along the $c_{\mathit{R}}$ axis lie in the film plane. In VO$_2$ $(110)_{\mathit{R}}$ films, the reduction in dimensionality along the surface-normal direction is not expected to impose a geometric constraint on V-V dimerization, unlike in VO$_2$ $(001)_{\mathit{R}}$ films. Nevertheless, the characteristic spectral changes associated with the temperature-driven metal-insulator transition observed in thick films persist down to 1.5 nm, whereas at 1 nm an insulating electronic phase is observed without V-V dimerization. This behavior is highly similar to that reported for VO$_2$ $(001)_{\mathit{R}}$, suggesting that the enhancement of Mott instability resulting from reduced dimensionality is a common and essential driving force for the emergence of the nondimerized insulating phase in VO$_2$ ultrathin films. Meanwhile, unlike in VO$_2$ $(001)_{\mathit{R}}$, the nondimerized insulating phase in VO$_2$ $(110)_{\mathit{R}}$ coexists with the phase exhibiting the temperature-driven metal-insulator transition. Its fraction increases exponentially with decreasing thickness and becomes dominant at 1 nm. The corresponding effective critical thickness is estimated to be 2.2 nm. These results imply that the geometric orientation of the V-V chains dictates the spatial evolution of electronic phase separation via strain-mediated phase competition.

</details>


### [211] [Evolving charge order in the CDW state of AV$_3$Sb$_5$ metals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29142)
*Anshu Kataria,Francis Pratt,Peter J. Baker,Stephen Cottrell,Miki Bonacci,Andrea Capa Salinas,Stephen D. Wilson,Zurab Guguchia,Samuele Sanna,Pietro Bonfà*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本研究利用避免能级交叉的μ子自旋谱技术，研究了AV3Sb5（A = Cs, K）中的电子态，发现CDW相内存在额外的电荷调制，且该调制温度低于或接近CDW转变温度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深入了解在超导相之前AV3Sb5类材料中的电子态特征，尤其是电荷密度波相内的复杂电子序。

Method: 采用避免能级交叉（ALC）μ子自旋谱技术，对CsV3Sb5和KV3Sb5系统进行实验，并与先前RbV3Sb5的研究结果进行对比分析。

Result: 在Cs和Rb体系中观测到T* < T_CDW时存在额外的电荷调制，在K体系中该调制发生在T_CDW附近；该现象不能仅由内禀磁场解释，表明有其他电子机制参与改变V平面内的电荷分布。

Conclusion: AV3Sb5体系中电荷密度波相内存在非磁性的额外电子序，可能对理解其超导前驱电子态具有重要意义。

Abstract: AV$_3$Sb$_5$ kagome metals are characterized by intertwined electronic and structural orders, which motivated extensive studies in recent years. Yet the details of the electronic state preceding the superconducting phase remain poorly understood. Here we extend our previous investigation [Phys. Rev. Research 7, L032046 (2025)] of RbV$_3$Sb$_5$ using avoided level crossing (ALC) muon-spin spectroscopy to the $A$ = Cs and K systems. Consistent with our previous study, we identify a second transition whose origin cannot be attributed solely to an internal magnetic field, indicating the involvement of an additional electronic mechanism that subtly modifies the charge distribution within the V plane. In particular, the ALC results point towards an additional charge modulation taking place within the charge density wave (CDW) phase and occurring at $T^{*}<T_{CDW}$ for $A$ = Cs and Rb, or in the vicinity of $T_{CDW}$ for $A$ = K.

</details>


### [212] [Crystal electric field excitations and an effective-spin-$1/2$ ground doublet in the hyperkagome magnet Yb$_3$Sc$_2$Ga$_3$O$_{12}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29276)
*Tingjun Zhang,Zehao Wang,Douglas L. Abernathy,Rong-Zhu Lin,Steven J. Gomez Alvarado,Chien-Lung Huang,Pengcheng Dai*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过非弹性中子散射研究Yb3+在超kagome磁体Yb3Sc2Ga3O12中的晶体电场激发，确定了其能级结构和基态双重态特性，为构建有效交换哈密顿量提供了微观基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 明确Yb3+在Yb3Sc2Ga3O12中的晶体电场能级结构及其对低能磁性的贡献，修正先前基于宏观测量推断的能级方案。

Method: 采用非弹性中子散射（INS）探测磁激发，并结合Stevens算符分析，在D2局域对称性约束下拟合激发能量和谱权重，获得g张量和波函数。

Result: 观测到三个约58、68和74 meV的无色散磁激发，对应J=7/2多重态到三个激发双重态的跃迁；基态为Ising型Kramers双重态，第一激发态约58 meV，形成低溫下有效的Jeff=1/2自由度；两种独立拟合方法得出一致的激发能、波函数和g张量；模型成功复现磁化率、磁化行为及比热中的Schottky反常。

Conclusion: 实验精确确定了Yb3Sc2Ga3O12的单离子晶体电场能谱，验证了低能伪自旋描述的有效性，为理解其集体磁激发和构建有效哈密顿量提供了关键微观依据。

Abstract: We use inelastic neutron scattering (INS) to determine the crystal electric field (CEF) excitations of Yb$^{3+}$ in the rare-earth hyperkagome magnet Yb$_3$Sc$_2$Ga$_3$O$_{12}$. Three nearly dispersionless magnetic excitations are observed near 58, 68, and 74~meV, corresponding to transitions from the ground-state Kramers doublet to the three excited doublets of the $J=7/2$ multiplet. A Stevens-operator analysis constrained by the local $222$ ($D_2$) symmetry reproduces the excitation energies and spectral weights and yields an Ising-type ground-state $g$ tensor. The first excited doublet lies approximately 58~meV above the ground state, establishing a well-isolated effective $J_{\mathrm{eff}}=1/2$ degree of freedom over the low-temperature regime relevant to collective magnetism. Notably, the directly measured CEF spectrum substantially revises the level scheme previously inferred from bulk measurements, while preserving the essential low-energy pseudospin description. Two independent fitting protocols give consistent excitation energies, ground-doublet wave functions, and $g$ tensors, despite the nonuniqueness of the individual CEF parameters. The resulting single-ion model also reproduces the characteristic susceptibility, magnetization, and field evolution of the Schottky anomaly in specific heat. These results establish the microscopic single-ion basis needed to construct an effective exchange Hamiltonian and to interpret future measurements of low-energy collective excitations in Yb$_3$Sc$_2$Ga$_3$O$_{12}$.

</details>


### [213] [Experimental observation of decoupled spin subsystems in decorated square kagomé lattice magnets of the nabokoite family](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29323)
*V. N. Glazkov,Ya V. Rebrov,M. A. Dubovitskii,M. M. Markina,A. F. Murtazoev,P. S. Berdonosov,A. N. Vasiliev*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在Nabokoite家族化合物中，通过电子顺磁共振揭示了二维平方笼目晶格扩展到三维网络时，自旋系统分裂为几乎解耦的两个子系统，其中一个子系统有序而另一个可能表现出自旋液体行为，挑战了传统统一交换网络模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究几何阻挫在三维扩展系统中的表现，探索装饰性阻挫晶格中是否存在解耦的自旋子系统。

Method: 使用电子顺磁共振（EPR）结合绝对吸收定标和静态磁化率测量，分析Nabokoite家族化合物中的自旋响应。

Result: 发现只有部分铜自旋是EPR活性的，并在奈尔点有序；EPR非活性子系统对总磁化率的贡献随温度降低而减小。

Conclusion: 同一化合物中可共存磁序与可能的自旋液体动力学，表明传统统一交换网络模型需要修正。

Abstract: The square kagomé lattice (SKL) offers a model platform for investigating geometric frustration in 2D systems. Nabokoite-family compounds \nabok{A}{X} (A=Na, K, Cs, Rb and X=Cl, Br) extend this physics to a 3D network, where 2D SKL layers are decorated by interlayer spins. Using electron paramagnetic resonance (EPR), we demonstrate a dramatic splitting of this complex exchange network into two virtually decoupled spin subsystems: absolute calibration of the electron paramagnetic resonance (EPR) absorption reveals that only a fraction of all copper spins in nabokoites is EPR-active and this fraction of the spins orders at the Néel point. Comparison of the EPR absorption and static susceptibility indicates that contribution of the EPR-silent spin subsystem to total magnetic susceptibility decreases on cooling. This direct observation of coexisting magnetic order and possible spin-liquid dynamics within a single compound challenges conventional models of unified exchange networks in decorated frustrated lattices.

</details>


### [214] [Collinear and noncollinear antiferromagnetic ordering in a highly frustrated decorated square kagomé lattice antiferromagnets of the nabokoite family](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29330)
*V. N. Glazkov,Ya V. Rebrov,M. A. Dubovitskii,M. M. Markina,K. V. Zakharov,A. F. Murtazoev,P. S. Berdonosov,A. N. Vasiliev*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: Nabokoite家族化合物展现出因碱金属离子轻重不同而导致的磁有序差异，重碱金属离子化合物呈现常规反铁磁序，而轻碱金属离子化合物通过铁电预排序和非共线磁序两步相变实现复杂有序态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究nabokoite家族化合物中由于结构和离子选择导致的不同磁有序行为，揭示其背后可能的量子机制。

Method: 采用多频电子自旋共振谱、比热、磁化率和介电常数测量等热力学手段，并结合零场能隙和临界场分析，以及对结构单元的有限簇模型进行模拟。

Result: 发现重碱金属nabokoites呈现常规共线反铁磁序且参数相近；轻碱金属成员则经历铁电转变解除阻挫，并在5-6K和3-4K发生两步相变形成非共线磁序，表现出多个零场能隙和自旋重取向场；模型表明交换耦合接近量子基态边界可能是导致两类行为差异的关键。

Conclusion: 轻重碱金属离子的选择决定了nabokoites的磁基态类型，结构单元中交换相互作用接近量子临界点是导致此类分叉行为的根本原因。

Abstract: Nabokoite family compounds ACu$_7$(TeO$_4$)(SO4)$_5$X (A=Na, K, Rb, Cs; X=Cl, Br) host frustrated 2D square kagom'{e} lattice layers decorated by additional inter-layer magnetic ions. We study magnetic order in nabokoites with multi-frequency electron spin resonance spectroscopy and thermodynamic measurement (specific heat, magnetization and dielectric permittivity). Our study reveals that the choice of the low-temperature ground state is qualitatively different in light-alkali-ion (K, Na) and heavy-alkali-ion (Rb, Cs) compounds. Heavy-alkali-ion nabokoites order in conventional collinear antiferromagnetic pattern with easy-axis anisotropy. The parameters of the ordered antiferromagnetic state are very close for all heavy-alkali-ion subfamily. Light-alkali-ion members of nabokoite family demonstrate much more complicated route to the ordered state: firstly, a ferroelectric transition at 25-90K lifts the frustration and thus pre-cooks the low-temperature ordering; secondly, an unusual noncollinear magnetic order develops via two-step phase transition with first transition temperature $T_{c1}\simeq 5-6$K and the second transition at $T_{c2}\simeq 3-4$K. Noncollinear order is evidenced by observation of characteristic non-Larmor antiferromagnetic resonance mode. Spin dynamics of light-alkali-ion nabokoites is characterized by three zero-field magnon gaps and two spin-reorientation fields, the values of magnon gaps and critical fields are quite different for different compounds. The finite-size cluster modeling of pyramidal structural block of nabokoite structure combined suggests that the critical closeness of the nabokoite exchange coupling parameters to the border-line between the different quantum ground state of pyramidal building block of nabokoite structure could be the clue to the choice of qualitatively different ordered state in light- and heavy-alkali-ion nabokoites.

</details>


### [215] [Magnetic Field-Tunable Repulsive Exciton-Exciton Interaction in the van der Waals Antiferromagnet NiPS$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29701)
*Kaiyang Huang,Jaena Park,Zhuo Yang,Je-Geun Park,Atsuhiko Miyata,Yoshimitsu Kohama,Yasuhiro H. Matsuda*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在二维范德瓦尔斯晶体NiPS$_3$中，通过高达178 T的脉冲磁场下的磁光测量，观察到两个尖锐激子峰之间的显著相互排斥和振子强度重新分布，揭示了磁场可调的激子-激子耦合是其主要高场响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究超高磁场是否会影响NiPS$_3$中由自旋轨道纠缠态引起的两个超窄吸收峰之间的相互作用驱动的杂化行为。

Method: 在高达178 T的脉冲磁场下对NiPS$_3$进行系统的磁光测量，并构建一个最小的两能级相互作用模型与实验数据进行半定量比较。

Result: 观察到两个激子峰之间明显的相互排斥和振子强度的重新分配，而带边在实验分辨率内无明显磁场诱导位移。

Conclusion: 激子-激子耦合是NiPS$_3$在高磁场下的主导响应机制，表明该材料是研究磁性范德瓦尔斯绝缘体中强关联激子物理的新平台。

Abstract: Two ultra-narrow absorption peaks around 1.5 eV, which are widely believed to originate from a transition from a spin-orbital entangled triplet to a singlet state, in the two-dimensional van der Waals crystal NiPS$_3$, have attracted considerable attention because of their pronounced spin-dependent character. An interesting question is whether ultrahigh magnetic fields modify an interaction-driven hybridization between those two peaks. In this work we perform systematic magneto-optical measurements of NiPS$_3$ in pulsed magnetic fields of up to 178 T and observe a pronounced mutual repulsion between the two sharp exciton peaks accompanied by a redistribution of oscillator strength, while the band edge shows no detectable field-induced shift within our experimental resolution. We construct a minimal two-level interaction model and compare it semi-quantitatively with the experimental data. Our results reveal a magnetic-field-tunable exciton-exciton coupling as the dominant high-field response of NiPS$_3$, and clarify this material as a new experimental platform for exploring strongly correlated exciton physics in magnetic van der Waals insulators.

</details>


### [216] [Emergent Pair Density Wave and Incoherent Metallic State in a Strongly Correlated Doped System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29742)
*Soham Maiti,Nandan Pakhira,A. Taraphder*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 研究在反铁磁性与d波超导共存区域中，通过slave-rotor平均场方法揭示PDW序的动态产生及其对强关联效应的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索强关联体系中反铁磁性与超导性相互作用导致的新奇量子态。

Method: 采用slave-rotor平均场框架，将电子分解为电荷和自旋自由度，以捕捉超越传统平均场的强关联效应。

Result: 发现PDW序在反铁磁与超导共存区动态产生；共存区受关联效应显著调制，四临界点发生系统性偏移；随着掺杂和温度变化，出现从相干到非相干金属的转变，并观察到准粒子谱权重的抑制。

Conclusion: 该模型成功揭示了反铁磁与d波超导竞争下涌现的复杂相变行为及电荷相干性的演化，表明在不同区域内可同时存在相干准粒子带与非相干激发。

Abstract: We investigate the dynamical emergence of a state driven by the interplay between antiferromagnetism (AFM) and singlet $d$-wave superconductivity (SC) within a slave-rotor mean-field framework. By decomposing the electron into charge and spin degrees of freedom, the formalism captures strong-correlation effects beyond conventional mean-field approaches. A PDW order is found to emerge dynamically in the coexistence region of AFM and SC. The AFM-SC coexistence region is significantly modified with correlation, leading to a systematic shift of the tetra-critical point. The doping and temperature evolution of the AFM, SC, and PDW order parameters, together with the rotor condensate amplitude $φ$, which characterizes charge coherence, shows a crossover from a coherent to an incoherent metal with the suppression of coherent quasi-particle spectral weight. Moreover, in the AFM + $(φ\neq 0)$ region, coherent quasi-particle bands coexist with incoherent Hubbard-like excitations, whereas only incoherent spectral features survive in the AFM + $(φ= 0)$ regime. The SC phase exhibits nodal quasi-particles consistent with $d$-wave pairing.

</details>


### [217] [Thermal Hall Signatures of Distinct Schwinger-Boson Flux Sectors on the Honeycomb Lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29792)
*Daiki Sasamoto*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了在具有次近邻Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用和垂直磁场的阻挫J₁-J₂海森堡模型中，不同规范场（0-通量、π-通量和手性π/2-通量）下的自旋子热霍尔电导率。通过有限温度Schwinger-玻色子平均场理论和Kubo公式，发现这三种通量结构在κₓᵧ/T的符号和温度依赖性上表现出显著差异，表明热输运可作为区分不同部分子平均场结构的敏感探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定热输运是否能够区分同一自旋系统中不同的部分子平均场结构，尤其是在蜂窝晶格上提出的多种通量相（如0-通量、π-通量和手性π/2-通量）之间的区别。

Method: 采用有限温度下的Schwinger-玻色子平均场理论结合Bogoliubov-de Gennes系统的Kubo公式，计算并比较三种通量扇区中的本征自旋子热霍尔电导率，并分析对称性对零场响应的影响。

Result: 在共同参数点下，0-通量响应为正，π-通量响应为负（当D>0且h>0时），而π/2-通量响应随温度升高发生符号变化；此外，六重旋转与时间反演联合的投影对称性导致固定手性π/2-通量域的零场响应消失。

Conclusion: 在所研究的平均场范围内，κₓᵧ/T的符号和温度依赖性可作为识别不同规范通量结构的输运特征，从而为实验上区分不同部分子态提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Thermal transport offers a bulk probe of charge-neutral excitations in frustrated magnets. It remains challenging, however, to determine whether such transport can distinguish different parton mean-field structures of the same spin system. This question is particularly relevant on the honeycomb lattice, where distinct $0$-flux, $π$-flux, and chiral $π/2$-flux sectors have been proposed. In this paper, we compare self-consistent saddle-point solutions in these three sectors for a frustrated $J_1-J_2$ Heisenberg model with a next-nearest-neighbor Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction and a perpendicular magnetic field. Using finite-temperature Schwinger-boson mean-field theory and the Kubo formula for bosonic Bogoliubov-de Gennes systems, we evaluate the intrinsic spinon thermal Hall conductivity. At a common parameter point where all three branches remain gapped, the $0$-flux response is positive and the $π$-flux response is negative for $D>0$ and $h>0$ under the sign conventions used in this paper, whereas the $π/2$-flux response changes sign with increasing temperature. We further show that a projective symmetry combining a sixfold rotation with time reversal forces the zero-field response of a fixed chiral $π/2$-flux domain to vanish. Within the mean-field regime examined in this paper, the sign and temperature dependence of $κ_{xy}/T$ therefore provide a flux-sensitive transport signature.

</details>


### [218] [Symmetry-Enforced Topological Structures in Quantum Phase Diagrams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29915)
*Linhao Li,Yuan Yao*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了能隙系统量子相图的拓扑结构，提出基于S^1-族分类的新方法，并揭示了对称性在其中的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索具有非平凡基态简并的能隙系统中量子相图的拓扑结构，弥补传统基于独立对称性分类的不足。

Method: 通过识别gapped相图中的非收缩环（S^1-族），结合混合反常、对称性破缺与未破缺对称性的半直积关系以及非可逆算符对称性进行分类。

Result: 发现了由对称性相互作用导致的新型S^1-族分类，且构造了其晶格实现并展示了其代数结构。

Conclusion: 对称性的复杂交织能够产生超越传统分类框架的新拓扑结构，为理解量子相提供了新视角。

Abstract: We study the topological structure of the quantum phase diagram of gapped systems by identifying the noncontractibility of loops, so-called $S^1$-families, within the gapped phase diagram in which many-body Hamiltonians can have nontrivial ground-state degeneracy. We manifest the role of symmetries in such $S^1$-family classifications by the exotic symmetry interplay: (i) nontrivial mixed anomalies, (ii) semi-direct product relation between the spontaneously broken and the unbroken symmetries, and (iii) symmetry with noninvertible operators. We find that such structures lead to the novel $S^1$-family classifications inaccessible by earlier classifications based on ``independent'' symmetries. Furthermore, we construct lattice realizations of these $S^1$-families and explicitly demonstrate their novel algebraic structures.

</details>


### [219] [Holographic Representations of Topological Quantum Criticality: Emergent Symmetry Approach around the Bott Clock](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30335)
*Fan Yang,Fei Zhou*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过引入涌现对称性构建了布特时钟中拓扑量子临界点的全息表示，并展示了如何利用这种对称性构造高维格点模型，其边界行为与低维临界点一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为理解布特时钟中不同拓扑相之间的量子临界行为，特别是拓扑量子临界点（tQCPs）的对称性结构，提供一个系统的全息描述框架。

Method: 运用涌现对称性U_{EM}的概念，在d维空间中构建具有升级对称性的(d+1)维格点模型，并通过加倍相邻拓扑类的自由度来实现对称群的扩展。

Result: (d+1)维格点模型展现出预期的扩大对称群，且其d维边界在红外极限下表现出与对应tQCP相同的动力学行为。

Conclusion: 该方法成功建立了tQCP与高维拓扑模型之间的全息联系，揭示了布特时钟中拓扑相变的深层对称性机制。

Abstract: In this article, we apply the idea of emergent symmetries to construct holographic representations of a broad class of topological quantum critical points (tQCPs) that appear in fermionic classes in the Bott clock. We show explicitly that around the Bott clock, an emergent symmetry $U_{EM}$ exists at a tQCP between different symmetry protected gapped phases with protecting symmetry $G_p$ in $d$ spatial dimensions. This emergent symmetry can be used to construct a $(d+1)$ spatial dimensional lattice model with a properly upgraded symmetry such as $U_{EM}\times G_p$ or $U_{EM} \rtimes G_p$, etc. By doubling the degrees of freedom of the adjacent topological class in $d$-dimensions, we successfully show that the $(d+1)$-dimensional lattice models constructed in this way have the desired enlarged symmetry groups that precisely belong to the adjacent topological classes, counterclockwise around the Bott clock. Furthermore, $d$-dimensional boundaries of these $(d+1)$ dimensional lattice models of gapped topological classes are shown to exhibit identical infrared dynamics as the corresponding tQCPs in the $d$-dimensional adjacent topological phases in the Bott clock, with lower symmetries.

</details>


### [220] [Pseudospin Dynamics of Charge Order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30381)
*Ping Tang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出了一个基于扩展Hubbard模型的有效赝自旋模型，用于描述电荷有序态的动力学行为，并揭示了外加电场可实现对电荷序极性的相干反转控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缺乏描述电荷序动力学及其在外场驱动下演化的微观理论框架，与自旋体系相比发展滞后。

Method: 从扩展Hubbard模型出发，推导出有效赝自旋模型，将电荷有序态映射为交错的赝自旋序，并通过类似Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert方程描述其动力学。

Result: 发现外加电场可直接调控交错赝自旋序，在阈值以上能通过失稳集体赝自旋激发实现电荷序极性的相干反转。

Conclusion: 建立了电荷赝自旋作为电荷序相干切换与控制的微观动力学自由度，为电荷序调控提供了类比自旋电子学的理论框架。

Abstract: Charge order is conventionally viewed as a static modulation of the electronic density, despite growing experimental capabilities to probe and manipulate its nonequilibrium evolution. In contrast to spin-ordered systems, a microscopic framework for charge-order dynamics and its control under external driving remains largely underdeveloped. Here, starting from an extended Hubbard model, we derive an effective pseudospin model in which the charge-ordered state maps onto staggered pseudospin order. The resulting charge-order dynamics is governed by Landau--Lifshitz--Gilbert-like equations for the pseudospins, closely analogous to those of a bipartite antiferromagnet. We show that an external electric field directly controls the staggered pseudospin order and, above a threshold field, drives coherent reversal of the charge-order polarity by destabilizing collective pseudospin excitations. Our results establish the charge pseudospin as a microscopic dynamical degree of freedom for coherent switching and control of charge order, providing a charge-sector analogue of the well-established framework for spin-order dynamics in spintronics.

</details>


### [221] [Chiral Color Ice: Exact Local Handedness Constraints and Möbius Zero Modes in Frustrated Magnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30802)
*Péter Kránitz,Yasir Iqbal,Karlo Penc*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的局域手性约束方法，通过SU(2)不变的正半定哈密顿量在量子系统中实现精确的手性选择，揭示了手性、非线性几何约束与量子简并之间的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统冰型规则无法敏感地响应手性，作者希望构建一种不依赖自旋空间特定轴的手性局域量子约束，以更好地理解受挫物质中的低能物理。

Method: 构造基于四面体结构的正半定、SU(2)不变的母哈密顿量，其零能空间具有指定的手性符号；对任意自旋，该哈密顿量可分解为通过单态湮灭算符的B†B形式，并分析其核空间结构。

Result: 自旋1/2情况下，局域项是投影到手性四面体单态的秩一投影子；相干态下，零能条件转化为由莫比乌斯变换决定的非线性约束；不同晶格连接方式导致不同的集体行为，如角共享晶格保留指数级大的零能空间，边共享晶格支持亚维零模等。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了可处理的理论框架，将局域手性、非线性约束几何与量子简并直接联系起来，并表明约束网络的连通性对量子基态性质有决定性影响。

Abstract: Local constraints govern the low-energy physics of frustrated matter, but familiar ice-type rules constrain flux-like quantities and are insensitive to handedness. Here we show that handedness itself can be imposed as an exact local quantum constraint without selecting an axis in spin space. We construct positive-semidefinite, SU(2)-invariant parent Hamiltonians whose complete zero-energy space on a tetrahedron has a prescribed chirality sign, rather than selecting a particular chiral wave function. For spin-1/2 the local term is a rank-one projector onto a chiral tetrahedral singlet, while for arbitrary spin it factorizes as $B^\dagger B$ through a singlet-annihilation operator, with a completely characterized kernel given by the span of the globally rotated chiral color-ice states. For coherent states, the same zero-energy condition becomes an $S$-independent nonlinear constraint in which three spin directions determine the fourth through a Möbius transformation; compositions of these maps define constraint holonomies on extended lattices. Connecting the same local constraint in different ways produces qualitatively different collective regimes: corner-sharing lattices retain exponentially large quantum ground-state kernels, with rigorous lower bounds exceeding conventional ice benchmarks; edge-sharing lattices support subdimensional plane or line zero modes; while triangular constructions suppress nonuniform coherent deformations and contain the complete Anderson tower of tetrahedral magnetic order at exactly zero energy. Two inequivalent triangular coverings further show that harmonic zero-mode counting does not determine the size of the quantum kernel. These results establish a tractable setting in which local handedness, nonlinear constraint geometry, and quantum degeneracy can be disentangled and related directly to the connectivity of the constraint network.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [222] [Max-Stable Survival Copulas under Dependent Censoring: Sharp Identification and Efficient Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28867)
*Djibril Gueye,Salima Helali,Modou Wade*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了在基于补偿器减少的广义Cox首次击中时间框架下的依赖删失问题，提出了最大稳定性和共同操作时钟的关系，并建立了完全和二元观测方案下的识别结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决依赖删失情况下的模型识别与估计问题，特别是在生存分析中常见的复杂依赖结构。

Method: 利用补偿器减少方法，在广义Cox首次击中时间模型中分析依赖删失；探讨最大稳定性与共同操作时钟之间的等价性，并发展非参数估计与推断方法。

Result: 在完全观测下获得 sharp 识别结果；在二元观测下，共同时钟和事件载荷可点识别，而删失载荷及潜变量结构仅部分可识别；提出有效受限估计量和可观测的最大稳定兼容性检验。

Conclusion: 共同操作时钟是依赖删失下模型可识别的关键，所提方法为实际数据中的依赖结构建模提供了实用工具。

Abstract: We study dependent censoring within a generalized Cox first-hitting-time. framework based on compensator reductions. In the genuinely dependent case, we show that max-stability of the associated survival copula is equivalent to a common operational clock, leading to a nonparametric Marshall Olkin family. We establish sharp identification results under complete and binary observation schemes. Under binary observations, the common clock and event loading are point identified, whereas the censoring loading and the associated latent survival and dependence structures are only partially identified. We develop nonparametric estimation and inference for the identifiable components, derive an efficient restricted estimator, and propose an observable test of max-stable compatibility. Simulations and a real-data application illustrate the practical implications of the identification and efficiency results.

</details>


### [223] [Pollak's Minimax Quickest Change Detection: Non-Asymptotic Optimality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29491)
*Ali Tajer*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种生存过程表示方法，将Pollak的极小极大准则优化问题转化为等效的线性变分优化问题，从而解决了有限平均游程长条件下 quickest change detection 的一般性优化问题，并自然导出了Shiryaev-Roberts型检测规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Pollak极小极大框架下的最优性结果多为渐近性质，缺乏对有限平均游程长（finite γ）情形的精确分析，本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 引入生存过程表示法，将停止时间上的优化转化为对允许生存过程的线性变分优化，结合递归加权似然比统计量进行求解。

Result: 证明了有限γ下Pollak极小极大值的存在性和精确刻画，导出可计算且性能逼近最优的停时规则，其结构自然呈现为带有时变注入和边界的Shiryaev-Roberts型递推形式，并在特定条件下得到闭式精确解。

Conclusion: 该框架为有限样本下 quickest change detection 提供了严格的优化基础，Shiryaev-Roberts类方法被证明是全历史依赖随机停时类中的自然最优解。

Abstract: Although Pollak's minimax formulation is one of the central frameworks in quickest change detection (QCD), the strongest general optimality results available for it are predominantly \emph{asymptotic}, applying as the average run-length-to-false-alarm constraint, $γ$, tends to infinity. Exact results for finite $γ$ have previously been available only for special models or restricted regimes. This paper addresses the general finite-$γ$ problem over the complete class of randomized, history-dependent stopping rules for i.i.d. change models. The key technical development is a \emph{survival-process representation} that recasts the optimization of Pollak's minimax criterion over stopping times as an equivalent linear variational optimization over admissible survival processes. Although this formulation is infinite-dimensional, it establishes the existence of an optimizer and provides an exact characterization of the finite-$γ$ Pollak minimax value. This characterization, in turn, provides a principled basis for constructing computable stopping rules whose performance can be made arbitrarily close to the optimum. The resulting rules are driven by a recursively updated weighted likelihood-ratio statistic with generally time-varying injections and boundaries. Importantly, this structure is not imposed a priori: the optimization is carried out over the full class of randomized, history-dependent stopping rules, and the Shiryaev--Roberts form emerges naturally from the solution. In particular, the classical Shiryaev--Roberts recursion arises as the time-homogeneous special case. Finally, under a likelihood-ratio floor condition, the framework yields closed-form exact minimax solutions for a nontrivial class of change models.

</details>


### [224] [Sturm-Liouville-Type Parity and Oscillation of a Cubic Spline Eigenbasis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29781)
*Shih-Hao Huang,Jephian C. -H. Lin,ShengLi Tzeng,Tzu-Lun Yuan*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 研究了等距节点三次平滑样条罚矩阵的特征结构，通过纯矩阵理论论证了其正特征值的单调性、特征向量的奇偶交替性及其符号变化规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示等距节点下罚矩阵的特征结构特性，为平滑样条提供更直观的理论基础。

Method: 采用纯矩阵理论方法分析罚矩阵的特征值和特征向量性质。

Result: 证明了正特征值是单重的，对应特征向量在奇偶性上交替，第k大特征值对应的特征向量恰好有k+1个符号变化。

Conclusion: 等距节点支持具有奇偶结构和振荡模式的样条基。

Abstract: We study the eigen-structure of the penalty matrix arising from cubic smoothing splines on equally spaced knots. Using purely matrix-theoretic arguments, we show that its positive eigenvalues are simple, that the associated eigenvectors alternate between even and odd, and that the eigenvector for the $k$th largest eigenvalue has exactly $k+1$ sign changes. The approach provides a direct and transparent alternative to existing variational proofs of the oscillation property. These results show that equally spaced knots support a spline basis with both a parity structure and an oscillation pattern.

</details>


### [225] [Self-normalization for Spectral Density Integrals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30018)
*Holger Dette,Sebastian Kühnert*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 研究了基于序列周期图的谱密度积分估计量的自标准化方法，建立了相应过程的弱收敛性。对于线性泛函，自标准化产生不依赖未知谱量的极限分布；而对于非线性泛函，极限过程中可能出现具有不同协方差结构的额外成分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了更好地总结线性过程的谱特征，需要对谱密度的积分进行估计，并研究其统计推断性质，特别是在不同函数形式下的极限行为差异。

Method: 采用基于序列周期图的自标准化方法，分析估计量的过程并建立弱收敛理论，比较线性与非线性泛函在极限分布中的表现。

Result: 证明了自标准化下线性泛函具有与未知谱量无关的极限分布（即具有枢轴性质），但非线性泛函（如平方谱密度积分）的极限过程会引入额外的、具有不同协方差结构的成分。

Conclusion: 自标准化在处理线性谱泛函时具有良好统计性质，但在非线性情况下需谨慎使用，因其极限分布更复杂，可能影响推断有效性。

Abstract: Integrals of spectral densities are frequently used to summarize spectral characteristics of linear processes. This work studies self-normalization for estimators of such integrals based on sequential periodograms and establishes weak convergence of the corresponding processes. For linear functionals of the spectral density, self-normalization yields pivotal limiting distributions that are free of unknown spectral quantities. For non-linear functionals, however, additional components with distinct covariance structures may arise in the limiting process. We demonstrate this phenomenon for the integrated squared spectral density.

</details>


### [226] [Distribution-free testing of linear type](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30074)
*Weixuan Xia*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 提出了一种新的分布无关的拟合优度检验方法——omega-1检验，作为Kolmogorov-Smirnov和Cramér-von Mises检验的线性类比，具有良好的局部与整体敏感性平衡，并与Wasserstein 1距离密切相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有拟合优度检验在处理局部化或扩散型替代假设时存在敏感性不平衡问题，且部分统计量缺乏直观解释或计算复杂。需要一种兼具鲁棒性、可解释性和计算便利性的新方法。

Method: 定义了一个基于经验过程的L¹泛函作为检验统计量，在有限样本下推导出其低维计算形式，并结合Khmaladze变换实现复合假设下的渐近分布无关检验，同时给出极限分布的显式表达式。

Result: omega-1检验能更好平衡对局部和扩散型替代假设的敏感性；获得了有限样本和极限情形下的显式分布公式；模拟显示其快速收敛且实用性强；可与Khmaladze变换兼容以处理复合假设。

Conclusion: omega-1检验是一种具有良好理论性质和实际应用价值的新型分布无关拟合优度检验方法，弥补了传统方法的不足，为分布检验提供了更稳健、可解释且易于计算的新工具。

Abstract: We introduce a distribution-free goodness-of-fit test, termed the omega-1 test, which naturally complements the Kolmogorov--Smirnov test and Cramér--von Mises test and can be viewed as their (piecewise) linear analog. Defined as an $\mathrm{L}^{1}$-functional of the empirical process, the test statistic improves on balancing sensitivity to localized and diffuse alternatives and gives a robust and interpretable measure of distributional discrepancy, apart from close connections to the Wasserstein 1-distance. For finite samples, we derive a finite-dimensional computational form for the statistic under general conditions, which leads to various explicit formulas for its null distribution. Under mild continuity assumptions, the limiting statistic is distribution-free, with explicit distribution formulas. In composite settings, the statistic is also compatible with the Khmaladze transformation, enabling asymptotically distribution-free testing. The limiting transformed statistic also has an explicit distribution that escapes reliance on intractable compensator processes or purely numerical evaluation. Simulation results indicate rapid convergence of the finite-sample distributions to their limiting counterparts and support the practical applicability of the test.

</details>


### [227] [Cubic-Root Gaussian Approximation under Unrestricted Covariance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30221)
*Zijun Gao,Weihan Zhang*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了高维矩形下具有无限制协方差的高斯逼近问题，提出了在特定条件下逼近误差的上界，并推翻了先前关于n^{-1/4}速率接近最优的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Chernozhukov等人（2023b）曾猜想在高维情况下，高斯逼近的收敛速率为n^{-1/4}（忽略对数因子）接近最优。本文旨在验证或反驳这一猜想，特别是在多项式维度增长的情形下。

Method: 通过两阶段插值法和无秩矩阵加权高斯表面积界的方法，结合坐标次指数条件与边际方差下界条件，推导出新的逼近误差上界。

Result: 在B_n有界且维度为多项式增长的情况下，得到了n^{-1/3}的逼近误差界，优于此前猜想的n^{-1/4}，从而证伪了该猜想。此外，证明过程可被机器验证（Lean形式化）。

Conclusion: 本文结果表明，在所考虑的条件下，高斯逼近的收敛速度可以达到n^{-1/3}，说明之前的n^{-1/4}猜想并非接近最优，提供了更强的理论保证。

Abstract: For Gaussian approximation over high-dimensional rectangles under unrestricted covariance, Chernozhukov et al. (2023b) conjectured that the $n^{-1/4}$ rate, up to logarithmic factors, is near-optimal. We show that, under the coordinatewise subexponential condition with scale $B_n$ and the marginal variance lower bound condition with constant $b$ in Chernozhukov et al. (2023b), the approximation error in dimension $d$ is bounded by \begin{align*} C_b\min\left\{ 1,\, \left(\frac{B_n^2}{n}\right)^{1/3}\{\log(2dn)\}^{7/3} + \frac{B_n}{\sqrt n}\{\log(2dn)\}^{5/2} \right\}. \end{align*} In particular, for bounded $B_n$ and polynomial dimension, the new bound is $n^{-1/3}$ and therefore falsifies the polynomial-dimensional $n^{-1/4}$ near-optimality conjecture. The proof uses a two-stage interpolation and a rank-free matrix-weighted Gaussian surface bound, which may be of independent interest.
  The initial proof attempt was generated by ChatGPT 5.6 Pro (OpenAI) and subsequently corrected and rewritten by the authors. The machine-checked Lean formalization of the proof can be found at the GitHub repository (https://github.com/WeihanZhang2001/cubic-root-gaussian-approximation-under-unrestricted-covariance).

</details>


### [228] [Bergsma--Dassios Sign Covariance Characterises Independence for Arbitrary Real-Valued Bivariate Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30331)
*Stefan Grünewald,Libo Huang*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文证明了Bergsma--Dassios符号协方差$τ^*$在所有实值二元分布下，其为零可完全刻画变量间的独立性，并通过路径树与有理逼近等方法去除了支持域和正则性限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在建立一个适用于广泛分布（包括混合和奇异分布）的非参数依赖性度量，解决现有秩相关方法在复杂分布下无法准确刻画独立性的问题。

Method: 利用有理概率单元的有限有序分布构造标记路径树，结合四元组协方差的非负平方和表示，通过嵌套量化和有理逼近技术推广到一般分布；同时联系经验距离协方差与边加权四元组量。

Result: 证明了对于任意实值二元分布，$τ^*(X,Y)=0$ 当且仅当 $X$ 与 $Y$ 独立，并得到定量不等式 $τ^* \ge 2\mathscr{B}$；此外，在二元系统发育树间四元组距离上得出渐近上界 $2/3$。

Conclusion: Bergsma--Dassios $τ^*$ 是一个普适的独立性刻画工具，该结果为基于秩的依赖检测提供了坚实的理论基础，且不依赖于样本层面的极限定理。

Abstract: Bergsma--Dassios sign covariance $τ^*$ is a rank-based population measure of dependence. Building on zero-characterisation results under specific regularity regimes, we prove that $τ^*(X,Y)=0$ characterises independence for every real-valued bivariate distribution, including mixed and singular laws. For the unnormalised four-sample convention for $τ^*$ defined in Subsection 4.3 and the unscaled Blum--Kiefer--Rosenblatt functional $\mathscr {B}$, the proof gives the quantitative inequality $τ^*\ge 2\mathscr {B}$. This is a population identification result; no new sample-level limit theorem is claimed. The argument first encodes finite ordered distributions with rational cell probabilities by labelled path trees and applies a nonnegative sum-of-squares representation for a quartet covariance. Rational approximation and nested quantisation then remove all support and regularity restrictions. On finite uniformly weighted label sets, the tree framework also relates an edge-weighted quartet quantity to empirical distance covariance squared. As a separate combinatorial consequence, it yields the asymptotic $2/3$ upper bound for the quartet distance between binary phylogenetic trees.

</details>


### [229] [Unconditional Finiteness of the Number of Critical Points of Gaussian Mixture Densities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30334)
*Akifumi Okuno*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 证明了每个有限异方差高斯混合模型只有有限个临界点和模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于Wang在同方差设定下的近期重要论证，扩展到异方差情况。

Method: 略微调整Wang的论证方法，应用于异方差高斯混合模型。

Result: 证明了每个有限异方差高斯混合模型具有有限个临界点和模式。

Conclusion: 异方差高斯混合模型的模式数量是有限的。

Abstract: By slightly adapting Wang's recent important argument for the homoscedastic setting, we prove that every finite heteroscedastic Gaussian mixture has only finitely many critical points, and, consequently, only finitely many modes.

</details>


### [230] [Compact and Infinite-Order Error Analysis for Null-Space SVD Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30374)
*Xin Li,Jonathan Cohen,Rami Puzis*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了从含噪声矩阵中估计零空间的问题，推导了最小左奇异向量误差的精确表达式，并提出了适用于单维和多维零空间的高阶展开方法。通过复本征点分析确定收敛半径，结合Wishart矩阵和高斯平均理论，揭示了经验风险与泛化风险的排序现象及其有限样本特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确估计含噪声矩阵的零空间在许多科学和工程问题中至关重要，但现有方法缺乏对误差结构和收敛性的深入理解。本文旨在建立严格的理论框架以分析零空间估计的性能极限。

Method: 采用奇异值分解（SVD）分析，推导最小左奇异向量的误差表达式；引入全阶级数展开描述SVD向量和投影算子；利用复异常点计算收敛半径；结合Wishart矩阵理论、高斯平均和蒙特卡洛模拟分析经验与泛化风险的排序行为。

Result: 得到了零空间估计误差的紧凑解析表达式和收敛半径的独立计算方法；证明了Wishart矩阵在τ≥m时对经验风险的严格二阶排序；发现高斯平均下主导泛化风险在高低噪声极限下趋于相等；提出了精确的总体重叠判据和99%置信度蒙特卡洛验证；揭示了‘等秩-等风险’现象及其与谱混合的关系。

Conclusion: 本文建立了噪声环境下零空间估计的统一理论框架，明确了误差、收敛性和风险排序之间的关系。‘等秩-等风险’作为有限样本诊断工具，有助于理解谱混合效应，且其容忍交叉、异常点半径与BBP阈值为三个不同概念。

Abstract: We study null-space estimation from a noisy matrix. For a simple left null space, we first derive an exact compact expression for the error of the smallest left singular vector. We then give an all-order series for the SVD vector and projector, followed by compact and consistently truncated series forms for the fixed-realization empirical risk and conditional population generalization risk. The recursion extends to a multiple-dimensional null space by following the complete invariant subspace. The convergence radius is not inferred from an error plot: it is computed independently from the nearest complex exceptional point that joins a retained eigenvalue branch to its complement. A reduced-nullity experiment shows that moving this spectral boundary can increase the radius, although the improvement is not monotone in the retained nullity. For individually ordered null directions under Gaussian training with \(τ\geq m\), we prove that the Wishart splitting matrix \(W\) gives a strict second-order empirical ranking. Gaussian averaging equalizes the leading generalization risks at both small and very large noise, while a column-swap theorem proves strict expected generalization ranking for an isotropic signal subspace. For unequal spikes, an exact population-overlap criterion and a simultaneous \(99\%\) Monte Carlo confidence certificate explain the observed intermediate ranking. A sixth-order risk correction improves the lower-crossover estimate in the reported experiment. This equal--ranked--equal phenomenon is a finite-sample diagnostic related to spectral mixing, but its tolerance crossings, the exceptional-point radius, and the asymptotic BBP threshold are three distinct quantities.

</details>


### [231] [Bounds on the Posterior-to-Prior Ratios for Inclusion Belief under Bounded Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30473)
*Jan Reiter Sørensen,Heidi Søgaard Christensen,Rasmus Rask Kragh Jørgensen,Martin Bøgsted*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了在有界概率差分隐私和近似差分隐私下，个体数据包含在数据集中的后验信念与先验信念比率的上下界，特别是在最坏情况下的对手拥有除一个参与者外的所有辅助信息时的情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 差分隐私虽然已成为生成隐私保护数据发布的标准，但其对披露风险的解释并不直观。特别是，在观察到受保护的数据发布后，对手关于个体是否包含在数据集中的信念变化程度仍不清楚。因此，需要量化这种变化以更好地理解实际的推断隐私保障。

Method: 通过假设最坏情况下的对手（即知道除一个以外所有参与者的辅助信息），推导出在有界概率差分隐私和近似差分隐私下，包含信念的后验-先验比值的上下界；并对高斯机制分析其失败概率，将其理论上限与蒙特卡洛估计进行比较。

Result: 得到了适用于任何对手的信念比值边界；对于高斯机制，理论上的失败概率上限远高于实际蒙特卡洛模拟观测到的失败率，表明该上限过于保守。

Conclusion: 不同差分隐私机制在实践中提供的推断隐私保障可能比理论上限所暗示的要强得多。

Abstract: Differential privacy has become the standard for generating privacy-protected data releases. However, differential privacy does not translate intuitively to disclosure risk. In particular, it remains unclear how much an adversary's belief about an individual's inclusion in a dataset can change after observing a protected release. To address this question, we derive upper and lower bounds on the posterior-to-prior ratios of inclusion beliefs under bounded probabilistic and approximate differential privacy. By assuming a worst-case adversary with all-but-one auxiliary information, i.e., knowledge of all except for one of the participants in a dataset, we obtain bounds that apply to any adversary. Because these bounds may fail with non-zero probability, we study the corresponding failure probability for the Gaussian mechanism. We derive a theoretical upper limit on this probability and compare it with Monte Carlo estimates across a wide range of parameter settings. The observed failure rate is several orders of magnitude smaller than its theoretical upper limit, indicating that the latter is highly conservative. These findings suggest that the inferential privacy guarantees provided by differentially private mechanisms may be substantially stronger in practice than what is implied by the theoretical upper limit.

</details>


### [232] [Scale Analysis and Shape Selection for the Generalized Gaussian Mechanism under Approximate Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31138)
*Xiang Zhang,Mohamedou Ould Haye,Yiqiang Q. Zhao*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了在(ε,δ)-差分隐私下广义高斯机制（GGM）的尺度参数估计与形状参数选择问题，提出了一种可计算的尺度近似方法和区间搜索算法以优化形状参数，在保持隐私保护的同时显著提升了效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在差分隐私中更好地平衡隐私保护与数据效用，需要对广义高斯噪声的形状参数进行优化，现有工作缺乏对一般p值下的系统分析与高效计算方法。

Method: 通过隐式方程组刻画一维情况下的最小尺度参数b(p)，构造多维查询下的可计算上界近似，并设计具有逼近保证的区间搜索算法来比较不同形状在尺度齐次效用准则下的表现。

Result: 理论结果包括最优形状对敏感性向量缩放的不变性及高隐私极限下的渐进行为；实验表明，优化形状参数可在不同场景下将各坐标方差降低5%至20%以上，并提升任务级效用或降低攻击成功率。

Conclusion: 优化广义高斯机制的形状参数能有效提升机制效用而不牺牲隐私保护，是一种实用且具理论支持的改进方法。

Abstract: Differential privacy provides a rigorous framework for protecting private information, typically achieved by adding random noise to query results. The generalized Gaussian family is a flexible class of additive noise distributions indexed by the shape parameter $p$ and includes the Laplace and Gaussian distributions as special cases $p=1$ and $p=2$, respectively. This paper studies the privacy-feasible scale estimation and the shape parameter selection of the generalized Gaussian mechanism (GGM) under $(\varepsilon,δ)$-differential privacy. For a given sensitivity vector $Δ$ and $p\in[1,\infty]$, let $b(p)$ denote the smallest value of the scale parameter for which the mechanism satisfies this privacy requirement. In the one-dimensional case, $b(p)$ can be implicitly characterized by a system of equations. For vector-valued queries, we construct a computable upper approximation of $b(p)$ that preserves the privacy guarantee. Shapes are compared under a scale-homogeneous utility criterion, with the $m$-th absolute moment as the main example. We develop an interval-wise shape search algorithm with an approximation guarantee that can be made arbitrarily precise. We also establish the invariance of the optimal shape under rescaling of the sensitivity vector and characterize its limiting behaviour under high privacy limits. Computational experiments show that optimizing shape parameters can improve utility by reducing the variance of each coordinate by 5% to 20% across a variety of cases, with some cases showing even greater reductions, while maintaining the same level of privacy protection. Task-specific experiments further show that shape optimization can improve task-level utility, reduce attacker success, or achieve both.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.stat-mech'></div>

# cond-mat.stat-mech [[Back]](#toc)

### [233] [Light-induced nonconservative static forces in many-body systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29122)
*Jinze Li,Xuemei Yang,Zhiyuan Sun*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了光在固体系统中对电子序参量产生的静态力，发现这些力可以具有源自耗散（即光吸收）的非保守成分。这种效应在激子绝缘体和具有非公度电荷密度波的材料中得到了验证，分别导致激子凝聚相位的加速和电荷密度波的滑动运动，产生直流电流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索周期性驱动如何在慢速集体自由度上生成有效的静态力，并理解光与物质相互作用中的非保守力机制。

Method: 理论分析结合两个非平凡例子：激子绝缘体中的带间激发和具有非公度电荷密度波材料中的传播光场。

Result: 发现在激子绝缘体中，光场通过带间激发在激子序参量相位上产生非保守力，导致光发射峰的偏移；在非公度电荷密度波材料中，传播光场产生非保守力，驱动电荷密度波滑动并产生直流电流。

Conclusion: 光可以在量子多体系统中产生由耗散引起的非保守力，这为通过光控制电子序提供了新途径。

Abstract: In a quantum many-body system, a periodic drive can often generate effective static forces on the slow collective degrees of freedom. We study the static forces generated by light on electronic order parameters in solid-state systems. We show that the forces can have nonconservative components that originate from dissipation, i.e., optical absorption. This effect is demonstrated in two nontrivial examples. In excitonic insulators, via interband excitations, the light field generates a nonconservative force on the phase of the excitonic order parameter. This force leads to an acceleration of the phase, which manifests as a shift of the photon emission peak from an exciton condensate. In materials with an incommensurate charge density wave, a propagating light field generates a nonconservative force on the phase of its order parameter. It drives the charge density wave into sliding motion, leading to a DC electric current via topological Thouless pumping.

</details>


### [234] [On the relaxation problem in statistical mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30871)
*Giuseppe Del Vecchio Del Vecchio*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文重新表述了统计力学中的弛豫问题，关注局部时间统计量作为弛豫的操作对象，并提出预测信念与观测者可用信息之间的关系，赋予熵以学习解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 明确统计力学中弛豫问题的操作对象，探讨预测的本质及其与观测者信息的关系。

Method: 通过分析无时钟观测者在随机时间点记录的信号局部时间统计量，结合数学模型推断分布，研究有限有界系统的弛豫行为。

Result: 发现全局不可逆弛豫可能发生但存在特殊初始条件，预测形式依赖于观测者选择的损失函数，熵被解释为观测者与系统未知历史之间的互信息。

Conclusion: 弛豫预测依赖于观测者的可用信息和损失函数选择，熵的稳态值由可用信息决定，具有学习意义。

Abstract: We reformulate the relaxation problem in statistical mechanics by making explicit what are the \emph{operational} objects subject to relaxation: the local time statistics of the recorded signal $Z(t)$. These local time statistics are simply the estimated histograms of observations $\{Z(t_i)\}_{i=1}^M$ performed at uniformly random times $\{t_i\}_{i=1}^M$ by a clockless observer. The subject of prediction is a belief about a future fresh out-of-sample reading of a measurement outcome whose distribution is inferred from the mathematical model believed to be true. For finite bounded systems of $N\ge 1$ degrees of freedom global irreversible relaxation of predictions can occur but special initial conditions exist. The form of the predictions depends on certain loss functions whose choice is up to the particular observer. Finally, entropy is given a learning interpretation as mutual information between the observer and the unknown past of the system under consideration and, in complete generality, its stationary value depends on the information available.

</details>


### [235] [Ornstein-Uhlenbeck Process Driven by Multiple Dichotomous Noises](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29226)
*Silvio Kalaj,Dongho Lee,Enzo Marinari,Jae-Hyung Jeon,Pascal Viot,Gleb Oshanin*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 研究了一种由K个独立二值噪声驱动的广义Ornstein-Uhlenbeck过程，得到了稳态位置分布和所有累积量的精确表达式，揭示了稳态结构的丰富特性，并分析了无序对稳态的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在非平衡、有界且具有有限相关时间的噪声下，广义Ornstein-Uhlenbeck过程的统计特性与经典高斯白噪声驱动情况的不同。

Method: 通过解析方法求解稳态分布和累积量，并建立到具有有界跳跃的异质随机飞行过程的映射，分析多种极限情形及无序平均下的稳态分布。

Result: 获得了稳态位置分布和所有累积量的精确表达式；发现稳态具有紧凑支撑、代数分支点奇异性、边缘发散和多重极值等复杂结构；对于指数分布的淬火振幅系综，推导出无序平均的稳态分布，显示无序导致指数尾部并带有非平凡指数的代数因子。

Conclusion: 该系统展现出比经典模型更丰富的稳态结构，非平衡有界噪声和无序显著改变了系统的统计行为，揭示了多源噪声叠加和无序效应在随机动力学中的重要作用。

Abstract: We study a generalized Ornstein-Uhlenbeck process driven by a superposition of $K$ independent dichotomous noises with arbitrary fixed amplitudes and switching rates. Unlike the classical Ornstein-Uhlenbeck process driven by equilibrium Gaussian white noise, the present system is governed by bounded nonequilibrium fluctuations with finite correlation times. We obtain exact expressions for the stationary position distribution and all cumulants, and show that the stationary state possesses an unexpectedly rich structure, including compact support, algebraic branch-point singularities, edge divergences, and multiple extrema. We establish a mapping onto a heterogeneous random-flight process with bounded jumps, yielding a transparent probabilistic interpretation of the stationary measure. We further analyze several limiting regimes, including the crossover to Gaussian statistics for large numbers of noise sources. For ensembles with exponentially-distributed quenched amplitudes, we derive exact disorder-averaged stationary distributions and show that disorder fundamentally alters the stationary state, producing exponential tails decorated by algebraic prefactors with non-trivial exponents.

</details>


### [236] [Phase Diagram and Critical Behaviour of the Two-Dimensional Potts Model with Long-Range Quenched Disorder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29710)
*Rodolfo Rocha,Leticia F. Cugliandolo,Marco Picco*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 研究了q=3和q=8 Potts模型在空间相关无序下的相图与临界性质，利用Fortuin-Kasteleyn簇的缠绕概率细化了相变行为，并通过磁性观测量阐明了磁化率双峰结构的物理起源，验证了超标度关系在所有研究区域的适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于Chippari等人提出的相图，进一步探究Potts模型在幂律衰减无序下的有限-无序到无限-无序固定点的转变机制。

Method: 采用Fortuin-Kasteleyn簇的缠绕概率分析相图，并结合磁性观测量研究磁化率的双峰结构及超标度关系。

Result: 细化了随指数a变化的相变行为，明确了磁化率双峰的物理起源，并证实超标度关系在所有参数区域均成立。

Conclusion: 在不同无序关联长度下，Potts模型的临界行为可通过缠绕概率和磁性响应准确刻画，且超标度关系保持有效。

Abstract: We study the phase diagram and critical properties of the $q = 3$ and $q = 8$ Potts models with spatially correlated disorder governed by a power-law decay with exponent $a$. Building on the phase diagram proposed by Chippari et al. [3], where a transition from finite-disorder to infinite-disorder fixed points was identified as a function of a, we refine this picture by using wrapping probabilities of Fortuin-Kasteleyn clusters. Furthermore, using magnetic observables, we clarify the physical origin of the double-peak structure in the magnetic susceptibility and establish the validity of the hyper-scaling relation across all investigated regimes.

</details>


### [237] [Representation Transitions Reveal Predictive Structure in Complex Systems: A Trajectory-Level Reconstruction in a Critical System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29777)
*Jiaqi Pan*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文通过重新模拟复杂系统中的轨迹，比较了不同表示形式对预测能力的影响，发现表示方法的选择决定了是否能捕捉到预测所需的结构信息，而非仅仅依赖相关性强度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上使用群体平均的标量统计量来描述复杂系统的轨迹，但这些方法无法捕捉到轨迹中的结构信息。作者希望通过系统性地比较不同的表示形式，揭示何种表示方法能够更好地提取预测所需的信息。

Method: 通过对同一轨迹集合采用从标量统计、离散多特征分类到轨迹级结构表示和连续低维压缩等多种表示形式进行数值重模拟，并结合诊断性替代测试和多尺度折叠验证，评估各表示方法的预测性能。

Result: 尽管某一离散表示的最强单个特征与目标的相关性更高（r=0.588），但它未能区分最重要的两种情况；而连续低维表示虽然 headline 统计量相关性较低（r=0.540），却在八次独立的尺度去除折叠中稳定地区分了这两类情况（r=0.677–0.717，全 p<0.001）。关键因素在于表示是否保留了与预测相关的结构，而非相关性强弱。

Conclusion: 在复杂系统中，数据表示方式本身是一个关键的科学变量，决定了潜在的预测结构能否被识别，不应被视为下游分析的次要选择。

Abstract: Trajectories in complex systems often contain structure that is not captured by the population-averaged, scalar statistics traditionally used to describe them. Here we reconstruct, through independent numerical re-simulation rather than literature review, a systematic search across representational forms - from single scalar statistics, through two independent discrete multi-feature classification attempts, to a trajectory-level structural representation and a diagnostic surrogate test, to a continuous low-dimensional compression - applied to the same underlying trajectory ensemble in a critical complex system. Predictive failure is not explained by insufficient correlation: one discrete representation's own strongest individual feature correlates with the target more strongly ($r=0.588$) than the eventual successful representation's own headline statistic ($r=0.540$), yet fails to separate the ensemble's two most consequential cases - while the continuous representation succeeds, robustly, across eight independent scale-removal folds ($r=0.677$-$0.717$, all $p<0.001$). The determining factor was not correlation strength but whether the representation preserved the structure relevant to prediction. These results indicate that the choice of representation determines whether predictive structure in complex systems becomes accessible - a scientific variable in its own right, not a downstream analysis choice.

</details>


### [238] [Branching stochastic mechanics. I. Clustering and connected correlations within a branching-process representation of the Schrödinger equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29807)
*Eric Dumonteil,Benoît Bischoff,Alain Letourneau,Loïc Thulliez,Corentin Doutre*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于Schrödinger-Nagasawa变换的量子态随机模型，通过前向和后向扩散场的乘积构建Born密度，并引入分支超过程来描述量子路径的统计行为。研究发现，即使在扩展的量子态中，有限范围的相关性仍可隐藏于统计分布中，且与粒子状组织结构相容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子系统中有限范围关联是否能在扩展态的统计中隐匿，从而为量子测量和经典极限提供新的随机过程解释。

Method: 利用Schrödinger-Nagasawa变换将波函数表示为正向和反向扩散场的乘积，引入受Bohm/Fisher控制的分支超过程（Φ_F 和 Φ_B），并通过涨落响应机制建立关联核C_FB，分析其谱分解与屏蔽长度。

Result: 发现了在平均意义上精确重现Schrödinger动力学的随机过程；识别出超临界、临界和次临界聚类行为；建立了有限关联长度ξ_FB = √(D_eff/μ_FB)的存在性，表明扩展态中可存在局域关联。

Conclusion: 扩展的量子态可以同时具有长程延伸性和短程关联性，这种双重特性暗示了类似粒子的组织结构可能源于内在的随机动力学机制。

Abstract: Can finite-range correlations hide in the statistics of an extended quantum state? The Schrödinger-Nagasawa transform represents the wave function by positive forward and backward diffusion fields whose product is the Born density. We promote them to branching superprocesses, \(Φ_F\) and \(Φ_B\): diffusion samples stochastic paths, whereas Bohm/Fisher-controlled branching generates genealogies of alternative continuations. With rates evaluated on the prescribed Born density, their means reproduce Schrödinger dynamics exactly. The connected sector exhibits supercritical, critical, and subcritical clustering in confinement and has critical dimension \(d_c=2\) in free space. Stationary eigenmodes remain extended; subcritical clusters acquire the reduced de~Broglie scale. We then let the branching rate respond to the fluctuating product \(Φ_FΦ_B\). In the reciprocal basis, the fields equal a smooth reference \(R\) plus centered fluctuations \(ψ_F,ψ_B\), defining the signed kernel \(C_{\rm FB}(x,y)=\mathbb E_ω[ψ_F(x)ψ_B(y)]\). On the anticorrelated branch, \(ρ_{\rm BSM}(x)=-C_{\rm FB}(x,x)\) is the positive paired density. Stationary recovery requires its diagonal to match the Born profile, while off-diagonal decay defines the correlation range. The pair equation splits into collective and relative sectors: spectral cancellation selects the Born collective mode, while suppression of the leading density fluctuation selects the anticorrelated source channel. With relative diffusivity \(D_{\rm eff}\) and positive relaxation rate \(μ_{\rm FB}\), correlations have screening length \(ξ_{\rm FB}=\sqrt{D_{\rm eff}/μ_{\rm FB}}\). Thus an extended Born density and a finite correlation range can coexist in one stochastic kernel, suggesting particle-like organization.

</details>


### [239] [Asymmetry-controlled resonant transport in a Brownian flashing ratchet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29991)
*Sudipta Mandal,Dipanjan Chakraborty,Debasish Chaudhuri*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 研究了一维布朗闪变棘轮中定向输运现象，发现稳态电流随切换频率呈非单调变化，并存在共振峰值。电流表现出与势参数分离的标度律，共振频率依赖于势不对称性和势垒高度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在具有分段线性非对称周期势的一维布朗闪变棘轮中，如何实现并调控定向输运。

Method: 通过求解福克-普朗克方程的数值解和布朗动力学模拟，分析稳态电流对切换频率、势不对称性和势垒高度的依赖关系。

Result: 发现电流在中等不对称条件下遵循标度律，实现数据坍缩；电流幅值与不对称参数δ呈线性关系，共振频率符合ν(δ,Δ)=ν₀(Δ)/(1−b₀δ²)形式；提出基于双支松弛机制的物理解释。

Conclusion: 共振频率的(1−δ²)⁻¹标度关系源于非对称势两支间的耦合弛豫过程，为调控布朗棘轮中的定向输运提供了物理依据。

Abstract: We investigate directed transport in a one-dimensional Brownian flashing ratchet with a piecewise-linear asymmetric periodic potential. Numerical solutions of the Fokker--Planck equation and Brownian dynamics simulations reveal a nonmonotonic dependence of the stationary current on the switching frequency, with a resonant maximum whose position depends on the potential asymmetry $δ$ and barrier height $Δ$. For moderate asymmetry, $|δ|<0.5$, the current obeys a scaling form that separates the dependence on the potential parameters from a common frequency dependence, resulting in a data collapse upon appropriate scaling of the current and frequency. The current amplitude varies linearly with $δ$, while the resonance frequency follows $ν(δ,Δ)=ν_0(Δ)/ [1-b_0\, δ^2]$. We interpret the resulting $(1-δ^2)^{-1}$ scaling in terms of coupled relaxation along the two branches of the asymmetric potential, which provides a physical basis for the observed dependence of the resonant frequency on the potential asymmetry.

</details>


### [240] [Chemical potentials from structure factors: II. Charged multi-component mixtures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30060)
*Xiaoyu Wang,Roya Savoj,Musahid Ahmed,Bingqing Cheng*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文扩展了S0方法以计算带电混合物的化学势，结合成分空间框架和库仑处理方法，有效分离中性组成涨落与宏观电荷涨落，并考虑电荷中性约束。该方法应用于多卤化物水溶液和熔融盐混合物，揭示了离子特异性热力学效应及长波长静电关联的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带电多组分混合物的化学势在电解质热力学中至关重要，但难以通过原子模拟准确计算，因此需要一种可靠的方法来解决这一问题。

Method: 将S0方法与Part I中开发的成分空间框架结合，并引入对静态结构因子小波数极限的库仑处理，从而处理带电混合物中的化学势计算问题。

Result: 成功计算了多种卤化物水溶液的化学势，揭示了离子特异性热力学效应；同时计算了熔融盐混合物的混合自由能，表明正确描述长波长静电关联对热力学性质的重要性。

Conclusion: 扩展后的S0方法能够有效且准确地计算带电多组分混合物的化学势，适用于复杂电解质体系，为电解质热力学研究提供了有力工具。

Abstract: The chemical potentials of charged multi-component mixtures are central to electrolyte thermo- dynamics, but remain difficult to compute from atomistic simulations. The S0 method enables computing chemical potentials of mixtures from equilibrium molecular dynamics simulations. Here we extend the S0 method to charged mixtures by combining the composition-space framework developed in Part I: Neutral Multi-component Mixtures with a Coulombic treatment of the small- wavenumber limits of static structure factors. This approach separates thermodynamically relevant neutral composition fluctuations from forbidden macroscopic charge fluctuations, and further ac- counts for charge-neutrality constraints. We use the method to compute the chemical potentials of multiple-halide aqueous salt solutions, elucidating the ion-specific thermodynamic effects. We also calculate the mixing free energies of molten salt mixtures, demonstrating the importance of correctly describing long-wavelength electrostatic correlations.

</details>


### [241] [d'Q = TdS for Infinitesimal Irreversible Processes: On the Differential Form of the Clausius Inequality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30421)
*Hiroshi Shimahara*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出，在准静态不可逆过程中，克劳修斯不等式的微分形式应修正为等式 d'Q=TdS，而非传统认为的 d'Q < TdS。这表明内熵产生为二阶或更高阶小量，且熵仍为良好定义的状态量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上使用克劳修斯不等式的微分形式描述无穷小不可逆过程，但存在对熵是否在不可逆过程中仍为状态量的争议。作者旨在从纯热力学角度澄清这一问题。

Method: 通过分析准静态不可逆过程中的热量传递与熵变关系，推导出一阶近似下的正确表达式，并论证熵产生的阶数。

Result: 得出在一阶近似下，正确的表达式是 d'Q = TdS，意味着内部熵产生至少为二阶小量。

Conclusion: 熵在包含准静态不可逆过程时仍是良好定义的状态函数，无需依赖玻尔兹曼关系即可证明。

Abstract: Conventionally, the differential form of the Clausius inequality is adopted for infinitesimal irreversible processes. We show that the correct first-order form for infinitesimal quasi-static irreversible processes is the equality d'Q=TdS rather than d'Q < TdS. This provides a purely thermodynamic proof that internal entropy production is second-order or higher and confirms that entropy remains a well-defined state quantity even when quasi-static irreversible processes are included, without resorting to the Boltzmann relation.

</details>


### [242] [Time-delayed feedback turns Arrhenius escape logarithmic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30624)
*Roy Podgaetsky,Vishwajeet Kumar Arnab Pal,Ohad Shpielberg*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 时间延迟反馈可破坏热逃逸的指数标度律，导致逃逸时间变为对数依赖，并引发类似弹弓的快速逃逸轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究非马尔可夫性（由时间延迟反馈引起）如何影响热激活逃逸过程的动力学行为。

Method: 通过理论分析和动力学模拟，研究时间延迟反馈对势阱中热逃逸过程的影响，特别是逃逸时间与势垒高度的关系。

Result: 发现超过阈值后，延迟会 destabilize 势阱，热噪声触发不稳定性并被放大，产生 slingshot 逃逸轨迹；逃逸时间变为对数依赖于势垒，涨落服从Gumbel分布，且存在最优延迟使逃逸快于自由扩散。

Conclusion: 时间延迟是一种可调且实验可行的控制参数，可用于加速激活过程。

Abstract: Thermal escape is governed by the Arrhenius law, where the mean escape time scales exponentially with the barrier height. We show that the non-Markovianity induced by time-delayed feedback in the confining force removes this exponential scaling. Beyond a threshold set by the curvature of the minimum, the delay destabilizes the well, and the thermal noise seeds an instability that is subsequently amplified deterministically to the boundary leading to \textit{slingshot} escape trajectories. The escape time becomes logarithmic in the barrier, its fluctuations follow a Gumbel law, and an optimal delay enables escape faster than free diffusion. Our results propose time delay as a tunable and experimentally feasible control parameter for accelerating activated processes.

</details>


### [243] [Effective Hardcore Exclusion Without Exclusion: Two-Species Pair Annihilation Revisited](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30630)
*Su-Chan Park,Foster Thompson*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无硬核排斥的模型，展示了在双物种相互湮灭反应中出现的异常密度衰减行为，揭示了密度衰减指数不仅取决于单个物种的输运性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究低维系统中双物种相互湮灭反应导致的异常密度衰减行为，并探究其是否依赖于硬核排斥机制。

Method: 构建了一个局部密度非线性影响扩散速率的非对称扩散模型，分析不同微观过程（成对或三粒子）对密度衰减指数的影响。

Result: 发现至少一个物种通过成对粒子引起的非对称扩散时，系统呈现 $t^{-1/3}$ 衰减；而三粒子非对称过程导致 $t^{-1/4}$ 衰减。

Conclusion: 双物种湮灭系统的密度衰减指数并非仅由孤立物种的输运特性决定，挑战了以往认为 $t^{-1/3}$ 行为必须依赖硬核排斥的观点。

Abstract: Reaction-diffusion systems with two species that mutually annihilate through $A+B\to\emptyset$ reactions display a slow decay of the total density $ρ$ which follows an anomalous power law in low dimensions. For hardcore particles in one dimension, this decay follows $ρ\sim t^{-1/4}$ under symmetric diffusion and $ρ\sim t^{-1/3}$ under asymmetric diffusion without relative bias between the two species. The $t^{-1/3}$ behavior, in particular, has so far been observed exclusively in systems with hardcore exclusion. Here we construct a model of particles without hardcore exclusion that reproduces this same $t^{-1/3}$ scaling. In our model, both species undergo asymmetric diffusion with a rate that depends nonlinearly on the local density, a form of transport that, in the absence of the other species, is superdiffusive and falls into the Kardar-Parisi-Zhang universality class. The scaling behavior of $t^{-1/3}$ occurs when at least one of the two species undergoes asymmetric diffusion induced by microscopic processes involving pairs of particles, while asymmetric processes involving groups of three particles lead to $t^{-1/4}$ scaling associated with symmetric diffusion. This demonstrates that the pair annihilation density decay exponent is not determined exclusively by the transport properties of the isolated particle species.

</details>


### [244] [Kinetic temperatures and inertial effects in a nonequilibrium bead-spring model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30809)
*Jetin E Thomas,Ramandeep S. Johal*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 研究了由两个不同质量的耦合珠子组成的非平衡稳态模型，发现熵产生和热流在质量交换下保持不变，而能量存储依赖于质量排列。动能温度是控制输运和熵产生的关键变量，并统一了能量、输运和不可逆性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨非平衡稳态下质量不对称对热输运、能量存储及熵产生的影响，揭示不同物理量在奇异质量极限下的行为差异。

Method: 采用协方差矩阵方法结合欠阻尼朗之万动力学的数值模拟，分析稳态概率分布、热流和熵产生。

Result: 发现熵产生和热流在珠子质量交换下不变，而能量存储量依赖于质量配置；动能温度差控制输运和熵产生，其和决定平均能量；在无限质量极限下，只有以动能温度表示的关系仍成立；并提出了一个有效温度描述类平衡态分布。

Conclusion: 动能温度是组织该系统热力学行为的核心变量，统一了能量、输运与不可逆性，揭示了能量观测量的路径依赖性与输运量的稳健性之间的根本区别。

Abstract: We investigate a nonequilibrium steady-state model consisting of two coupled beads with arbitrary masses in contact with two thermal baths at different temperatures. Using a covariance-matrix approach together with numerical simulations of the underdamped Langevin dynamics, we characterize steady-state probability distributions, heat transport, and entropy production. We show that irreversibility measures such as entropy production and heat current are invariant under an exchange of the bead masses, whereas energy-storage observables depend explicitly on the mass arrangement in a symmetrical set up. This reveals a fundamental distinction: energy observables exhibit path dependence in singular mass limits, while transport and irreversibility remain well defined. We show that kinetic temperatures provide the natural variables governing the thermodynamics of the system: their difference controls transport and entropy production, while their sum determines the mean energy via a model specific generalized equipartition relation. In the infinite-mass limit, only constitutive relations expressed in terms of kinetic temperatures remain meaningful. Thus, energy, transport, and irreversibility are unified through kinetic temperatures as the organizing variables. We also derive an effective temperature that defines an equilibrium-like canonical distribution. Finally, we analyze the notion of ergodicity and show that the time-averaged observables converge significantly faster than the ensemble averages.

</details>


### [245] [Inertia-Driven Information Flow and Symmetry Breaking in a Nonequilibrium Two-Bead System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30849)
*Jetin E. Thomas,Ramandeep S. Johal*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了非平衡双粒子系统中信息流的生成机制，发现惯性和热不对称性在信息流动中的关键作用，揭示了其在纳米机器中构建信息引擎的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解非平衡系统中信息流的产生机制，探索惯性和微观异质性如何影响信息景观，为设计纳米尺度的信息引擎提供理论基础。

Method: 通过分析过阻尼和欠阻尼条件下耦合两个热浴的双粒子系统的动力学行为，结合对称性扰动分析与信息流响应面的几何结构研究，揭示参数空间中信息流的发散特性。

Result: 发现在欠阻尼条件下，特定参数路径上信息流出现标度发散，且惯性使平坦的信息景观产生曲率；质量不对称导致极值移动，Hessian分析显示主模式存在能级接触和双峰结构。

Conclusion: 建立了描述非平衡系统中信息景观形成的最小框架，表明惯性和微观异质性可调控信息流，为扩展至复杂异质网络提供了可能路径。

Abstract: We investigate information-flow generation in a nonequilibrium two-bead system coupled to two heat baths. We show that the system acts as an information-flow generator in both overdamped and underdamped regimes, with the underdamped dynamics revealing a divergence of the scaled information flow along specific paths in the thermal asymmetry--inertia parameter space that is hidden in the overdamped limit. This information generation suggests a possible route toward information engines and demon-like mechanisms in nanomachines. A symmetry-perturbation analysis of the response landscape of information flow reveals a geometric structure reminiscent of a Ginzburg--Landau framework: the symmetric reference state can correspond to a minimum or maximum depending on the perturbation direction, while the flat overdamped landscape develops a finite curvature under inertia. Mass asymmetry shifts the resulting maxima, and Hessian eigenvalue and eigenvector analysis reveals level touching of principal modes and bimodality along a constant-diffusion path. These results establish a minimal framework for understanding how inertia and microscopic heterogeneity shape information landscapes in nonequilibrium systems, with potential extensions to more complex heterogeneous networks.

</details>


### [246] [Compensation in continuous symmetric trilayered planar ferrimagnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30941)
*Olivia Mallick,Muktish Acharyya*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 研究了三层各向异性XY磁性模型的平衡性质，发现系统在特定参数范围内表现出高温临界行为和低温补偿行为，且补偿温度与临界温度随单点各向异性的增强而升高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索具有面内铁磁和面间反铁磁相互作用的三层各向异性XY模型的热力学行为，特别是临界与补偿现象。

Method: 采用蒙特卡洛模拟结合Metropolis单自旋更新算法对系统进行数值研究。

Result: 发现在一定参数范围内存在临界温度和补偿温度，二者均随单点各向异性增强而上升；有限尺寸分析显示临界温度附近关联长度增长。

Conclusion: 该三明治结构的各向异性XY系统展现出丰富的相变行为，单点各向异性可有效调控其临界与补偿特性。

Abstract: The trilayered (A-B-A type) anisotropic (single site) XY magnetic model is considered with the intra-plane ferromagnetic and
  the inter-plane antiferromagnetic interactions. The equilibrium properties of such trilayered anisotropic XY system has been studied by
  Monte Carlo simulation with Metropolis single spin (randomly chosen) update algorithm. In a given range of interaction parameters, the system has been found to exhibit the high temperature {\it critical} (vanishing sublattice magnetisations and total magnetisation) and low temperature {\it compensation} (vanishing total magnetisation even for non-vanishing sublattice magnetisations) behaviours. The compensation temperature and the critical temperature both are found to increase with the increase of single site anisotropy. The comprehensive phase diagram is drawn. The finite size study reveals the growth of correlations near the critical temperature.

</details>


### [247] [Overcoming critical slowing down in frustrated spin systems by learned multiscale sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31114)
*Gabriele Bandini,Giulio Biroli,Patrick Charbonneau,Andrea Gambassi*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于学习的多尺度采样方法WCRG，用于克服传统簇算法在受挫系统中失效的问题，能有效缓解临界慢化并实现高效采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统构造性簇算法（如Swendsen-Wang和Wolff方法）在存在微弱受挫时失效，无法有效处理受挫系统的临界慢化问题。

Method: 采用小波条件重整化群（WCRG）采样方法，通过学习二维软自旋受挫模型的集体涨落概率分布，并从粗到细递归生成构型。

Result: WCRG方法在不同物相下均能复现系统的统计特性，在Ising类临界点处每尺度仅需O(1)次扫描即可去相关，总采样复杂度为O(log₂L)，显著优于局部MCMC方法；但其精度依赖于能量模型对小波条件分布的表达能力。

Conclusion: 学习型多尺度采样可突破传统簇算法在受挫系统中的局限，WCRG为解决临界慢化提供了新途径，其效率与模型表达力之间存在权衡。

Abstract: Cluster algorithms, such as the Swendsen--Wang and Wolff methods, are among the most successful MCMC methods for mitigating critical slowing down in statistical systems. These constructive cluster algorithms, however, fail in the presence of even extremely weak frustration. Here, we sidestep this fundamental limitation by learning rather than constructing the relevant clusters. Specifically, we use the wavelet conditional renormalization group (WCRG) sampling method to learn the probability distribution of collective fluctuations of a frustrated two-dimensional soft-spin model. Configurations are then generated recursively from coarse to fine scales by sampling conditional wavelet distributions. The WCRG method reproduces the main statistical properties of the system across different phases, including the local-field distribution and the structure factor. At an Ising-like critical point, the conditional dynamics remains decorrelated within $\mathcal{O}(1)$ sweeps at each scale, yielding an overall sampling complexity of $\mathcal{O}(\log_2 L)$, thus making WCRG much more efficient than standard local MCMC methods. These results show that learned multiscale sampling can overcome critical slowing down in frustrated systems for which conventional cluster algorithms fail. By assessing the sampling accuracy of different observables, we also clarify the main tradeoff of the WCRG method: the accuracy of the fast sampling scheme depends on the expressiveness of the energy-based model used to estimate the wavelet conditional distributions.

</details>
