# physics.comp-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [248] [Characteristic Mode Analysis of Composite Nanostructures using a Coupled System of Volume Integral and Hydrodynamic Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29709)
*Meruyert Khamitova,Ran Zhao,Doolos Aibek Uulu,Sebastian Celis Sierra,Hakan Bagci*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 提出了基于体积分方程和流体动力学方程的全结构和子结构特征模式分析方法，用于复合金属-介质纳米结构，子结构方法在计算上更高效，并能有效识别金属区域的共振。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了更高效地分析复合金属-介质纳米结构中的共振特性，特别是金属部分的模式响应。

Method: 基于体积积分方程与流体动力学方程构建全结构和子结构的广义特征值方程，后者通过消除介质区域未知量来降低系统维度。

Result: 子结构CMA比全结构CMA计算更高效，在非谐振介质环境下可识别相同共振，并能揭示介质环境对金属共振的影响，如红移和模态杂化。

Conclusion: 所提出的子结构CMA方法在保持精度的同时提升了计算效率，适用于分析复杂纳米结构中金属与介质的相互作用。

Abstract: Full-structure and sub-structure characteristic mode analysis (CMA) formulations are developed for composite metallic--dielectric nanostructures based on a coupled system of volume integral equations (VIE) and the hydrodynamic equation (HDE). In the full-structure CMA, the generalized eigenvalue equation (GEE) is constructed from the matrix of the complete coupled system, and the resulting characteristic currents describe the response of the entire composite nanostructure. In the sub-structure CMA, the GEE is constructed from a reduced system, derived from the coupled system by eliminating the dielectric-region unknowns, so that it is expressed only in terms of the metallic-region currents. This isolates the resonances of the metallic region while still incorporating the effect of the dielectric region through the reduced system. Because the reduced system has a smaller dimension, the sub-structure CMA is computationally more efficient than the full-structure CMA and, when the dielectric does not resonate in the frequency range of interest, identifies the same resonances. Both formulations are validated against extinction cross-section (ECS) results and are used to characterize how a dielectric environment reshapes the metallic resonances, including substrate-induced red-shifts and, for high-contrast substrates in direct contact, hybridized modal responses.

</details>


### [249] [A wide-range temperature-dependent deep potential for sodium with near-experimental accuracy from melting to the critical point](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29826)
*G. S. Demyanov,D. V. Minakov,V. B. Fokin,P. R. Levashov*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本研究利用基于PBE、AM05和r²SCAN交换关联泛函的有限温度DFT数据构建了温度依赖的深度势模型，用于研究钠从室温到临界区的热物理性质。其中r²SCAN模型预测的临界参数与推荐值高度一致，并进一步计算了多种热力学和输运性质，验证了结果的热力学一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了准确描述钠在宽温区特别是临界区域的热物理性质，需构建基于有限温度密度泛函理论（DFT）的高效分子动力学模型，以克服传统方法在自由能精度和温度依赖性方面的局限。

Method: 采用不同交换-相关泛函（PBE、AM05、r²SCAN）生成有限温度DFT数据，构建温度依赖的深度势（deep potential）模型；结合直接固液共存模拟、液气共存模拟、声学模拟及状态方程计算，系统研究钠的相变、输运和热力学性质。

Result: 基于r²SCAN泛函的模型给出临界温度Tc=2.508(8) kK、密度ρc=0.203(4) g/cm³、压力Pc=0.249(6) kbar，与推荐值高度一致；常压bcc密度偏差仅0.06%；熔化温度为346(2) K，略低于推荐值；表面张力在临界点附近趋近于零；自扩散系数和剪切粘度扩展至膨胀液体和近临界区；声速的两种计算方式结果一致，验证了热力学自洽性。

Conclusion: 基于r²SCAN交换-相关泛函构建的深度势模型能高精度描述钠在宽温区的热物理行为，适用于从固体到临界区域的多相态模拟，为金属体系的高温高压物性预测提供了可靠工具。

Abstract: We construct temperature-dependent deep potentials for sodium using finite-temperature DFT data obtained with the PBE, AM05, and $r^2$SCAN exchange-correlation functionals and investigate the thermophysical properties of sodium from room temperature to the critical region. The predicted critical parameters show a dependence on the exchange-correlation functional. The model based on the $r^2$SCAN functional gives a critical temperature $T_c=2.508(8)$ kK, density $ρ_c=0.203(4)$ g/cm$^{3}$, and pressure $P_c=0.249(6)$ kbar, in close agreement with the recommended values. This model is then used to reconstruct the normal-pressure and critical isobars and to calculate the enthalpy, heat capacities, thermal expansion coefficient, bulk moduli, Grüneisen parameter, and speed of sound. The calculated normal-pressure bcc density differs by only 0.06% from the experimental value. Direct solid-liquid coexistence simulations give a melting temperature of $346(2)$ K, 25 K below the recommended value, consistent with the sensitivity expected from meV/atom free-energy errors. Liquid-vapor coexistence simulations reproduce the binodal and yield a surface tension that approaches zero near the critical point. The calculated self-diffusion coefficient and shear viscosity extend the available transport-property data into the expanded-liquid and near-critical regions, where direct experimental information is sparse. We also demonstrate the thermodynamic consistency of the results by comparing the speed of sound obtained from direct acoustic simulations with that calculated from the equation of state.

</details>


### [250] [Cartesian tensor equivariant machine-learning force field for spin-dependent atomistic simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30338)
*Junjie Wang,Yijie Zhu,Zhongwei Zhang,Zhiyue Guo,Xudong Zhu,Lixin He,Chi Ding,Jian Sun*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了HotPP-Spin，一种基于笛卡尔张量等变消息传递的磁性机器学习原子间势模型，能够统一描述交换主导和自旋轨道耦合诱导的磁相互作用，并在多种磁性体系中准确再现磁能景观与动力学行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁性材料中原子结构与自旋自由度之间存在复杂耦合，传统原子模拟方法难以在实验相关的时空尺度上进行精确建模，因此需要一种兼具精度与可扩展性的新方法。

Method: 提出HotPP-Spin模型，将原子磁矩作为显式的轴向矢量自由度处理，利用笛卡尔张量等变消息传递传播空间反演和时间反演奇偶性，通过标量自旋依赖势能面导出能量守恒的原子力和有效磁场。

Result: 该模型在共线磁性、非共线磁性和自旋轨道耦合导致的磁各向异性等基准测试中表现出高精度；对单层VSe₂的随机自旋动力学模拟预测居里温度为415–435 K，与实验值418.5±7.8 K高度一致。

Conclusion: 笛卡尔张量消息传递为连接第一性原理磁性能量与大规模原子级结构-自旋耦合模拟提供了通用途径。

Abstract: Magnetic materials exhibit an intricate coupling between atomic structure and spin degrees of freedom, posing a fundamental challenge for atomistic simulations across experimentally relevant length and time scales. Here we introduce HotPP-Spin, a spin-dependent extension of HotPP for magnetic machine learning interatomic potentials, built on Cartesian tensor equivariant message passing. Atomic magnetic moments are treated as explicit axial-vector degrees of freedom, while spatial-inversion and time-reversal parities are propagated through the tensor couplings. This construction provides a unified representation of exchange-dominated and spin-orbit-induced interactions without imposing predefined analytical interaction forms. A scalar spin-dependent potential energy surface yields energy-conserving atomic forces and magnetic effective fields through differentiation. Benchmarks spanning collinear magnetism, noncollinear magnetism, and spin-orbit-coupling-induced magnetic anisotropy show that HotPP-Spin accurately describes magnetic energy landscapes, magnetic forces, and magnetic-order-dependent energy-volume relations within the same general framework. For H-phase monolayer VSe\(_2\), stochastic spin-dynamics simulations using the learned magnetic effective fields locate the finite-size magnetic ordering crossover at 415--435~K, in close numerical agreement with the reported experimental value of \(418.5\pm7.8\)~K. These results establish Cartesian tensor message passing as a general route for connecting first-principles magnetic energetics with large-scale atomistic simulations of coupled structural and spin phenomena.

</details>


### [251] [OpenMUSTANC (MUltiple Scattering Theory At Nanoplasmonic Cavities): A MATLAB toolbox for the simulation of Plasmonic Sphere Aggregates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30565)
*Xin Zheng,Christos Mystilidis,Christos Tserkezis,Guy A. E. Vandenbosch,Xuezhi Zheng*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个用于模拟多个球形界面的MATLAB工具箱，集成了多种介观物理模型，适用于纳米等离激元研究中的非局域效应分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了研究纳米结构中的非局域效应，需要结合经典电磁学与介观材料模型的新计算方法。

Method: 利用矢量球面波函数表示电磁场，采用S矩阵方法处理球形界面上的入射和散射场，并使用平移矩阵处理不同展开中心的传播波。

Result: 实现了对平面波、偶极子和电子束等多种激励源的模拟，支持截面计算、远/近场映射、荧光增强、Purcell因子、量子产率以及阴极发光和电子能量损失概率的后处理分析。

Conclusion: 该工具箱模块化设计，高效且通用，为纳米等离激元领域的研究人员提供了详细的使用指南。

Abstract: Mesoscopic physical models, including the Hydrodynamic Drude Model (HDM), the Generalized Nonlocal Optical Response (GNOR) Model, and the Surface Response Model (SRM), have been proposed to investigate nonlocal effects in nanometric structures. The combination of classical electromagnetism with these mesoscopic material models calls for new computational electromagnetic (CEM) algorithms, or update of conventional ones, in what is termed computational mesoscopic electromagnetics (CMEM). In this work, we present a MATLAB toolbox for the simulation of multiple spherical interfaces with arbitrary relative positions, with the incorporation of the aforementioned mesoscopic models. The method exploits vector spherical wave functions to properly express electric and magnetic fields, an S matrix formulation for the efficient treatment of incident and scattered fields at spherical interfaces, and a translation matrix to deal with propagating waves with different expansion centers. Excitation sources can be chosen among arbitrarily polarized plane waves, dipoles and electron beams. For the post-processing part, the calculation of cross sections and far/near-field mapping; fluorescence enhancement, Purcell factor and quantum yield; and cathodoluminescence and electron energy-loss probability is implemented. The toolbox is built in a modular manner, and each part (routine) has its own important functionality. This paper provides a full explanation of the proposed highly efficient and general toolbox, and a detailed guideline for researchers in the nanoplasmonics community.

</details>

### [252] [Fractal Confinement and Magnetic Self-sabotage of Current Flow: Electrons Near the Metal-insulator Crossover in a 2D $δ$-layer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28739)
*Xinghai Zhang,Matthew S. Foster,Markus Mueller*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 研究了二维δ掺杂半导体中的低温输运和巡游磁性，发现近临界密度下存在两种输运机制：低温初期电导随温度降低而增加，随后因微弱磁矩形成导致绝缘下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索二维δ掺杂半导体中低温下的电子输运行为与巡游磁性的关联，理解无序和相互作用对量子相变的影响。

Method: 采用大规模Hartree-Fock计算，结合随机跃迁和库仑排斥参数U₀，模拟低温下的输运和磁性行为。

Result: 发现电导σ(T)在T低于U₀/10时上升，但在T~U₀/100时因形成少量磁矩而出现绝缘转变；分形波函数增强局域磁相互作用，导致电流先集中于分支细丝后被动态SU(2)对称性破缺抑制。

Conclusion: 在二维δ掺杂系统中，局域磁矩与分形电子态共同作用，引发非单调温度依赖的输运行为，揭示了动态对称性破缺在绝缘转变中的关键作用。

Abstract: We study low-$T$ transport and itinerant magnetism in 2D $δ$-doped semiconductors. Large-scale Hartree-Fock calculations with random hoppings and repulsion $U_0$ reveal two transport regimes near a critical density. The conductivity $σ(T)$ initially increases as $T$ drops below $U_0 / 10$. However, at $T \sim U_0/100$, a tiny density of magnetic moments forms, driving an insulating downturn. Fractal wavefunctions locally amplify magnetic interactions, initially concentrating currents into branched filaments, but ultimately choking them off via dynamic SU(2) symmetry breaking.

</details>


### [253] [Pseudo Entropy in Quantum Spin Chains: from Integrability to Chaos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29810)
*Tara Bahadur Rana,Yadav Raj Dahal,Kiran Adhikari*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了量子自旋链中伪熵和谱动力学在量子混沌诊断中的作用，推导出热伪熵实部与谱形因子的精确关系，并发现其虚部可提供费舍尔零点信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索伪熵是否可作为量子混沌的有效诊断工具，并揭示其在不同量子系统中的行为特征。

Method: 通过解析推导热伪熵实部与谱形因子的关系，分析XXZ模型和混合场伊辛模型中的伪熵行为，并研究空间伪熵的临界标度特性。

Result: 发现热伪熵实部呈现典型的dip-ramp-plateau结构，可有效探测混沌；虚部虽不直接诊断混沌，但能反映Fisher零点信息；空间伪熵在可积与混沌体系中均保持对数标度，且满足非正性猜想。

Conclusion: 伪熵的实部可作为量子混沌的可靠探针，而虚部提供了额外的非平衡物理信息，同时伪熵与谱复杂度及全息间可能存在深刻联系。

Abstract: In this work, we study pseudo entropy and spectral dynamics in quantum spin chains (Heisenberg XXZ model and the mixed-field Ising model) to investigate whether they can serve as diagnostics of quantum chaos. First, we derive an exact relation between the real part of the thermal pseudo entropy and the spectral form factor. This provided evidence that pseudo entropy could exhibit the characteristic dip, logarithmic ramp, and plateau, implying the precise manner in which pseudo entropy probes chaos. We found that the imaginary part, while it does not diagnose chaos, can still provide crucial information about Fisher zeros that is not available in the real counterpart. For spatial pseudo entropy in the XXZ chain, we find that the logarithmic critical scaling persists in both integrable and chaotic regimes, and that the non-positivity conjecture holds. Finally, we derive an exact connection between pseudo entropy, relative pseudo entropy, and spectral complexity with a potential connection to holography.

</details>


### [254] [Hartree Fragmentation and Long-Range Pair Networks in Aperiodic Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30746)
*Yogeshwar Prasad*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 研究了在缓慢变化的非周期势和随机相互作用下的费米子行为，发现共振对象集合在跨越特定参数时发生重构，并讨论了不同条件下耦合的持续性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索费米子在复杂势场和随机相互作用下的共振行为及其集合重构机制。

Method: 通过理论分析和标度律推导，研究势场和相互作用对共振供应和对象集合的影响。

Result: 发现共振对象在跨越α-2n时发生重构，孤立翼键以有限密度存活并恢复部分对数行为，标度律保持不变。

Conclusion: 非周期势和随机相互作用显著影响费米子系统的共振特性，揭示了新的集体行为模式。

Abstract: For fermions in a slowly varying aperiodic potential with random interactions $V_{ij}/|i-j|^α$, the potential fixes the resonance supply $N_0 \propto L^{2-n}/h$. On crossing $α-2n$, wherever Hartree fragmentation is operative, the resonant-object ensemble reconstructs: turning-point runs fragment into clusters whose intrinsic matching law resolves no logarithmic singularity, while isolated wing bonds survive at finite density and restore a reduced logarithm. The scaling $x^{4-2n-α}_{pair} ln{x_{pair}} \propto h^2/(V t0)$ is unchanged. Random coefficient fluctuations sustain the $R^{-α}$ coupling; uniform ones cancel to $R^{-α-2}$.

</details>


### [255] [Resonance statistics, Fock-space branching, and long-range pair networks in slowly varying interacting chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30761)
*Yogeshwar Prasad*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文研究了在长程相互作用和准周期势下的共振态统计性质，揭示了不同α值下共振对象的演化规律，并提出了微观共振理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解在复杂无序系统中，长程相互作用如何影响局域化与共振态的形成机制。

Method: 通过平均相位统计、Hartree方差分析、Fock空间图构建以及壳层理论发展，系统研究共振态的分布与连接性。

Result: 发现了三个关键的长程阈值（α=1/2, α=2n, α=2），分别对应方差发散、共振对象转变和壳层求和临界；证明相互作用不改变主导供应律；构造了小系统下的共振图并揭示其分支特性。

Conclusion: 不同α区域下共振结构发生本质变化，但这些阈值本身并不直接对应局域化转变，需结合更多物理判据判断相变。

Abstract: In a slowly varying aperiodic potential $h_i=h\cos(2πβi^n+φ)$ with random power-law density interactions $V_{ij}/|i-j|^α$, the resonant-object ensemble changes across $α=2n$, wherever Hartree fragmentation is operative, from isolated two-level bonds to a mixture of fragmented multi-site clusters and isolated wing bonds that survive at finite density, while the pair-resonance scaling $x_{\rm pair}^{4-2n-α}\ln x_{\rm pair}\propto h^2/(Vt_0)$ is unchanged~\cite{letter}. Here we develop the microscopic resonance theory underlying these results, together with its domain of validity. We derive the exact phase-averaged resonance statistics --- the supply $N_0\propto L^{2-n}/h$, the correlated common-phase comb, and the closed-form Hartree variance --- proving that the interaction leaves the leading supply law unchanged. Exact construction of the resonant Fock-space graph at $L\le18$ shows that the order-one forward-branching scale $h_{\rm FB}\propto L^{2-n}$ carries no giant component: one-step branching and connectivity are inequivalent. We separate the fixed-pair matching law from the shell-averaged $q\ln(1/q)$ law of the bond ensemble and develop the comb into a mesoscopic shell theory; we map the fragmentation domain, with its support threshold $V_*(α)$ and the boundary-healing recursion; and we treat the marginal case $α=2$, where shell and matching marginalities compound into a double logarithm. Three long-range thresholds emerge with distinct meanings --- $α=1/2$ (the exact variance threshold of the random Fock-space energy and the square-summability boundary of the leading LIOM-dressing estimate), $α=2n$ (change of the local resonant objects), and $α=2$ (marginality of the long-range shell sum) --- none of which is, by itself, a localization transition.

</details>


### [256] [Learning rules for complex-valued patterns in networks of oscillators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30800)
*Federico Sbravati,Aida Todri-Sanial*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文将学习规则从实二值扩展到复数值自旋，提出了一种优于Hebbian学习的编码方法，适用于振荡器网络中的模式检索，并验证了其在局部和增量学习中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了更自然地表示灰度图案并提升模式存储与检索的性能，需要将学习规则扩展到复数值自旋系统。

Method: 提出了复数值自旋的学习规则，利用复数值权重矩阵存储多态神经元信息，并验证了其在振荡器网络中的局部和增量学习能力。

Result: 该方法在编码信息用于模式检索时表现优于标准Hebbian学习，在处理实值、复值相关及随机模式下均展现出良好的关联记忆行为。

Conclusion: 复数值自旋学习规则为灰度图像提供了更鲁棒的表示方式，并在振荡器神经网络中实现了高效的信息存储与检索。

Abstract: In this article, we extend learning rules from real binary to complex-valued spins. This formulation allows for a robust and natural representation of grayscale patterns, where spins behave as multi-state neurons and can be stored in a complex-valued weight matrix. We describe a rule that performs better than standard methods, such as Hebbian learning, to encode information in a suitable form for pattern retrieval with networks of oscillators. Since in neural networks it is of interest to have local and incremental learning rules, we prove the extension of a result by Diederich and Opper with our complex-valued spin formulation. We then test the behavior of the associative memory for the system of oscillators under different circumstances for both real-valued, as well as complex-valued correlated and random patterns.

</details>


<div id='hep-lat'></div>

# hep-lat [[Back]](#toc)

### [257] [Machine Learning Unveils Finite-volume Energy Shifts in Three-body System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28700)
*Wei-Jie Zhang,Zhenyu Zhang,Jifeng Hu,Bing-Nan Lu,Jin-Yi Pang,Qian Wang*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本研究通过结合符号回归与物理约束，提出了一种数据驱动的方法来推导适用于短程和长程相互作用的普适性三体有限体积外推公式，克服了传统方法在处理长程相互作用时的困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在格点计算中，有限体积外推（FVE）对于提取物理观测量至关重要。尽管短程势的FVE理论已较为成熟，但在两体和三体系统中，当相互作用力程接近格点尺寸L时，长程势的FVE仍具挑战性，因此需要发展新的方法以统一处理不同力程下的三体FVE问题。

Method: 扩展此前用于两体系统的数据驱动方案，采用符号回归工具PySR，结合物理约束条件，在三体系统中搜寻普适的有限体积外推解析表达式，并分别考虑短程势、纯长程势以及混合情况下的贡献。

Result: 成功复现了短程势下κ_3≫κ_2和κ_3∼κ_2两种极限情形；获得了纯长程势下的解析表达式；进一步引入短程修正后，得到了一个统一的FVE公式，该公式在力程约为1 fm的中间区域表现优异，且与原始PySR结果一致。

Conclusion: 机器学习与物理约束相结合的方法能够发现传统理论工具难以获得的新解析结果，展示了数据驱动方法在强子物理中的潜力，推动了格点QCD中复杂多体系统有限体积效应研究的发展。

Abstract: Finite-volume extrapolation (FVE) is essential for extracting physical observables in the lattice calculation. While rigorous FVE formulations are well established for short-range potentials in both two- and three-body systems, long-range interactions with force ranges comparable to the lattice size $L$ remain challenging. Extending a previous data-driven scheme for two-body systems, we apply symbolic regression (PySR) to uncover universal three-body FVE formulae. For short-range potentials, we reproduce the two limiting cases, i.e. $κ_3\ggκ_2$ and $κ_3\simκ_2$. For pure long-range potentials, we obtain a dedicated analytic expression, and after incorporating short-range contributions, we uncover a unified formula consistent with the original PySR solution, which performs excellently in the intermediate force range around 1 fm. This work demonstrates that combining machine learning with physical constraints can yield novel analytical results inaccessible to conventional theoretical tools, advancing data-driven methodologies in hadron physics.

</details>


### [258] [Quantum Simulations of Two-Dimensional Non-Abelian Adjoint String Breaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28752)
*Anthony N. Ciavarella,Roland de Putter,Ed Younis,Ermal Rrapaj*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于局部Krylov截断的方法，用于在三角晶格上的纯SU(2)格点规范理论中实现量子模拟，并在IBM量子设备上实现了对伴随弦断裂过程的模拟，结果与张量网络模拟具有定量一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了实现利用量子计算机直接研究强耦合量子场论动力学的潜力，需要发展能够在当前含噪声中等规模量子（NISQ）设备上运行的有效模拟方法。

Method: 采用局部Krylov方法对SU(2)规范理论进行截断，构建了包含动态胶子的伴随弦模型，并设计了局域算符以探测弦的振荡与断裂；随后在8×8和16×8三角晶格上使用IBM量子处理器进行了量子电路模拟。

Result: 成功在156个物理量子比特上实现了多达7634个CZ门、双量子比特门深度达218的电路模拟，观察到了弦振荡和胶球产生速率，表现出明显的非阿贝尔特征，且结果与张量网络计算高度一致。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了在真实量子硬件上模拟复杂规范理论动力学的可行性，验证了局部截断方法的有效性，并揭示了非阿贝尔规范群的动力学特征，为未来量子模拟高能物理现象提供了路径。

Abstract: Quantum computers offer the potential to directly probe the dynamics of strongly coupled quantum field theories. As a step towards reaching this potential, local Krylov-based truncations of a pure SU(2) lattice gauge theory on a triangular lattice are constructed. Adjoint strings connected to dynamical gluons are constructed in this truncated theory, and the resonances dominating the long-term dynamics at a large value of the gauge coupling are determined. Local operators are constructed to identify string oscillations and breakings. This is used to perform a quantum simulation of adjoint string breaking on an $8\times8$ and $16\times8$-site lattice with ibm_boston using all 156 qubits. Quantitative agreement with tensor network simulations is obtained for circuits with 7,634 CZ gates with a two-qubit gate depth of 218. In this simulation, the rates of oscillations and glueball production are identified with a distinctly non-Abelian signature of the underlying gauge group.

</details>


### [259] [Detecting Multiple Phase Transitions in Lattice Systems with Intrinsic Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29090)
*Jie Mei,Tetsuo Hatsuda,Mei Huang,Lingxiao Wang*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本论文提出使用蒙特卡洛构型集合的本征维度（通过双最近邻方法估计）作为晶格系统中多重相变的几何诊断工具，可有效分辨不同转变尺度并识别各转变相关的主要自由度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决具有多个相近相变的晶格系统中难以分辨不同转变尺度以及对应自由度的问题。

Method: 采用双最近邻方法估计蒙特卡洛构型集合的本征维度，并引入通道分解的本征维度分析，基于规范不变的plaquette和Higgs变量分别探测与各自由度相关的相变；同时检验规范变量场上的本征维度受规范轨道主导的现象。

Result: 在二维q态时钟模型中，本征维度能清晰区分有序、准临界和无序区域，即使在能量和磁化强度无法分辨两个相变的q=5情况下，中间相仍表现为低维宽谷；在四维U(1) Higgs模型中，各通道的本征维度分别追踪对应自由度的相变，而组合通道保留两者特征，且发现规范变量场的本征维度受规范轨道方向主导。

Conclusion: 通道分解的本征维度是一种有效的几何探针，可用于研究具有多重相变的晶格系统，并为在完整QCD中分离禁闭与手性交叉尺度提供了新思路。

Abstract: Lattice systems with multiple nearby transitions pose two related challenges: resolving distinct transition scales and identifying the degrees of freedom primarily associated with each transition. We show that the intrinsic dimension of Monte Carlo configuration ensembles, estimated by the two-nearest-neighbors method, provides a geometric diagnostic for both problems. In the two-dimensional $q$-state clock model, the intrinsic dimension distinguishes the ordered, quasi-critical, and disordered regimes for both well-separated ($q=9$) and closely spaced ($q=5$) Berezinskii--Kosterlitz--Thouless transitions. In the $q=5$ case, the intermediate phase appears as a broad low-dimensional valley even when energy and magnetization do not separately resolve the two transitions. In the four-dimensional $U(1)$ Higgs model, we introduce channel-decomposed intrinsic dimensions based on gauge-invariant plaquette and Higgs variables. The dominant response of each channel tracks transitions associated with the corresponding degrees of freedom, while the combined channel retains features of both. We further show that intrinsic dimensions evaluated directly on gauge-variant fields are dominated by gauge-orbit directions, demonstrating the importance of removing gauge redundancy before interpreting configuration-space geometry. These results establish channel-decomposed intrinsic dimension as a geometric probe of lattice systems with multiple transitions and motivate its application to disentangling deconfinement and chiral crossover scales in full QCD.

</details>


### [260] [Symmetric Mass Generation for Domain-Wall Fermions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29963)
*Sho Araki,Hidenori Fukaya,Tetsuya Onogi,Satoshi Yamaguchi*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 首次数值研究对称质量生成（SMG）在域壁费米子中的应用，通过二维欧几里得晶格模拟Fidkowski-Kitaev马约拉纳链，揭示了局域四费米子相互作用选择性地使镜像壁边缘费米子能隙化，而保持物理壁完整。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索对称质量生成机制在域壁费米子中的实现，解决镜像费米子解耦问题。

Method: 在二维欧几里得晶格上模拟Fidkowski-Kitaev马约拉纳链，计算路径积分，并分析晶格狄拉克算子本征值谱。

Result: 发现四费米子相互作用选择性地使镜像壁的边缘费米子产生能隙，且狄拉克算子的能隙沿复平面虚轴打开，不同于传统的实轴质量偏移；同时，复Pfaffian相位的重加权使得传统混合蒙特卡洛算法依然实用。

Conclusion: 为相互作用诱导的镜像费米子解耦提供了实用的非微扰框架，验证了对称质量生成在域壁费米子中的可行性。

Abstract: We report the first numerical examination of symmetric mass generation (SMG) for domain-wall fermions. Our simulation on a two-dimensional Euclidean lattice computes the path-integral of the Fidkowski-Kitaev Majorana chain with two boundaries, corresponding to the two (physical and mirror) walls. We demonstrate by evaluating the lattice Dirac operator eigenvalue spectrum that the local four-Fermi interaction selectively gaps the edge fermions on the mirror wall while leaving the physical wall intact. We further identify a characteristic signature of SMG in the path-integral: the gap of the Dirac operator opens along the imaginary axis of the complex spectrum, in contrast to the standard mass shift in the real direction. The conventional hybrid Monte Carlo algorithm remains practical with reweighting the complex phase of the Pfaffian, which is found to be remarkably small throughout the simulation. These results provide a practical nonperturbative framework for interaction-induced mirror-fermion decoupling of lattice domain-wall fermions.

</details>


### [261] [Bias-Corrected Machine-Learning Estimation of Chiral Condensate Cumulants: A Retrospective Lattice QCD Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30416)
*Benjamin J. Choi,Hiroshi Ohno,Akio Tomiya*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本研究通过固定格点QCD数据集，评估了基于偏差校正的机器学习方法在估计狄拉克算符逆的迹及其衍生应用中的性能，结果显示偏差校正显著提升精度，且使用Tr M^{-1}作为输入特征可大幅降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了提高格点QCD中迹估计的效率和准确性，探索机器学习方法在减少狄拉克反演计算成本方面的潜力。

Method: 采用两种监督学习方法：一种以Tr M^{-1}为输入特征，另一种使用规范观测量（如plaquette和rectangle）；并对估计结果进行偏差校正，评估其在单体系和多体系重加权下的累积量计算中的表现。

Result: 偏差校正后的估计与全数据基准高度一致，未校正的估计在非线性步骤后可能出现较大偏差；使用Tr M^{-1}作为输入时，狄拉克反演成本可降至传统方法的约25.75%。

Conclusion: 偏差校正对保持机器学习估计在非线性变换和重加权过程中的准确性至关重要，且基于Tr M^{-1}的模型具有显著的计算效率优势。

Abstract: We present a retrospective case study of bias-corrected machine learning (ML) estimates of traces of the inverse Dirac operator, $\text{Tr}\,M^{-n}$ ($n=1,2,3,4$), using a fixed lattice QCD dataset and examining how the results depend on the relative proportions of the labeled and training sets. Two supervised learning approaches are examined: one using $\text{Tr}\,M^{-1}$ as the input feature, and the other employing gauge observables such as the plaquette and rectangle. Beyond the direct estimation of $\text{Tr}\,M^{-n}$, we further investigate two derived applications of the ML estimations: the evaluation of the cumulants of the chiral condensate within a single ensemble and that obtained through multi-ensemble reweighting across ensembles with different quark masses. Within this fixed dataset, the bias-corrected estimates show close agreement with the full-data reference under the adopted evaluation criteria, while the uncorrected estimates can exhibit amplified deviations after the nonlinear cumulant and reweighting steps. For the approach using $\text{Tr}\,M^{-1}$ as the input feature, nominal solve-count accounting suggests that the Dirac-inversion cost could be reduced to approximately $25.75\%$ of that of the conventional calculation in the present setup. This value is a cost projection rather than an end-to-end benchmark: it assumes comparable costs for successive inversions and excludes model-training and analysis overhead.

</details>


### [262] [$D \to (K π)_{\mathbf{27}}$ at the SU(3)-flavour-symmetric point I: Methodology and strong phase determination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30737)
*Matthew Black,Felix Erben,Maxwell T. Hansen,Fabian Joswig,Nelson Pitanga Lachini,Rajnandini Mukherjee,Srijit Paul,Antonin Portelli*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文是关于SU(3)味对称性下格点QCD计算D介子衰变到Kπ终态振幅的第一部分工作，利用OpenLat合作组生成的Wilson-Clover规范系综，在多个格点间距下提取了有限体积能谱，并通过两种连续极限外推策略得到散射相移，最终给出了D介子衰变的强相位值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在通过格点QCD方法研究D介子在SU(3)味对称条件下的非轻衰变过程，特别是(Kπ)₂₇表示中的散射相移和强相位，为理解弱衰变振幅提供关键输入。

Method: 采用Wilson-Clover规范系综（Mπ = MK ≈ 410 MeV），结合蒸馏框架构建单强子算符的欧几里得关联函数矩阵，求解广义本征值问题获取有限体积能谱；利用Lüscher方法由能谱确定散射相移，并在三个格点间距下进行连续极限外推，分别在散射参数和有限体积能量层面实施外推。

Result: 得到了从阈值到约1640 MeV范围内的(Kπ)₂₇散射相移δ₂₇(E_cm)，两种外推方法结果一致；采用仅散射长度参数化，推断出D介子质量处的强相位为δ₂₇(M_D^SU(3)) = -38.4(2.4)°；同时介绍了后续用于提取⟨(Kπ)₂₇|H_W|D⟩三点半衰变矩阵元的方法框架。

Conclusion: 该研究成功实现了SU(3)对称条件下D→Kπ衰变强相互作用相移的格点QCD计算，提供了可靠的强相位结果，验证了不同连续极限外推路径的一致性，为完整衰变振幅的后续计算奠定了基础。

Abstract: We present part one of an SU(3)-flavour-symmetric lattice QCD calculation of the amplitude for a $D$-meson decaying to a $Kπ$ final state in the 27-dimensional irreducible representation of the flavour symmetry group, denoted $(Kπ)_{\mathbf{27}}$. The Wilson--clover gauge ensembles used in this work, generated by the OpenLat collaboration, are tuned such that $M_π= M_K \approx 410\,\mathrm{MeV}$. Using the distillation framework, we construct a matrix of Euclidean correlation functions from pairs of single-hadron operators projected to definite spatial momentum. Solving a generalised eigenvalue problem yields the finite-volume energy spectrum that is used to determine the scattering phase shift from threshold up to $4 M_π\approx 1640 \,\mathrm{MeV}$, which sits below but plausibly within reach of $M_D^{\rm SU(3)} \simeq 1900\,\mathrm{MeV}$. The calculation is performed across three lattice spacings, and we apply two strategies in which the continuum limit is taken at different stages of the computation: (i) on the extracted scattering parameters and (ii) on the finite-volume energies at fixed physical volume before extracting the scattering parameters. We find consistent results across these methods for the scattering phase shift as a function of the centre-of-mass energy, $δ_{\mathbf{27}}(E_{\sf cm})$. Taking a scattering-length-only parametrisation, we infer a value for the strong phase of the weak decay, $δ_{\mathbf{27}}(M_D^{\rm SU(3)})=-38.4(2.4)^\circ$. We further describe the methodology for using the same operator basis to compute three-point correlation functions to extract $\langle (Kπ)_{\mathbf{27}}| H_W| D\rangle$, for the tree-level effective weak Hamiltonian $H_W$, and for relating such finite-volume matrix elements to the full decay amplitude. The complete analysis leading to the latter will be presented in a forthcoming manuscript.

</details>


### [263] [First-principles determination of anomaly-induced pion decay beyond the chiral limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31024)
*Tian Lin,Xu Feng,Lu-Chang Jin,Chuan Liu,Qi-Yuan Luo*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文通过格点QCD计算中性π介子双光子衰变的微小质量修正，利用反常PCAC关系避免了传统方法中的问题，实现了约1%的统计精度，并首次从第一性原理确认了π⁰-η-η'混合增强效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确测试超出手征极限的QCD需要确定非零夸克质量引起的几百分比修正，而传统方法受限于四点函数和手征对数抵消等问题。

Method: 使用反常PCAC关系，在q²=0处计算偏离精确反常条件的修正，采用接近物理参数的域壁费米子系综进行格点QCD模拟。

Result: 在两个接近物理质量的域壁费米子系综上，获得中性π介子双光子衰变宽度为Γ(π⁰→γγ)=8.09(22) eV，质量修正为2.3(1.4)%，具有正号且以同位旋破缺为主导。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了首个从第一性原理出发对π⁰-η-η'混合增强效应的确认，展示了格点QCD在精确计算反常过程中的能力。

Abstract: The two-photon decay of the neutral pion is fixed in the chiral limit by the Adler-Bell-Jackiw anomaly, while nonzero quark masses induce few-percent corrections that must be determined for precision tests of QCD beyond the chiral limit. Using the anomalous PCAC relation, we compute them in lattice QCD as deviations from the exact anomaly condition at $q^2=0$, thereby avoiding both four-point functions and the cancellation of chiral logarithms that limits conventional approaches. Because the correction is proportional to the light-quark masses, both statistical and systematic uncertainties are correspondingly suppressed, making a precision calculation feasible. On two nearly-physical domain-wall ensembles we achieve $\sim1\%$ statistical precision each for the decay width, obtaining $Γ(π^0\toγγ)=8.09(22) eV$ after continuum extrapolation. The $2.3(1.4)\%$ mass correction to the decay amplitude, positive and isospin-breaking dominated, provides the first ab initio confirmation of the $π^0$-$η$-$η'$ mixing enhancement.

</details>


<div id='physics.ao-ph'></div>

# physics.ao-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [264] [Is the Atmosphere Really Such a Good Blanket?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29471)
*Slavoljub Mijovic*

Main category: physics.ao-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的地球表面温度处理和平均方法，纠正了传统方法的偏差，发现大气对地表变暖的影响可能被高估了约10摄氏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统温度分析方法存在物理基础不足的问题，导致气候变化研究中的误解，本文旨在提供一个更准确的物理框架。

Method: 基于ECMWF的ERA5再分析数据集，使用1950-2025年每小时的地表皮肤温度数据，在约104万个网格单元上进行全球分析，并采用作者先前提出的物理一致的方法进行温度平均和评估。

Result: 新方法显示大气对地表温度的影响比传统估计低约10°C，同时确认了长期变暖趋势的存在，并实现了对全球变暖的绝对评估。

Conclusion: 当前气候科学中对大气增温贡献的估计可能存在显著偏差，采用更物理一致的方法有助于提高全球变暖评估的准确性。

Abstract: The incorrect application of the current methodology for treating and averaging Earth's surface temperatures has contributed to contradictions and misinterpretations in different scientific disciplines, particularly in climate change and global warming research. The methodology previously proposed by the author addresses these shortcomings and provides a physically based framework for further analysis.
  This paper demonstrates that the contribution attributed to the atmosphere in determining the warming of the Earth's surface may be substantially overestimated, with the estimated effect differing by approximately 10 degrees Celsius from the conventional interpretation. The analysis is based on the ERA5 reanalysis dataset produced by the European Centre for Medium-Range Weather Forecasts (ECMWF), using hourly skin temperature data for 1950-2025 on a global grid of approximately 1.04 million cells representing the Earth's surface.
  The proposed method enables an absolute assessment of global warming, confirms the long-term warming trend, and provides a comparative analysis with results obtained using the conventional methodology.

</details>


### [265] [Python-Fortran Hybrid Programming to Fuse AI and Physical Models: Examples of AI-LDA in climate and weather models (Hf2pMDA_v1.0)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29532)
*Xianrui Zhu,Zikuan Lin,Shaoqing Zhang,Zebin Lu,Songhua Wu,Xiangyun Hou,Zhisheng Xiao,Zhicheng Ren,Jiangyu Li,Jing Xu,Yang Gao,Rixu Hao,Xiaolin Yu,Mingkui Li,Guangliang Liu*

Main category: physics.ao-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于F2PY协议的基础设施Hf2pMDA，实现了人工智能算法与物理模型之间的高效互调，促进了气候和天气数据同化系统的升级与高分辨率应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于传统物理模型多用Fortran编写，而AI算法通常使用Python开发，二者直接融合存在困难，因此需要一种高效集成框架以实现AI与物理建模的深度融合。

Method: 基于F2PY协议构建Hf2pMDA基础设施，通过Python主控制器（PMC）整合Fortran编写的物理模型组件与Python实现的AI算法，实现双向调用。

Result: 成功将气候耦合同化系统升级为强耦合同化系统，并在多层降尺度模型中实现了1公里高分辨率天气同化系统，验证了Hf2pMDA在多尺度、多模块耦合中的有效性。

Conclusion: Hf2pMDA为AI与物理模型的深度结合提供了通用解决方案，有望推动AI与科学建模的共同发展。

Abstract: AI provides an unprecedented opportunity for advancing physics numerical modeling including data assimilation, which is a highly efficient and critically-important tool for advancing our understanding on Earth system and its applications. At the same time, deep incorporation of AI and physical modeling can make great driving to advance AI by injecting it rich physics from long time physics-based modeling development. However, since such physics models are conventionally coded in Fortran and AI algorithms usually are conveniently designed in Python, difficulties exist to directly incorporate AI algorithms into physics models, vice versa. Here, based on the F2PY protocol, we have developed a procedure that implements an infrastructure which conveniently conducts Hf2pMDA to form a program entity so that AI algorithms and physical models can invoke mutually. As examples, within Hf2pMDA, a climate coupled data assimilation (CDA) system is naturally upgraded to a strongly CDA (SCDA) system, and a 1 km high-resolution weather DA system is conveniently implemented within a multi-layer downscaling model that has multiscale DA in different nesting layers. In the climate SCDA system, a coupled general circulation model (CGCM) and a multiscale filtering algorithm is integrated by a Python main controller (PMC) that calls Fortran CGCM components and Weakly-CDA modules as well as a data-trained SCDA algorithm by latent space autoencoder in Python. In the high-resolution weather DA system, the downscaled model consisting of traditional Fortran DA modules in all mother domains and Python AE DA algorithm in the central child domain is integrated by a PMC that organizes these components. With convenient realization of deep incorporation of any AI algorithm and physics model, the Hf2pMDA has a great potential to make progress on both AI and scientific modeling.

</details>


### [266] [Ice-thickness based scaling of wave attenuation in sea ice: Application and assessment of wave spectra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30590)
*W. Erick Rogers,Jie Yu,Jean Rabault,Ana Carrasco,Malte Müller*

Main category: physics.ao-ph

TL;DR: 本研究评估了WAVEWATCH III中两种海冰对波浪衰减模型（IC4M9和IC4M6）在北极边缘海域的性能，发现考虑冰厚依赖性的IC4M9表现更优，但受CICE海冰模型提供的冰厚偏高影响其预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了提高海冰覆盖海域波浪模拟的准确性，特别是在边缘冰区（MIZ），需要评估并改进现有波浪-海冰相互作用模型的性能。

Method: 使用美国海军区域建模系统，在包括巴伦支海、喀拉海等东部北冰洋边缘海域应用WAVEWATCH III模型，比较两种基于不同实地数据集的波浪衰减参数化方案（IC4M9和IC4M6），并通过SWIM/CFOSAT卫星观测和SvalMIZ-24浮标数据进行验证。

Result: 与卫星和浮标观测相比，IC4M9在统计性能上显著优于IC4M6，尤其是在考虑冰厚依赖性时能更好反映MIZ中的波浪衰减；然而，由于CICE提供的冰厚在MIZ区域存在高估偏差，导致IC4M9的预测出现系统性误差。

Conclusion: 引入冰厚依赖性的波浪衰减模型（如IC4M9）可提升海冰区波浪模拟精度，但其性能受限于输入冰厚场的准确性，未来需改进海冰模型以优化波浪-海冰耦合模拟。

Abstract: This study discusses recent advances in modeling waves in sea ice in the U.S. Navy's regional modeling system. It is applied in the marginal seas of the eastern Arctic Ocean, including the Barents Sea, Kara Sea, parts of the Greenland Sea, Norwegian Sea, and waters north of Svalbard. The focus is to assess the skills of two formulations of wave attenuation by sea ice used operationally in WAVEWATCH III. Both are derived from large field datasets, one from the Arctic and the other from the Antarctic. The new model (IC4M9) describes wave attenuation depending on the ice thickness in association with the dependence on wave frequency, while the earlier default scheme (IC4M6) omits the dependence on ice thickness. The modeling results are evaluated against the satellite wave observations from SWIM/CFOSAT and the buoy measurements from the Svalbard Marginal Ice Zone 2024 Campaign (SvalMIZ-24). The comparisons with SWIM data validate the wave model skill in regions of open water or with light ice coverage. When evaluated against the SvalMIZ-24 data, the statistical performance of IC4M9 is substantially better than that of IC4M6, showing the influence of ice thickness on waves in the MIZ. Moreover, diagnosing systematic errors in the predictions by IC4M9, we find that the ice thickness field provided by the sea ice model CICE to the wave model is biased high in the MIZ, thus penalizing the performance of IC4M9 while not affecting the model IC4M6, which depends on frequency only.

</details>


### [267] [Universal ordinary differential equations and the parameterization of warm-rain processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30610)
*Axel Seifert*

Main category: physics.ao-ph

TL;DR: 本研究利用通用常微分方程（UODE）框架结合机器学习与微分方程，从时间序列数据中直接学习暖雨形成过程的动力算子，无需依赖传统的自动转化和聚并速率作为训练目标。模型在训练域内准确再现了核集合方程（KCE）的轨迹，并揭示了Seifert和Beheng（2001）参数化方案中聚并抑制项主要补偿小雨滴分数下自动转化的高估问题。基于此发现，提出了一种改进的解析公式，在保持简洁性的同时更好地匹配KCE结果，表明UODE不仅是高效的代理模型，还可用于理解和优化云微物理参数化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暖雨形成过程的参数化长期受限于核集合方程（KCE）无法唯一确定宏观云变量演化的问题，传统参数化方案依赖经验假设，缺乏对动力过程的准确描述，亟需一种既能精确拟合数据又能提供物理解释的新方法。

Method: 采用通用常微分方程（UODE）框架，结合神经网络与常微分方程，直接从超粒子模型生成的时间序列轨迹中学习暖雨过程中云水与雨水的演化算子；仅使用预报状态变量进行训练，不依赖人工标注的自动转化或聚并速率；通过分析学习到的算子结构，解析现有参数化方案的缺陷，并据此提出改进的解析表达式。

Result: 所学闭合模型在训练范围内精确复现KCE轨迹，且隐式输出的自动转化与聚并速率与KCE诊断结果高度一致；分析发现Seifert-Beheng方案中的聚并抑制项主要用于补偿小雨滴时自动转化的高估；基于该机制提出的新参数化公式显著提升与KCE的吻合度，同时保持原有简洁形式。

Conclusion: UODE不仅能构建高精度的暖雨形成代理模型，还可作为揭示和改进传统经验参数化的有力工具，为云微物理过程的建模提供了可解释、可改进的科学机器学习路径。

Abstract: The parameterization of warm-rain formation is a long-standing
  problem in cloud microphysics because the evolution of bulk cloud
  variables cannot be derived uniquely from the kinetic collection
  equation (KCE). Universal ordinary differential equations (UODEs)
  provide a scientific machine learning framework that combines neural
  networks with ordinary differential equations and can learn
  dynamical operators directly from time series. Here, the UODE
  framework is applied to warm-rain formation by training on
  trajectories generated with a super-droplet model that approximates the
  KCE. In contrast to most previous machine-learning approaches, only the
  prognostic state variables are used for training, without requiring
  autoconversion or accretion rates as targets. The learned closure
  accurately reproduces the KCE trajectories within the training
  domain and, although not explicitly constrained to do so, yields
  autoconversion and accretion rates that closely resemble those
  diagnosed from the KCE. Analyzing the learned operator provides
  new insight into the structure of the widely used Seifert and Beheng
  (2001) warm-rain parameterization. In particular, it suggests that
  the empirical suppression of accretion primarily compensates for an
  overestimation of autoconversion at small rain fractions. Motivated
  by these findings, a refined analytical formulation is proposed that
  improves agreement with the KCE while retaining the simplicity of
  the original parameterization. The results demonstrate that UODEs
  can serve not only as accurate surrogate models but also as a tool
  for understanding and improving analytical parameterizations of
  cloud microphysical processes.

</details>


### [268] [Uncertainty-Aware End-to-End AI Weather Forecasting: Disentangling Observation and Model Contributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30795)
*Rodrigo Almeida,Noelia Otero,Jost Arndt,Simon Baur,Wojciech Samek,Jackie Ma*

Main category: physics.ao-ph

TL;DR: 通过在Aardvark天气模型中引入组件级随机机制，实现了概率化预报，提升了预报准确性与不确定性量化能力，使端到端气象预报更具透明性和实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的端到端气象预报系统为确定性模型，缺乏对预报不确定性的刻画，限制了其在实际中的应用。本文旨在通过引入可解释的概率机制，提升模型的可靠性与透明度。

Method: 在Aardvark模型的观测编码器中引入学习型输入依赖噪声以捕捉数据本身的随机性（aleatoric uncertainty），并在处理器中采用蒙特卡洛dropout来建模动力学学习中的认知不确定性（epistemic uncertainty）；通过总方差分解和观测流屏蔽实验对不确定性来源进行归因分析，并进行概率化微调。

Result: 概率化模型平均提升了4.2%的均值预报性能；在中程预报中校准后具有良好的离散-技巧比（0.98），站点RMSE仅比确定性模型高2.4%，但在CRPS上在所有预见期内均优于后者，且整体性能接近ECMWF业务集合预报系统。编码器分支表现出明显的观测驱动不确定性特征。

Conclusion: 组件级的概率扩展不仅提升了预报精度，还实现了对不确定性来源的有效归因，增强了端到端深度学习气象模型的可解释性与实用性，是迈向观测驱动的大气数字孪生的重要一步。

Abstract: End-to-end weather forecasting systems produce skillful global gridded and station forecasts directly from raw Earth observations, replacing the numerical weather prediction pipeline, including data assimilation, at a fraction of its cost. These systems are deterministic and issue no uncertainty. Here we render the Aardvark Weather model probabilistic by attaching one stochastic mechanism to each component: learned, input-dependent noise at the observation encoder, capturing aleatoric uncertainty inherited from the observing system, and Monte Carlo dropout in the processor, capturing epistemic uncertainty in the learned dynamics. The resulting nested ensemble attributes forecast spread to the two sources through a law-of-total-variance decomposition, cross-checked by withholding observation streams. Probabilistic finetuning significantly improves the mean forecast, by 4.2% on average across variables and lead times. The ensemble is calibrated against ERA5 through the medium range (spread-skill ratio 0.98), keeps station RMSE within 2.4% of the deterministic model while beating it in CRPS at every lead time, and trails the operational ECMWF ensemble. The encoder branch behaves as observation-driven uncertainty. Component-attributed uncertainty makes end-to-end forecasts more transparent, a step toward observation-driven digital twins of the atmosphere.

</details>

### [269] [Deep Hedging Under Realistic Market Frictions: A Regime-Conditional Empirical Study of Dynamic Option Hedging on Bitcoin Options](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29025)
*Sheryan Kumar*

Main category: q-fin.ST

TL;DR: 本文通过真实BTC期权数据（2020-2024年）评估深度对冲方法相对于经典对冲策略的实际表现，发现Whalley-Wilmott模型在降低交易成本方面显著优于传统Black-Scholes Delta，而三种深度对冲模型均未在任何指标上超越经典方法，且交易频率几乎不受惩罚项影响。结果表明，在真实市场中当前深度对冲模型并无明显优势，可能受限于训练数据规模和模型缺乏“静止”机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验深度对冲方法在真实市场中的有效性，而非仅依赖模拟数据与无摩擦基准比较，以判断其优势是否真实存在。

Method: 使用Deribit五年的实际BTC期权数据，比较Black-Scholes delta、Leland调整对冲、Whalley-Wilmott无交易区间三种经典方法与两种神经网络结构（LSTM和前馈网络）的深度对冲模型（采用CVaR损失及交易频率惩罚），所有策略统一设置5个基点的交易成本。

Result: 在2023年9月至2024年12月的11,546个测试片段中，Whalley-Wilmott相比每小时再平衡显著降低交易成本（每段节省$1.79，p < 0.0001），交易频率降低约八倍；其P&L和尾部风险略优但不显著。所有深度对冲模型未能超越任一经典方法，且交易频率接近每小时一次，惩罚项影响微弱。在较平稳的验证期，Whalley-Wilmott的P&L优势减弱或消失，但成本优势仍存。

Conclusion: 当前深度对冲模型在真实市场数据下并未表现出超越经典方法的优势，可能由于训练数据有限及模型缺乏内在的‘不交易’机制。研究强调了基于真实数据验证的重要性，并指出此前基于模拟的研究结论需谨慎对待。

Abstract: Classical option-hedging methods like Black-Scholes delta assume constant, free rebalancing, which real markets don't allow. Deep hedging trains a neural network to handle these frictions directly, and prior work reports strong results. But those comparisons usually pit deep hedging against a frictionless classical baseline on simulated price data. That's not a fair fight, and it leaves open whether the advantage is real.
  We test this using five years of actual BTC options data from Deribit (2020-2024), comparing Black-Scholes delta, Leland's cost-adjusted hedge, and the Whalley-Wilmott no-trade band against three deep hedging setups: an LSTM and a feedforward network, each trained with a CVaR loss and, in some runs, a penalty for trading too often. All six strategies face the same 5 basis point transaction cost.
  On 11,546 test episodes from September 2023 to December 2024, Whalley-Wilmott cuts transaction costs significantly versus hourly rebalancing, saving $1.79 per episode against plain BS delta (95% CI [-2.21, -1.39], p < 0.0001) by trading about eight times less often. Its P&L and tail-risk numbers are better too, though not quite significant at this sample size. None of the three deep hedging models beat any classical benchmark on any metric, and all kept trading almost every hour regardless of penalty weight, a twenty-fold range barely moved the needle. A calmer validation period shows Whalley-Wilmott's P&L edge shrinks or disappears there while its cost edge holds, so the result depends on market regime. We think the likely causes are a small training set and the lack of any built-in mechanism for sitting still in the architectures tested. Not a flashy result, but an honest one: a real-data check on a claim mostly supported by simulations so far.

</details>


### [270] [Wasserstein-Barycentric Interaction Fields for Spatial Factor Models: Evidence from Language-Model Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29669)
*Marcus Gawronsky,Chun-Sung Huang*

Main category: q-fin.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于语言模型文章嵌入分布的带宽无关场构建方法，通过目标锚定的Wasserstein重心重建来量化企业间的反馈，并引入二次暴露调整问题将反馈映射为同行错配惩罚比率。实证结果显示该方法在预测能力上优于传统加权方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空间回报模型将交互矩阵视为给定，未能解释反馈机制。本文旨在通过文本数据构建更合理的动态交互场，以捕捉企业间隐含的结构性影响。

Method: 利用企业新闻文本的语言模型嵌入分布，采用目标锚定的Wasserstein重心重建方法构建无带宽参数的场；通过求解二次暴露调整问题，将反馈转化为同行错配惩罚比率，并与RBF加权和等权基准进行比较。

Result: 对52家企业的研究表明，基于2018-2022年新闻数据构建的场在2023-2026年期间产生3.46（95%区间[2.89, 4.17]）的惩罚比率，条件拟然更高；与共提及字段联合检验时，联合惩罚比率为2.33和0.86，边界校准检验拒绝两者被排除。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于文本嵌入和最优传输理论的方法能更有效地捕捉企业间的隐性互动结构，提升空间回报模型的解释力和预测性能。

Abstract: Spatial return models take the interaction matrix as given and leave feedback uninterpreted. We construct a bandwidth-free field from firms' language-model article embedding distributions using target-anchored Wasserstein barycentric reconstruction. A quadratic exposure-adjustment problem maps feedback into a peer-misalignment penalty ratio. For 52 firms, the field, frozen from 2018-2022 news, yields a 2023-2026 penalty ratio of 3.46 (95% interval [2.89, 4.17]) and higher conditional quasi-likelihood than equal-weighted peer support or RBF weighting of the same distances. Joint penalty ratios for the barycentric and news co-mention fields are 2.33 and 0.86 with boundary calibrated tests which reject both exclusions.

</details>


### [271] [Portfolio Risk Bounds without Cross-Asset Return Covariances: Distributional Fields from Language-Model Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29692)
*Marcus Gawronsky,Chun-Sung Huang*

Main category: q-fin.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于公司层面分布值特征的组合风险评估新方法，利用Wasserstein-2散度为系统性投资组合方差提供上界，无需依赖跨资产收益协方差估计，适用于高维短面板数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统组合风险管理依赖于跨资产收益率协方差的可靠估计，在高维短时间序列下难以实现；因此需要一种不依赖协方差矩阵的风险认证方法。

Method: 通过建立特征到系统风险暴露、再由暴露到收益的映射关系，使用多公司Wasserstein-2散度构建系统性方差的尖锐上界，并采用加权成对松弛法得到一个在可验证条件下具有凸性的目标函数。

Result: 在2018–2022年52家公司面板数据中，基于Qwen3-Embedding-8B新闻表示构建的配置方案其样本内方差排名介于四个预设受限组合群体的0.69至1.33百分位之间，显著优于等风险权重配置（21.1–28.6百分位）。

Conclusion: 该框架成功将分布值公司信息转化为可实施的投资组合配置规则和一致的风险上界，且无需估计跨资产收益协方差，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Portfolio risk assessment ordinarily relies on reliable estimates of cross-asset return covariances, which are difficult to obtain in short, high-dimensional panels. We show that firm-level distribution-valued characteristics can instead provide one-sided certificates of portfolio risk. Under maintained links from characteristics to systematic exposures and from exposures to returns, multi-firm Wasserstein-2 dispersion yields a sharp upper bound on systematic portfolio variance and a corresponding bound for standardized returns. A weighted pairwise relaxation produces an objective that is convex under a checkable condition and requires marginal volatility scales but no cross-asset return covariances. With zero firm-specific slack, the common-map scale changes the certified variance reduction but not the normalized allocation, which depends only on observed information geometry. In a 52-firm panel from 2018-2022, an allocation constructed from Qwen3-Embedding-8B news representations lies between the 0.69th and 1.33rd in-sample variance percentiles across four prespecified capped portfolio populations; equal risk weighting lies between the 21.1st and 28.6th percentiles. The lower in-sample variance ranking relative to equal risk also appears across the reported frozen language-model representations. The framework therefore distribution-valued firm information into a coherent risk bound and an implementable allocation rule constructed without cross-asset return covariances.

</details>


### [272] [Analysis of the Principal Components of Correlation Matrices of S&P 500 Financial Data from an Econophysics Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26128)
*Javier Gómez Morales*

Main category: q-fin.ST

TL;DR: 该论文从经济物理视角研究S&P 500的集体动态，利用滑动窗口构建时变相关矩阵，并通过谱分析和聚类识别市场状态，揭示危机时期的广泛参与特征及COVID-19的独特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将金融市场视为复杂系统，旨在通过谱分析与聚类方法识别可区分的市场状态，并理解不同市场环境下资产间的集体行为模式。

Method: 基于430家公司的日对数收益率，构建滑动窗口内的时变Pearson相关矩阵，分析其主特征值和特征向量；使用k-Means对最大特征值、主导特征向量的平方项及截断重构矩阵C^l进行聚类以识别市场状态，并计算稳定性、转移矩阵与逆参与比。

Result: 识别出若干稳定的市场状态，发现COVID-19时期是一个异常状态，需多个主成分才能重建；在危机期间，股票参与度更分散；2017-2018年未形成独立市场机制；C^2和C^3重构在q=40、k=5时表现最佳。

Conclusion: 该方法为市场状态提供了描述性分类框架，虽不具预测性，但为投资组合设计与交易策略提供了概念与方法工具，且可推广至其他金融市场。

Abstract: This thesis analyzes the collective dynamics of the S&P 500 from an econophysics perspective, treating the financial market as a complex system. Using daily logarithmic returns of 430 companies, time-dependent Pearson correlation matrices are constructed over sliding windows and their spectral structure is studied through the leading eigenvalues and eigenvectors. Market States (MS) are identified by applying k-Means clustering to several spectral quantities: the largest eigenvalue (interpreted as the State of the Market), the squared entries of the dominant eigenvector (read as a relative participation of each stock), and truncated matrix reconstructions C^l built from the l largest eigenpairs and normalized to retain a correlation-matrix interpretation.
  The stability of the resulting clusters is assessed, transition matrices and their stationary vectors are computed, and the inverse participation ratio is used to quantify how participation spreads across assets. The COVID-19 episode emerges as an atypical state in the full correlation matrices; reconstructing it requires more than one principal component, and the C^2 and C^3 constructions reproduce it, particularly for window length q=40 and k=5. During crisis periods the relative participation distributes over a larger number of assets, while the 2017-2018 interval contributes to the dynamics without appearing as an isolated regime. The analysis is descriptive rather than predictive, and provides conceptual and methodological tools relevant to portfolio design and trading strategies, with natural extensions to other international exchanges.

</details>
